# Siva Purana. Chapter 5

## Section 9 - Umā-Saṃhitā

## Chapter 1 - Kṛṣṇa meets Upamanyu

## \|\| 9.1.1 \|\|

*yo dhatte bhuvanāni sapta guṇavānsraṣṭā rajassaṃśrayassaṃhartā tamasānvito guṇavatīṃ māyāmatītya sthitaḥ \|*  
*satyānandamanantabodhamamalaṃ brahmādisaṃjñāspadaṃ nityaṃ sattvasamanvayādadhigataṃ pūrṇaṃ śivaṃ dhīmahi \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. We meditate upon Śiva, the perfect deity, who supports the seven worlds with the Sattva Guṇa as his attribute, who creates them resorting to Rajas and who annihilates them when endowed with Tamas; who is beyond Māyā consisting of the Guṇas and who stands firm; who is truth, bliss, and the infinite pure consciousness; who assumes the names of Brahmā etc. and who is attained always by the application of Sattva Guṇa.

## \|\| 9.1.2 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*sūtasūta mahāprājña vyāsaśiṣyana mo'stu te \|*  
*caturthī koṭirudrākhyā śrāvitā saṃhitā tvayā \|\| 2 \|\|*

The sages said:—

2\. O Sūta of great intelligence, O Sūta, disciple of Vyāsa, obeisance be to you. The fourth compendium Koṭirudra-saṃhitā has been narrated by you.

## \|\| 9.1.3 \|\|

*athomāsaṃhitāntaḥstha nānākhyānasamanvitam \|*  
*brūhi śaṃbhoścaritraṃ vai sāmbasya paramātmanaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Now please narrate the story of Śiva, the supreme soul, accompanied by Umā-Pārvatī—the story that is full of incidental anecdotes.

## \|\| 9.1.4 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*maharṣayaśśaunakādyāḥ śṛṇuta premataḥ śubham \|*  
*śāṃkaraṃ caritaṃ divyaṃ bhuktimuktipradaṃ param \|\| 4 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

4\. O Śaunaka and other sages, please listen with devotion to the divinely auspicious story of Śiva, that yields worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 9.1.5 \|\|

*itīdṛśaṃ puṇyapraśnaṃ pṛṣṭavānmunisattamaḥ \|*  
*vyāsassanatkumāraṃ vai śaivaṃ saccaritaṃ jagau \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. This same holy question was put by the excellent sage Vyāsa to Sanatkumāra who narrated the story of Siva.

## \|\| 9.1.6 \|\|

*\|\| sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*vāsudevāya yatproktamupamanyumaharṣiṇā \|*  
*taducyate mayā vyāsa caritaṃ hi maheśituḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

6\. O Vyāsa, the story of lord Śiva which was mentioned to Kṛṣṇa by the sage Upamanyu, I narrate to you.

## \|\| 9.1.7 \|\|

*purā putrārthamagamatkailāsaṃ śaṃkarālayam \|*  
*vasudevasutaḥ kṛṣṇastapastaptuṃ śivasya hi \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Formerly Kṛṣṇa, son of Vasudeva went to Kailāsa the abode of Śiva in order to perform the penance to propitiate Śiva for obtaining a son.

## \|\| 9.1.8 \|\|

*atropamanyuṃ saṃdṛṣṭvā tapaṃtaṃ śṛṃga uttame \|*  
*praṇamya bhaktyā sa muniṃ paryapṛcchatkṛtāñjaliḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. On seeing the sage Upamanyu performing the penance on the excellent summit he bowed to him with devotion and asked him with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 9.1.9 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*upamanyo mahāprājña śaivapravara sanmate \|*  
*putrārthamagamaṃ taptuṃ tapo'tra giriśasya hi \|\| 9 \|\|*

Lord Kṛṣṇa said:—

9\. O Upamanyu of great intellect, O foremost of the devotees of Śiva with good intellect. I have come here to perform the panance of Śiva for obtaining a son.

## \|\| 9.1.10 \|\|

*brūhi śaṃkaramāhātmyaṃ sadānandakaraṃ mune \|*  
*yacchrutvā bhaktitaḥ kuryyāṃ tapa aiśvaramuttamam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O sage, please narrate the greatness of Śiva, delightful to the good, on hearing which I shall perform the penance of Śiva with devotion.

## \|\| 9.1.11 \|\|

*\|\| sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasya vāsudevasya dhīmataḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca prasannātmā hyupamanyussmarañchivam \|\| 11 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

11\. On hearing these words of the intelligent Kṛṣṇa, Upamanyu was delighted in his mind. Remembering Śiva, he replied.

## \|\| 9.1.12 \|\|

*\|\| upamanyuruvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu kṛṣṇa mahāśaiva mahimānaṃ maheśituḥ \|*  
*yamadrākṣamahaṃ śaṃbhorbhaktivarddhanamuttamam \|\| 12 \|\|*

Upamanyu said:—

12\. O Kṛṣṇa, great devotee of Śiva, listen to the glory of Śiva which I have witnessed myself. It is excellent story that enhances devotion to Śiva.

## \|\| 9.1.13 \|\|

*tapaḥstho'haṃ samadrākṣaṃ śaṃkaraṃ ca tadāyudhān \|*  
*parivāraṃ samastaṃ ca viṣṇvādīnamarādikān \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. While performing penance, I saw Śiva, his weapons, his followers and Viṣṇu and other gods.

## \|\| 9.1.14 \|\|

*tribhiraṃśaiśśobhamānamajasrasukhamavyayam \|*  
*ekapādaṃ mahādaṃṣṭraṃ sajvālakavalairmukhaiḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. He was shining with his three parts. He was of permanent happiness. He was imperishable. He had one foot and huge teeth with feces having mouthfuls of blazing flame.

## \|\| 9.1.15 \|\|

*dvisahasramayūkhānāṃ jyotiṣā'tivirājitam \|*  
*sarvāstrapravarābādhamanekākṣaṃ sahasrapāt \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. He was shining with the brilliance of two thousand rays. He was whirling excellent missiles. He had many eyes and a thousand legs.

## \|\| 9.1.16-17 \|\|

*yaśca kalpāntasamaye viśvaṃ saṃharati dhruvam \|*  
*nāvadhyo yasya ca bhavettrailaukye sacarācare \|\| 16 \|\|*

*maheśvarabhujotsṛṣṭaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram \|*  
*nirdadāha drutaṃ kṛtsnaṃ nimeṣārddhānna saṃśayaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. He who decisively annihilates the universe at the end of a kalpa and for whom there was none to be spared from death in the three worlds consisting of the mobile and immobile beings which being cast away from his hands burnt instantaneously and entirely, certainly within half a moment.

## \|\| 9.1.18 \|\|

*tapaḥstho rudrapārśvasthaṃ dṛṣṭavānahamavyam \|*  
*guhyamastraṃ paraṃ cāsya na tulyamadhikaṃ kvacit \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. While performing penance, I saw at the side of Rudra the imperishable highly secret weapon which had no other missile superior or equal to it.

## \|\| 9.1.19 \|\|

*yattacchūlamiti khyātaṃ sarvalokeṣu śūlinaḥ \|*  
*vijayābhidhamatyugraṃ sarvaśastrāstranāśakam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. It was the weapon Vijaya, the trident of the fierce weapon that destroyed all other weapons and missiles.

## \|\| 9.1.20 \|\|

*dārayedyanmahīṃ kṛtsnāṃ śoṣayedyanmahodadhim \|*  
*pātayedakhilaṃ jyotiścakraṃ yannātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. There is no doubt that it could pierce through the entire earth, dry up the big ocean and cause all the luminary bodies to fall.

## \|\| 9.1.21 \|\|

*yauvanāśvo hato yena māṃdhātā sabalaḥ purā \|*  
*cakravartī mahātejāstrailokyavijayo nṛpaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. It was the weapon with which Yauvanāśva the emperor of great refulgence and Māndhātṛ the powerful king who conquered the three worlds were killed formerly.

## \|\| 9.1.22 \|\|

*darpāviṣṭo haihayaśca niḥ kṣipto lavaṇāsuraḥ \|*  
*śatrughnaṃ nṛpatiṃ yuddhe samāhūya samaṃtataḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The arrogant Haihaya\[7\] king was killed with it. When the demon Lavaṇa challenged king Śatrughna this missile was hurled at him.

## \|\| 9.1.23 \|\|

*tasmindaitye vinaṣṭe tu rudrahaste gataṃ tu yat \|*  
*tacchūlamiti tīkṣṇāgraṃ saṃtrāsajananaṃ mahat \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. When that demon was killed this trident had returned to Rudra. It had a sharp point and was highly terrifying.

## \|\| 9.1.24 \|\|

*triśikhāṃ bhṛkuṭīṃ kṛtvā tarjayaṃtamiva sthitam \|*  
*vidhūmrānalasaṃkāśaṃ bālasūryamivoditam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. It stood as if threatening all with the three spikes for its knit eyebrows. It was like a blazing smokeless fire and the rising sun.

## \|\| 9.1.25-26 \|\|

*sūryya hastamanirddeśyaṃ pāśahastamivāṃtakam \|*  
*paraśuṃ tīkṣṇadhāraṃ ca sarpādyaiśca vibhūṣitam \|\| 25 \|\|*

*kalpāntadahanākāraṃ tathā puruṣavigraham \|*  
*yattadbhārgavarāmasya kṣatriyāntakaraṃ raṇe \|\| 26 \|\|*

25-26. I saw his sharp-edged axe decorated with serpents etc. It was as terrible as the god of death holdingthe noose in his hand. It was like an indescribable ray of the sun and the features of the fire at the end of Kalpas. It had the size of a full-grown man. Bhārgava Rāma had used it in the battle for the extermination of Kṣatriyas.

## \|\| 9.1.27 \|\|

*rāmo yadbalamāśritya śivadattaśca vai purā \|*  
*triḥsaptakṛtvo nakṣatraṃ dadāha hṛṣito muniḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. It was given to Rāma by Śiva formerly.\[9\] Strengthened by it the delighted sage burnt the Kṣatriyas twenty-one times.

## \|\| 9.1.28 \|\|

*sudarśanaṃ tathā cakraṃ sahasravadanaṃ vibhum \|*  
*dvisahasrabhujaṃ devamadrākṣaṃ puruṣākṛtim \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. I saw the discus Sudarśana in the shape of a human being with a thousand faces and two thousand hands. It was divine and lordly.

## \|\| 9.1.29 \|\|

*dvisahasrekṣaṇaṃ dīptaṃ sahasracaraṇākulam \|*  
*koṭisūryapratīkāśaṃ trailokyadahanakṣamam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. It had two thousand eyes of fiery brilliance, and one thousand legs. It shone like a crore suns. It was capable of burning the three worlds.

## \|\| 9.1.30 \|\|

*vajraṃ mahojjvalaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ śataparvapranuttamam \|*  
*mahādhanuḥ pinākaṃ ca satūṇīraṃ mahādyutim \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. I saw the sharp and refulgent thunderbolt of hundred spikes of extreme excellence. I saw the great bow Pināka of excessive brilliance and its quiver of arrows.

## \|\| 9.1.31 \|\|

*śaktiṃ khaṅgaṃ ca pāśaṃ ca mahādīptaṃ samāṃkuśam \|*  
*gadāṃ ca mahatīṃ divyāmanyānyastrāṇi dṛṣṭavān \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. I saw the Śakti, the sword, the noose of great brilliance and a goad, the great divine iron club, and many other weapons.

## \|\| 9.1.32 \|\|

*tathā ca lokapālānāmastrāṇyetāni yāni ca \|*  
*adrākṣaṃ tāni sarvāṇi bhagavadrudrapārśvataḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. On either side of Lord Rudra, I saw the weapons of the guardians of the quarters.

## \|\| 9.1.33 \|\|

*savyadeśe tu devasya brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ \|*  
*vimānaṃ divyamāsthāya haṃsayuktaṃ manonugam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. To the right of the lord was Brahmā, the grandfather of the worlds seated in his aerial chariot fitted with a swan. It was divine and as speedy as mind.

## \|\| 9.1.34 \|\|

*vāmapārśve tu tasyaiva śaṃkhacakragadādharaḥ \|*  
*vainateyaṃ samāsthāya tathā nārāyaṇaḥ sthitaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Nārāyaṇa, holding conch, discus and mace, stood on bis left, seated on the Garuḍa.

## \|\| 9.1.35 \|\|

*svāyaṃbhuvādyā manavo bhṛgvādyā ṛṣayastathā \|*  
*śakrādyā devatāścaiva sarva eva samaṃ yayuḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Brahmā and other Manus, Bhṛgu and other sages, Indra and other gods, all were there.

## \|\| 9.1.36 \|\|

*skaṃdaśśaktiṃ samādāya mayūrasthassaghaṃṭakaḥ \|*  
*devyāssamīpe saṃtasthau dvitīya iva pāvakaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Holding his Śakti and a bell and seated on his peacock, Skanda stood near the goddess like another fìregod.

## \|\| 9.1.37 \|\|

*naṃdī śūlaṃ samādāya bhavāgre samavasthitaḥ \|*  
*sarvabhūtagaṇāścaivaṃ mātaro vividhāḥ sthitāḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Nandin stood in front of Śiva holding the trident. All the goblins, Gaṇas and the mothers were present there.

## \|\| 9.1.38 \|\|

*te'bhivādya maheśānaṃ parivāryya samaṃtataḥ \|*  
*astuvanvividhaiḥ stotrairmahādevaṃ tadā surāḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Bowing to lord Śiva and surrounding him on all sides, the gods eulogised the lord with different kinds of hymns.

## \|\| 9.1.39 \|\|

*yatkiṃcittu jagatyasmindṛśyate śrūyate'thavā \|*  
*tatsarvaṃ bhagavatpārśve nirīkṣyāhaṃ suvismitaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. I saw on cither side of the lord whatever is seen or heard in this world. I was surprised at it.

## \|\| 9.1.40-41 \|\|

*sumahaddhairyyamālaṃbya prāṃjalirvividhaiḥ stavaiḥ \|*  
*paramānandasaṃmagno'bhūvaṃ kṛṣṇāhamaddhvare \|\| 40 \|\|*

*saṃmukhe śaṃkaraṃ dṛṣṭvā bāṣpagadgadayā girā \|*  
*apūjayaṃ suvidhivadahaṃ śraddhāsamanvitaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

40-41. O Kṛṣṇa, it appeared as though some sacrifice was going on there. In that sacrifice I became immersed in great delight. I was bold to a great extent on seeing Śiva in front. With palms joined in reverence and words choked with tears of joy I worshipped him duly with various kinds of hymns.

## \|\| 9.1.42 \|\|

*bhagavānatha suprītaśśaṃkaraḥ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*vāṇyā madhurayā prītyā māmāha prahasanniva \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Then the delighted lord Śiva spoke to me laughingly in sweet words with great pleasure.

## \|\| 9.1.43 \|\|

*na vicālayituṃ śakyo mayā vipra punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*parīkṣitosi bhadraṃ te bhavānbhaktyānvito dṛḍhaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. “O brahmin, you cannot be shaken by me though I try again and again. You have been tested by me. Welfare be to you You are steadily endowed with devotion.

## \|\| 9.1.44 \|\|

*tasmātte parituṣṭo'smi varaṃ varaya suvrata \|*  
*durlabhaṃ sarvadeveṣu nādeyaṃ vidyate tava \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. O one of good rites, I am delighted. Choose the boon. There is nothing which cannot be given to you even if it happens to be something rare and inaccessible to all the gods.”

## \|\| 9.1.45 \|\|

*sa cāhaṃ tadvacaḥ śrutvā śaṃbhoḥ satpremasaṃyutam \|*  
*devaṃ taṃ prāṃjalirbhūtvā'bruvaṃ bhaktānukaṃpinam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. On hearing the loving words of Śiva I spoke to the lord sympathetic with the devotees with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 9.1.46 \|\|

*\|\| upamanyuruvāca \|*  
*bhagavanyadi tuṣṭo'si yadi bhaktiḥ sthirā mayi \|*  
*tena satyena me jñānaṃ trikālaviṣayaṃ bhavet \|\| 46 \|\|*

Upamanyu said:—

46\. “O lord, if you are satisfied and if my devotion is firm and steady, may, in virtue of that truth, my knowledge comprehend the past, present and future.

## \|\| 9.1.47 \|\|

*prayaccha bhaktiṃ vipulāṃ tvayi cāvyabhicāriṇīm \|*  
*sānvayasyāpi nityaṃ me bhūri kṣīraudanaṃ bhavet \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Grant me infinite devotion never swerving or straying from you. Let me and my family have milk pudding every day.

## \|\| 9.1.48 \|\|

*mamāstu tava sānnidhyaṃ nityaṃ caivāśrame vibho \|*  
*tava bhakteṣu sakhyaṃ syādanyonyeṣu sadā bhavet \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. O lord, may you be present in my hermitage every day. Let my mutual friendship with your other devotees flourish for ever.”

## \|\| 9.1.49 \|\|

*evamukto mayā śaṃbhurvihasya parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*kṛpādṛṣṭyā nirīkṣyāśu māṃ sa prāha yadūdvaha \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. O leading scion of the family of Yadus, thus requested by me, lord Śiva laughed and glanced at me with his merciful eyes. He said to me.

## \|\| 9.1.50 \|\|

*\|\| śrīśiva uvāca \|*  
*upamanyo mune tāta varjjitastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi \|*  
*jarāmaraṇajairdoṣaissarvakāmānvito bhava \|\| 50 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

50\. O Upamanyu, O dear, O sage, you will be free from the defects of old age and death. Achieve all desires.

## \|\| 9.1.51 \|\|

*munīnāṃ pūjanīyaśca yaśodhanasamanvitaḥ \|*  
*śīlarūpaguṇaiśvaryaṃ matprasādātpadepade \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. You will deserve the worship of the sages. You will have fame as your wealth. Thanks to my grace, step by step, you will possess good conduct, beauty, good qualities and riches.

## \|\| 9.1.52 \|\|

*kṣīrodasāgarasyaiva sānnidhyaṃ payasāṃ nidheḥ \|*  
*tatra te bhavitā nityaṃ yatrayatrecchase mune \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O sage, wherever you wish it to be, the milk ocean will flow to and be present there.

## \|\| 9.1.53 \|\|

*amṛtātmakaṃ tu tatkṣīraṃ yāvatsaṃyāmyate tataḥ \|*  
*imaṃ vaivasvataṃ kalpaṃ paśyase bandhubhissaha \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. By the time that nectarine milk is fully controlled you will see Vaivasvata Kalpa along with your kinsmen

## \|\| 9.1.54 \|\|

*tvadgotraṃ cākṣayaṃ cāstu matprasādātsadaiva hi \|*  
*sānnidhyamāśrame te'haṃ kariṣyāmi mahāmune \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. O great sage, may your family be never-ending due to my grace. I shall be always present in your hermitage.

## \|\| 9.1.55 \|\|

*madbhaktistu sthirā cāstu sadā dāsyāmi darśanam \|*  
*smṛtaśca bhavatā vatsa priyastvaṃ sarvathā mama \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Let your devotion to me be permanent. O dear one, whenever remembered by you I shall appear before you. You are by all means my beloved.

## \|\| 9.1.56 \|\|

*yathākāmasukhaṃ tiṣṭha notkaṇṭhāṃ kartumarhasi \|*  
*sarvaṃ prapūrṇatāṃ yātu ciṃtitaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Be happy realising all your desires. Do not get anxious or worried. There is no doubt that everything you think of will be fulfilled.

## \|\| 9.1.57 \|\|

*upamanyuruvāca \|*  
*evamuktvā sa bhagavānsūryyakoṭisamaprabhaḥ \|*  
*mameśāno varāndattvā tatraivāntaradhīyata \|\| 57 \|\|*

Upamanyu said:—

57\. After granting me the boons and saying this, lord Śiva of the brilliance of a crore suns vanished there itself.

## \|\| 9.1.58 \|\|

*evaṃ dṛṣṭo mayā kṛṣṇa parivārasamanvitaḥ \|*  
*śaṃkaraḥ parameśāno bhaktimuktipradāyakaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. O Kṛṣṇa, Śiva, the supreme lord the bestower of worldly pleasures and salvation, was thus seen by me accompanied by his followers.

## \|\| 9.1.59 \|\|

*śaṃbhunā parameśena yaduktaṃ tena dhīmatā \|*  
*tadavāptaṃ ca me sarvaṃ devadevasamādhinā \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Whatever was mentioned by the intelligent lord Śiva has been wholly acquired by me by meditating on the lord of the gods.

## \|\| 9.1.60 \|\|

*pratyakṣaṃ caiva tai jātāngandharvāpsarasastathā \|*  
*ṛṣīnvidyādharāṃścaiva paśya siddhānvyavasthitān \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. See for yourself the Gandharvas, Apsaras, sages, Vidyādharas and the well arranged Siddhas.

## \|\| 9.1.61 \|\|

*paśya vṛkṣānmanoramyānsnigdhapatrānsugaṃdhinaḥ \|*  
*sarvartukusumairyuktānsadāpuṣpaphalanvitān \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. See the beautiful trees endowed with glossy leaves, fragrant with seasonal flowers and bearing fruits and flowers.

## \|\| 9.1.62 \|\|

*sarvametanmahābāho śaṃkarasya mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*prasādāddevadevasya viśvaṃ bhāvasamanvitama \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. O mighty hero, all these are the results of the grace of Śiva the great soul, the lord of gods. The universe is emotionally agreeable.

## \|\| 9.1.63 \|\|

*mamāsti tvakhilaṃ jñānaṃ prasādācchūlapāṇinaḥ \|*  
*bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ ca sarvaṃ jānāmi tattvataḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. I have the perfect knowledge of everything, thanks to the grace of the Trident-bearing lord. I know factually the past, present and the future, everything.

## \|\| 9.1.64 \|\|

*tamahaṃ dṛṣṭavāndevamapi devāḥ sureśvarāḥ \|*  
*yaṃ na paśyaṃtyanārādhya ko'nyo dhanyataro mayā \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. I have seen the lord whom even the great gods do not see without propitiating. Who can be more blessed than I?

## \|\| 9.1.65 \|\|

*ṣaḍviṃśakamiti khyātaṃ paraṃ tattvaṃ sanātanam \|*  
*evaṃ dhyāyaṃti vidvāṃsau mahatparamamakṣaram \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. The eternal principles are known as twenty-six. Learned men meditate on the great and imperishable thus.

## \|\| 9.1.66 \|\|

*sarva tattvavidhānajñaḥ sarvatattvārthadarśanaḥ \|*  
*sa eva bhagavāndevaḥ pradhānapuruṣeśvaraḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. That great lord alone is the knower of well-ordained principles, the direct perceiver of all true objects and the lord of Prakṛti and Puruṣa.

## \|\| 9.1.67 \|\|

*yo nijāddakṣiṇātpārśvādbrahmāṇaṃ lokakāraṇam \|*  
*vāmādapyasṛjadviṣṇuṃ lokarakṣārthamīśvaraḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. It was he who created, for the protection of the worlds, Brahmā, the cause of the worlds, from his right hand and Viṣṇu from the left hand side.

## \|\| 9.1.68 \|\|

*kalpānte caiva saṃprāpte'sṛjadrudraṃ hṛdaḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*tatassamaharatkṛtsnaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṃgamam \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. When the Kalpa came to an end, the lord created Rudra from his heart. Then he annihilated the universe including the mobile and immobile beings.

## \|\| 9.1.69 \|\|

*yugāṃte sarvabhūtāni saṃvartaka ivānalaḥ \|*  
*kālo bhūtvā mahādevo grasamānassa tiṣṭhati \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. At the end of Yugas, lord Śiva becomes Kāla and stands devouring all the living beings, like the fire of dissolution Saṃvartaka.

## \|\| 9.1.70 \|\|

*sarvajñassarvabhūtātmā savarbhūtabhavodbhavaḥ \|*  
*āste sarvagato devo dṛśyassarvaiśca daivataiḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. The lord is omniscient, the soul and the material and effective cause of all living beings. He is all-pervasive and visible to all deities.

## \|\| 9.1.71 \|\|

*atastvaṃ putralābhāya samārādhaya śaṃkaram \|*  
*śīghraṃ prasanno bhavitā śivaste bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. Hence, propitiate him for the acquisition of a son. Śiva, favourably disposed to his devotees will become delighted with you, soon.

## Chapter 2 - Upamanyu’s instruction

## \|\| 9.2.1 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇya munervākyamupamanyormahātmanaḥ \|*  
*jātabhaktirmahādeve kṛṣṇaḥ provāca taṃ munim \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

1\. On hearing the words of sage Upamanyu the great soul, devotion to lord Śiva was generated in Kṛṣṇa and he spoke to the sage.

## \|\| 9.2.2 \|\|

*\|\| śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*upamanyo mune tāta kṛpāṃ kuru mamopari \|*  
*ye ye śivaṃ samārādhya kāmānāpuśca tānvada \|\| 2 \|\|*

Lord Kṛṣṇa said:—

2\. O dear, O Upamanyu, be merciful to me. Please mention the details of the persons who have attained their desires by propitiating Śiva.

## \|\| 9.2.3 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇyopamanyussa muniśśaivavaro mahān \|*  
*kṛṣṇavākyaṃ supraśasya pratyuvāca kṛpānidhiḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

3\. On hearing the words of Kṛṣṇa, that sage Upamanyu, the great devotee of Śiva, the storehouse of mercy praised him and said.

## \|\| 9.2.4 \|\|

*upamanyuruvāca \|*  
*yairyairbhavārādhanataḥ prāpto hṛtkāma eva hi \|*  
*tāṃstānbhaktānpravakṣyāmi śṛṇu tvaṃ vai yadūdvaha \|\| 4 \|\|*

Upamanyu said:—

4\. O leading scion of the family of Yadus, I shall enumerate the various devotees, by whom the cherished desires of their hearts have been realised through the propitiation of Śiva. Please listen.

## \|\| 9.2.5 \|\|

*śarvātsarvāmaraiśvaryyaṃ hiraṇyakaśipuḥ purā \|*  
*varṣāṇāṃ daśalakṣāṇi so'labhaccandraśekharāt \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Formerly Hiraṇyakaśipu attained the glory of the suzerainty over all the gods for a period of a million years from the moon-crested lord Śīva.

## \|\| 9.2.6 \|\|

*tasyā'tha putrapravaro nandano nāma viśrutaḥ \|*  
*sa ca śarvavarādindraṃ varṣāyutamadhonayat \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. His excellent son Nandana was very famous. Thanks to Śiva’s boon he kept Indra under him for ten thousand years.

## \|\| 9.2.7 \|\|

*viṣṇucakraṃ ca taddhoraṃ vajramākhaṇḍalasya ca \|*  
*śīrṇaṃ purā'bhavatkṛṣṇa tadaṃgeṣu mahāhave \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O Kṛṣṇa, the terrible discus of Viṣṇu and the thunderbolt of Indra became shattered against his person during a great battle.

## \|\| 9.2.8 \|\|

*na śastrāṇi vahaṃtyaṃge dharmatastasya dhīmataḥ \|*  
*grahasyātibalasyājau cakravajramukhānyapi \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The weapons, missiles, discus, thunderbolt never became effective in the body of Graha of great might during battles, thanks to the Dharma of that intelligent one.

## \|\| 9.2.9 \|\|

*ardyamānāśca vibudhā graheṇa subalīyasā \|*  
*devadattavarā jaghnurasurendrāssurānbhṛśam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The gods were harassed by Graha the mighty one. Leading Asuras to whom the lord had granted boons killed gods with impunity.

## \|\| 9.2.10 \|\|

*tuṣṭo vidyutprabhasyāpi trailokyeśvaratā madāt \|*  
*śatavarṣasahasrāṇi sarvalokeśvaro bhavaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Śiva, the lord of all worlds, when satisfied granted the lordship over the three worlds to Vidyutprabha for a period of a hundred thousand years.

## \|\| 9.2.11 \|\|

*tathā putrasahasrāṇāmayutaṃ ca dadau śivaḥ \|*  
*mama cānucaro nityaṃ bhaviṣyasyabravīditi \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Śiva blessed him with ten million sons also and said “You will become my follower for ever.”

## \|\| 9.2.12 \|\|

*kuśadvīpe śubhaṃ rājyamadadādbhagavānbhavaḥ \|*  
*sa tasmai śaṅkaraḥ prītyā vāsudeva prahṛṣṭadhīḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. O Kṛṣṇa, the delighted lord Śiva, lovingly gave him an auspicious kingdom in Kuśa Dvīpa.

## \|\| 9.2.13 \|\|

*dhātrā sṛṣṭaśśatamakho daityo varṣaśataṃ purā \|*  
*tapaḥ kṛtvā sahasraṃ tu putrāṇāmalabhadbhavāt \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The Daitya Śatamukha created by Brahmā, performed the penance of Śiva formerly for a hundred years and obtained a thousand sons.

## \|\| 9.2.14 \|\|

*yājñavalkya iti khyāto gīto vedeṣu vai muniḥ \|*  
*ārādhya sa mahādevaṃ prāptavāñjñānamuttamam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. The famous sage Yājñavalkya glorified in the Vedas, propitiated him and acquired excellent knowledge.

## \|\| 9.2.15 \|\|

*vedavyāsastu yo nāmnā prāptavānatulaṃ yaśaḥ \|*  
*so'pi śaṃkaramārādhya trikālajñānamāptavān \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The sage Vedavyāsa who had acquired unequalled glory propitiated him and attained knowledge of the three times—past, present and future.

## \|\| 9.2.16 \|\|

*indreṇa vālakhilyāste paribhūtāstu śaṅkarāt \|*  
*lebhire somahartāraṃ garuḍaṃ sarvadurjayam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The Bālakhilyas harassed by Indra secured from Śiva the invincible Garuḍa who brought them Soma juice.

## \|\| 9.2.17 \|\|

*āpaḥ pranaṣṭāḥ sarvāśca pūrvaroṣātkaparddinaḥ \|*  
*śarvaṃ samakapālena devairiṣṭvā pravartitam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. When all the waters became dried up and lost by the prior fury of Śiva they were worshipped and made to flow again by the gods through the skull.

## \|\| 9.2.18-19 \|\|

*atrerbhāryyā cānasūyā trīṇi varṣaśatāni ca \|*  
*muśaleṣu nirāhārā suptvā śarvāttatassutān \|\| 18 \|\|*

*dattātreyaṃ muniṃ lebhe candraṃ durvāsasaṃ tathā \|*  
*gaṃgāṃ pravartayāmāsa citrakūṭe pativratā \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. Anasūyā, the chaste wife of Atri observed fast for three hundred years sleeping only on the threshing rod. She secured the three sons—the sage Dattātreya, Durvāsas and the moon, thanks to Śiva’s favour. She made Gaṅgā flow in Citrakūṭa.

## \|\| 9.2.20 \|\|

*vikarṇaśca mahādevaṃ tathā bhaktasukhāvaham \|*  
*prasādya mahatīṃ siddhimāptavānmadhusūdana \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. O Madhusūdana, after pleasing Mahādeva the bestower of happiness to the devotees, Vikarṇa obtained great Siddhi.

## \|\| 9.2.21 \|\|

*citraseno nṛpaśśaṃbhuṃ prasādya dṛḍhabhaktimān \|*  
*samastanṛpabhītibhyo'bhayaṃ prāpātulaṃ ca kam \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. King Citrasena of steady devotion delighted Śiva and attained freedom from the terror of all other kings and unequalled pleasure as well.

## \|\| 9.2.22 \|\|

*śrīkaro gopikāsūnurnṛpapūjāvilokanāt \|*  
*jātabhaktirmahādeve paramāṃ siddhimāptavān \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Śrīkara, the son of a cowherdess, became his devotee on seeing the worship of Śiva performed by the king and attained great Siddhi.

## \|\| 9.2.23 \|\|

*citrāṅgado nṛpasutassīmantinyāḥ patirhare \|*  
*śivānugrahato magno yamunāyāṃ mṛto na hi \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. O Kṛṣṇa, prince Citrāṅgada, the husband of Sīmantinī, was drowned in Yamunā but was saved by Śiva’s blessings

## \|\| 9.2.24 \|\|

*sa ca takṣālayaṃ gatvā tanmaitrīṃ prāpya suvrataḥ \|*  
*āyātaḥ svagṛhaṃ prīto nānādhanasamanvitaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. He went to the abode of Takṣaka and gained his friendship. The delighted prince of good rites then returned to his house with diverse riches.

## \|\| 9.2.25 \|\|

*sīmaṃtinī priyā tasya somavrataparāyaṇā \|*  
*śivānugrahataḥ kṛṣṇa lebhe saubhāgyamuttamam \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. O Kṛṣṇa, his beloved wife Sīmantinī who regularly performed Somavrata acquired excellent blessedness and fortune, thanks to Śiva’s grace.

## \|\| 9.2.26 \|\|

*tatprabhāvādvrate tasminneko dvijasutaḥ purā \|*  
*kaścitstrītvaṃ gato lobhātkṛtadārākṛtiśchalāt \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. While she was performing this Vrata a certain brahmin boy, out of greed, had assumed the guise of a woman fraudulently. Due to her power he became a woman permanently.

## \|\| 9.2.27 \|\|

*caṃcukā puṃścalī duṣṭā gokarṇe dvijataḥ purā \|*  
*śrutvā dharmakathāṃ śaṃbhorbhaktyā prāpa parāṃ gatim \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Cañculā, a vicious harlot, heard a virtuous story from a brahmin at Gokarṇa and became a devotee of Śíva. She attained the great goal.

## \|\| 9.2.28 \|\|

*svastryanugrahataḥ pāpī biṃdugo caṃcukāpatiḥ \|*  
*śrutvā śivapurāṇaṃ sa sadgatiṃ prāpa śāṃkarīm \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Binduga the sinning husband of Cañculā heard Śivapurāṇa through the good offices of his wife and attained the good goal of Siva.

## \|\| 9.2.29 \|\|

*piṃgalā gaṇikā khyātā madarāhvo dvijādhamaḥ \|*  
*śaivamṛṣabhamabhyarcya lebhāte sadgatiṃ ca tau \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Piṅgalā a notorious prostitute and the base brahmin Mandara worshipped the bull of Śiva and attained the supreme goal.

## \|\| 9.2.30 \|\|

*mahānandābhidhā kaścidveśyā śivapadādṛtā \|*  
*dṛḍhātpaṇātsuprasādya śivaṃ lebhe ca sadgatim \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. A certain prostitute Mahānandā devoted to Śiva, dedicating herself at the feet of Śiva delighted him and attained good goal.

## \|\| 9.2.31 \|\|

*kaikeyī dvijabālāḥ ca sādarāhvā śivavratā \|*  
*paramaṃ hi sukhaṃ prāpa śiveśavratadhāraṇāt \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Kaikeyī, a brahmin girl, respectfully performing Śiva’s Vratas attained great happiness, thanks to the regular performance of Śīva’s vrata.

## \|\| 9.2.32 \|\|

*vimarṣaṇaśca nṛpatiśśivabhaktiṃ vidhāya vai \|*  
*gatiṃ lebhe parāṃ kṛṣṇa śivānugrahataḥ purā \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. O Kṛṣṇa, king Vimarṣaṇa adopting Śiva’s devotion attained great goal formerly, thanks to Śiva’s blessings.

## \|\| 9.2.33 \|\|

*durjanaśca nṛpaḥ pāpī bahustrīlaṃpaṭaḥ khalaḥ \|*  
*śivabhaktyā śivaṃ prāpa nirliptaḥ sarvakarmasu \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Durjana, a sinful knavish king running after women attained Śiva, thanks to devotion to Śiva. He was unaffected by all actions.

## \|\| 9.2.34 \|\|

*sastrīkaśśabaro nāmnā śaṃkaraśca śivavratī \|*  
*citābhasmarato bhaktyā lebhe tadgatimuttamām \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. A hunter Śaṅkara performing Śivavrata along with his wife and attached to the Bhasma from the funeral pyre attained the great goal by his devotion.

## \|\| 9.2.35 \|\|

*sauminī nāma cāṇḍālī saṃpūjyājñānato hi sā \|*  
*lebhe śaivīṃ gatiṃ kṛṣṇa śaṃkarānugrahātparāt \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. O Kṛṣṇa, a cāṇḍāla woman Sauminī performed the worship by ignorance and attained the goal of Śiva by the blessings of the lord.

## \|\| 9.2.36 \|\|

*mahākālābhidho vyādho kirātaḥ parahiṃsakaḥ \|*  
*samabhyarcya śivaṃ bhaktyā lebhe sadgatimuttamām \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. A hunter Mahākāla, a wild Kirāta habitually injuring others, worshipped Śiva with devotion and attained the goal of the good.

## \|\| 9.2.37 \|\|

*durvāsā muniśārdūlaśśivānugrahataḥ purā \|*  
*tastāra svamataṃ loke śivabhaktiṃ vimuktidām \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Durvāsas a leading sage popularised his cult in the world, thanks to the blessings of Śiva. He spread Śiva’s cult of devotion that yields salvation to the devotee.

## \|\| 9.2.38 \|\|

*kauśikaśca samārādhya śaṃkaraṃ loka śaṃkaram \|*  
*brāhmaṇo'bhūtkṣatriyaśca dvitīya iva padmabhūḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Propitiating Śiva, the benefector of the worlds, Viśvāmitra a Kṣatriya became a brahmin. Ho was as though a second Brahmā since he created a new heaven.

## \|\| 9.2.39 \|\|

*śivamabhyarcya sadbhaktyā viraṃciśśaivasattamaḥ \|*  
*abhūtsargakaraḥ kṛṣṇa sarvalokapitāmahaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O Kṛṣṇa, by worshipping Śiva with devotion Brahmā, the most excellent devotee of Śiva, became the creator and grandfather of the worlds.

## \|\| 9.2.40 \|\|

*mārkaṇḍeyo munivaraściraṃjīvī mahāprabhuḥ \|*  
*śivabhaktavaraḥ śrīmāñśivānugrahato hare \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. O Kṛṣṇa, it was due to Śiva’s blessings that the excellent sage Mārkaṇḍeya, the holy lord and foremost among Śiva’s devotees, became long-lived.

## \|\| 9.2.41 \|\|

*devendro hi mahāśaivastrailokyaṃ bubhuje purā \|*  
*śivānugrahataḥ kṛṣṇa sarvadevādhipaḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. O Kṛṣṇa, thanks to Śiva’s blessings, Devendra, a great devotee of Śiva, enjoyed the three worlds formerly. He became the lord of all the gods.

## \|\| 9.2.42 \|\|

*baliputro mahāśaivaśśivānugrahato vaśī \|*  
*bāṇo babhūva brahmāṇḍanāyakassakaleśvaraḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Bāṇa, the son of Bali, was a great devotee of Śiva and had great control over senses. Thanks to Śiva’s blessings he became the leader of the universe and lord of all.

## \|\| 9.2.43-44 \|\|

*hariśśaktiśca sadbhaktyā dadhīcaśca maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*śivānugrahato'bhūvaṃstathā rāmo hi śāṃkaraḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*kaṇādo bhārgavaścaiva gururgautama eva ca \|*  
*śivabhaktyā babhūvuste mahāprabhava īśvarā \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. Śakti became Viṣṇu due to great devotion, Dadhīca became a great Īśvara, Rāma became a devotee of Śankara. Kāṇāda, Bhārgava, Guru and Gautama became great masters and lords due to devotion to Śiva.

## \|\| 9.2.45 \|\|

*śākalyaśśaṃsitātmā ca navavarṣaśātānyapi \|*  
*bhavamārādhayāmāsa manoyajñena mādhava \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. O Kṛṣṇa, Śākalya, the praiseworthy soul propitiated Śiva for nine hundred years by means of mental sacrifice.

## \|\| 9.2.46 \|\|

*tutoṣa bhagavānāha graṃthakartā bhaviṣyasi \|*  
*vatsākṣayyā ca te kīrtistrailokye prabhaviṣyati \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The lord was satisfied and said:—“O dear, you will become the writer of a book. Your fame in the three worlds will never fade.

## \|\| 9.2.47 \|\|

*akṣayaṃ ca kulaṃ te'stu maharṣibhiralaṃkṛtam \|*  
*bhaviṣyasi ṛṣiśreṣṭha sūtrakartā tatastataḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Your family will never be extinct. It will be adorned by the sages. O excellent sage, you will be the composer of Aphorisms later on.”

## \|\| 9.2.48 \|\|

*ityevaṃ śaṃkarātprāpa varaṃ munivarassa vai \|*  
*trailokye vitataścāsītpūjyaśca yadunandana \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. O descendant of Yadu, thus the great sage secured boons from Śiva and became well known and honoured in the three worlds.

## \|\| 9.2.49 \|\|

*sāvarṇiriti vikhyāta ṛṣirāsītkṛte yuge \|*  
*iha tena tapastaptaṃ ṣaṣṭivarṣaśatāni ca \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. In the Kṛta Yuga there was a sage famous as Sāvarṇi. He performed penance for six thousand years.

## \|\| 9.2.50 \|\|

*tamāha bhagavānrudrassākṣāttuṣṭosmi te'nagha \|*  
*graṃthakṛllokavikhyāto bhavitāsyajarāmaraḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Lord Rudra spoke to him directly—“O faultless one, I am satisfied with you. You will become the writer of a book and attain fame. You will be free from death and old age.”

## \|\| 9.2.51 \|\|

*evaṃvidho mahādevaḥ puṇyapūrvataraistataḥ \|*  
*samarccitaśśubhānkāmānpradadāti yathepsitān \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Such is lord Śiva. Worshipped by the meritorious devotees of yore he confers all auspicious desires as wished by them.

## \|\| 9.2.52 \|\|

*ekenaiva mukhenāhaṃ vaktuṃ bhagavato guṇāḥ \|*  
*ye saṃti tānna śaknomi hyapi varṣaśatairapi \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. I am incompetent to describe with a single mouth the qualities that exist in the lord even in hundreds of years.

## Chapter 3 - The greatness of Śiva

## \|\| 9.3.1 \|\|

*\|\| sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*etacchrutvā vacastasya sobravīttaṃ mahāmunim \|*  
*vismayaṃ paramaṃ gatvopamanyuṃ śāṃtamānasam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

1\. On hearing his words Kṛṣṇa was much surprised. He spoke to the sage Upamanyu of quiet mind.

## \|\| 9.3.2 \|\|

*vāsudeva uvāca \|*  
*dhanyastvamasi viprendra kastvāṃ stotumalaṃ kṛtī \|*  
*yasya devādidevaste sānnidhyaṃ kurute śrame \|\| 2 \|\|*

Kṛṣṇa said:—

2\. O great brahmin, you are blessed. Who can adequately eulogise you and be contented. In your hermitage the lord of the gods himself is ever present.

## \|\| 9.3.3 \|\|

*darśanaṃ muniśārdūla dadyātsa bhagavāñchivaḥ \|*  
*api tāvanmamāpyevaṃ prasādaṃ vā karotvasau \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. O leading sage, will the lord Śiva grant me his vision? Will he do me this favour?

## \|\| 9.3.4 \|\|

*upamanyuruvāca \|*  
*acireṇaiva kālena mahādevaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*tasyaiva kṛpayā tvaṃ vai drakṣyase puruṣottama \|\| 4 \|\|*

Upamanyu said:—

4\. O Puruṣottama, there is no doubt that ere long you will see lord Śiva, thanks to his own grace.

## \|\| 9.3.5 \|\|

*ṣoḍaśe māsi suvarān prāpsyasi tvaṃ maheśvarāt \|*  
*sapatnīkātkathaṃ no dāsyate devo varānhare \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. In the sixteenth month you will get good boons from lord Śiva and his wife. O Kṛṣṇa, why should not the lord grant you the boons?

## \|\| 9.3.6 \|\|

*pūjyosi daivataissarvaiḥ ślāghanīyassadā guṇaiḥ \|*  
*jāpyaṃ te'haṃ pravakṣyāmi śraddadhānāya cācyuta \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. O Viṣṇu, thanks to your good qualities, you deserve worship of the gods. You are always praiseworthy. I shall explain a mantra if you have faith enough.

## \|\| 9.3.7 \|\|

*tena japaprabhāveṇa satyaṃ drakṣyasi śaṃkaram \|*  
*ātmatulyabalaṃ putraṃ labhiṣyasi maheśvarāt \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. By the virtue of that mantra yon will see Śiva. You will acquire a son, equal in strength to you, by the favour of lord Śiva.

## \|\| 9.3.8 \|\|

*japo namaśśivāyeti maṃtrarājamimaṃ hare \|*  
*sarvakāmapradaṃ divyaṃ bhuktimuktipradāyakam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. O Kṛṣṇa, perform the Japa of this most efficacious mantra viz. “Om Namaḥ Śivāya.” It yields all desires. It is divine. It bestows worldly pleasures and salvation.”

## \|\| 9.3.9 \|\|

*\|\| sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ kathayatastasya mahādevāśritāḥ kathāḥ \|*  
*dinānyaṣṭau prayātāni muhūrtamiva tāpasa \|\| 9 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

9\. O sage, even as he was narrating these stories about lord Śiva, eight days passed by as it were a single Muhūrta.

## \|\| 9.3.10 \|\|

*navame tu dine prāpte muninā sa ca dīkṣitaḥ \|*  
*maṃtramadhyāpitaṃ śārvamātharvaśirasaṃ mahat \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. When the ninth day dawned he was initiated. He was instructed in Śiva’s mantra, the great one mentioned in the Atharvaveda.

## \|\| 9.3.11 \|\|

*jaṭī muṇḍī ca sadyo'sau babhūva susamāhitaḥ \|*  
*pādāṃguṣṭhoddhṛtatanustepe corddhvabhujastathā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Immediately he grew matted hair and then cleanshaven. With great purity of mind he began to perform penance standing on the big toes of the feet and lifting up the hands.

## \|\| 9.3.12 \|\|

*saṃprāpte ṣoḍaśe māsi saṃtuṣṭaḥ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*pārvatyā sahitaśśaṃbhurdadau kṛṣṇāya darśanam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. When the sixteenth month arrived lord Śiva was satisfied. Accompanied by Pārvatī, Śiva granted his vision to Kṛṣṇa.

## \|\| 9.3.13-16 \|\|

*pārvatyā sahitaṃ devaṃ trinetraṃ candraśekharam \|*  
*brahmādyaisstūyamānaṃ tu pūjitaṃ siddhakoṭibhiḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

*divyamālyāmbaradharaṃ bhaktinamraissurāsuraiḥ \|*  
*praṇataṃ ca viśeṣeṇa nānābhūṣaṇabhūṣitam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*sarvāścaryamayaṃ kāṃtaṃ maheśamajamavyayam \|*  
*nānāgaṇānvitaṃ tuṣṭaṃ putrābhyāṃ saṃyutaṃ prabhum \|\| 15 \|\|*

*śrīkṛṣṇaḥ prāṃjalirdṛṣṭvā vismayotphullalocanaḥ \|*  
*īdṛśaṃ śaṃkaraṃ prītaḥ praṇanāma mahotsavaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

13-16. Lord Kṛṣṇa saw Śiva thus:—He was accompanied by Pārvatī. The moon-crested lord had three eyes. He was being eulogised by Brahma and others and worshipped by crores of Siddhas. He was wearing divine garlands and cloths. He was being bowed to by gods and Asuras, humbled by devotion. He was bedecked in different ornaments. He was lustrous. The unborn and imperishable god was full of wondrous things. He was accompanied by various Gaṇas and his two sons. On seeing him lord Kṛṣṇa was much surprised. His eyes beemed with wonder. Delightedly and jubilantly he bowed to him,

## \|\| 9.3.17 \|\|

*nānāvidhaiḥ stutipadairvāṅmayenārcayattadā \|*  
*sahasranāmnā deveśaṃ tuṣṭāva natakaṃdharaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. He worshipped him with different hymns. With drooping shoulders he eulogised the lord of gods with the thousand names.

## \|\| 9.3.18 \|\|

*tato devāssagaṃdharvā vidyādharamahoragāḥ \|*  
*mumucuḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiṃ ca sādhuvādānmanonugān \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Thus the gods, Gandharvas, Vidyādharas and the great serpents showered flowers and congratulated him delightfully.

## \|\| 9.3.19 \|\|

*pārvatyāśca mukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhagavānbhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*uvāca keśavaṃ tuṣṭo rudraścātha biḍaujasā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Glancing at Pārvatī’s face the delighted lord Rudra, favourably disposed towards his devotees spoke to Keśava even as his splendour spread all round.

## \|\| 9.3.20 \|\|

*śrīmahādeva uvāca \|*  
*kṛṣṇaṃ jānāmi bhaktaṃ tvāṃ mayi nityaṃ dṛḍhavratam \|*  
*vṛṇīṣva tvaṃ varānmattaḥ puṇyāṃstrailokyadurlabhān \|\| 20 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

20\. O Kṛṣṇa, I know you are my staunch devotee, steady in my rites. Choose holy boons from me even if they be inaccessible to the people of the three worlds.

## \|\| 9.3.21 \|\|

*\|\| sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā kṛṣṇaḥ prāṃjalirādarāt \|*  
*prāha sarveśvaraṃ śambhuṃ supraṇamya punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

21\. On hearing his words Kṛṣṇa spoke to Śiva the lord of all, respectfully, with palms joined in reverence, after bowing to him again and again.

## \|\| 9.3.22 \|\|

*\|\| kṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*devadeva mahādeva yāce'haṃ hyuttamānvarān \|*  
*tvatto'ṣṭapramitānnātha tvayoddiṣṭānmaheśvara \|\| 22 \|\|*

Kṛṣṇa said:—

22\. O lord Śiva, lord of the gods, I solicit eight excellent boons from you, O Maheśvara, you have already thought of them.

## \|\| 9.3.23-25 \|\|

*tava dharmme matirnityaṃ yaśaścāpracalaṃ mahat \|*  
*tvatsāmīpyaṃ sthirā bhaktistvayi nityaṃ mamāstviti \|\| 23 \|\|*

*putrāṇi ca daśādyānāṃ putrāṇāṃ mama saṃtu vai \|*  
*vadhyāśca ripavassarve saṃgrāme baladarpitāḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

*apamāno bhavennaiva kvacinme śatrutaḥ prabho \|*  
*yogināmapi sarveṣāṃ bhaveyamativallabhaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

23-25. Let my mind be always in your cult. Let my fame be steady. Let me have nearness to you. Let my devotion to you be always unflinching. Let my senior sons have ten sons each. Let all my enemies who are arrogant by their might be killed in battle. O lord, nowhere shall I suffer dishonour from enemies. I shall be the beloved of all Yogins.

## \|\| 9.3.26 \|\|

*ityaṣṭau suvarāndehi devadeva namo'stu te \|*  
*sarveśvarastvamevāsi matprabhuśca viśeṣataḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. O lord of gods, grant me these eight good boons. Obeisance be to you. You alone are the lord of all and especially my master.

## \|\| 9.3.27 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā tamāha bhagavānbhavaḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ bhaviṣyatītyevaṃ punassa prāha śūladhṛk \|\| 27 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

27\. On hearing his words, lord Śiva said to him. Let your desire be fulfilled. After saying this the tridentbearing lord said again.

## \|\| 9.3.28 \|\|

*sāmbo nāma mahāvīryaḥ putraste bhavitā balī \|*  
*ghorasaṃvartakādityaśśapto munibhireva ca \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The terrible sun Saṃvartaka at the time of dissolution cursed by the sages will be born as your mighty and virile son Sāmba.

## \|\| 9.3.29 \|\|

*mānuṣo bhavitāsīti sa te putro bhavi ṣyati \|*  
*yadyacca prārthitaṃ kiṃcittatsarvaṃ ca labhasva vai \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. (The curse was) “You will be born as a man.” He will become your son. Whatever you have sought you will obtain.”

## \|\| 9.3.30 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ labdhvā varānsarvāñchrīkṛṣṇaḥ parameśvarāt \|*  
*nānāvidhābhirbahvībhisstutibhissamatoṣayat \|\| 30 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

30\. After getting the boons from lord Śiva, lord Kṛṣṇa delighted him with hymns.

## \|\| 9.3.31 \|\|

*tamāhātha śivā tuṣṭā pārvatī bhaktavatsalā \|*  
*vāsudevaṃ mahātmānaṃ śaṃbhubhaktaṃ tapasvinam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The delighted Pārvatī favourably disposed to her devotees, spoke to lord Kṛṣṇa the noble soul, the devotee of Śiva and who had performed the penance of lord Śiva.

## \|\| 9.3.32 \|\|

*\|\| pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*vāsudeva mahābuddhe kṛṣṇa tuṣṭāsmi te'nagha \|*  
*gṛhāṇa mattaśca varānmanojñānbhuvi durlabhān \|\| 32 \|\|*

Pārvatī said:—

32\. O Kṛṣṇa of great intellect, O sinless son of Vasudeva, I am delighted with you. Take from me too some boons that are rare in the world.

## \|\| 9.3.33 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇya vacastasyāḥ pārvatyāssa yadūdvahaḥ \|*  
*uvāca suprasannātmā bhaktiyuktena cetasā \|\| 33 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

33\. On hearing the words of Pārvatī the leading descendant of Yadu, very much delighted in his heart, spoke with great devotion in his mind.

## \|\| 9.3.34-35 \|\|

*śrīkṛṣṇa uvāca \|*  
*devi tvaṃ parituṣṭāsi ceddadāsi varānhi me \|*  
*tapasā'nena satyena brāhmaṇānprati māsmabhūt \|\| 34 \|\|*

*dveṣaḥ kadācidbhadraṃ pūjayeyaṃ dvijānsadā \|*  
*tuṣṭau ca mātāpitarau bhavetāṃ mama sarvadā \|\| 35 \|\|*

Lord Kṛṣṇa said:—

34-35. O goddess, if you are satisfied, if you are going to giant me boons due to my penance, truly let not my heart be inimical to brahmins. Let it be full of good will. I shall always worship the brahmins. My parents shall remain pleased with me.

## \|\| 9.3.36 \|\|

*sarvabhūteṣvānukūlyaṃ bhajeyaṃ yatra tatragaḥ \|*  
*kule prabhṛti rucitā mamāstu tava darśanāt \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Wherever I go let me be agreeable to all living beings. As a result of seeing you, let there be the birth of deserving progeny in my family.

## \|\| 9.3.37 \|\|

*tarpayeyaṃ surendrādīndevān yajñaśatena tu \|*  
*yatīnāmatithīnāṃ ca sahasrāṇyatha sarvadā \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. I shall propitiate Indra and other gods with a hundred sacrifices and honour thousands of ascetics and guests always.

## \|\| 9.3.38 \|\|

*bhojayeyaṃ sadā gehe śraddhāpūtaṃ tu bhojanam \|*  
*bāṃdhavaissaha prītistu nityamastu sunirvṛtiḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Let me feed every one at my house with dishes sanctified by faith. Let there be pleasure and excellent satisfaction amongst the kinsmen.

## \|\| 9.3.39 \|\|

*devi bhāryyāsahasrāṇāṃ bhaveyaṃ prāṇavallabhaḥ \|*  
*akṣīṇā kāmyatā tāsu prasādāttava śāṃkari \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O goddess, consort of Śiva, I shall be the beloved lover of thousands of wives. Let my love appeal to them and remain unfading and unfailing.

## \|\| 9.3.40 \|\|

*āsāṃ ca pitaro loke bhaveyuḥ satyāvādinaḥ \|*  
*ityādyāḥ suvarāssaṃtu prasādāttava pārvati \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Let their parents be the speakers of truth in the world. O Pārvatī, thanks to your grace, let these and other excellent boons take effect.

## \|\| 9.3.41 \|\|

*\|\| sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā devī taṃ cāha vismitā \|*  
*evamastviti bhadraṃ te śāśvatī sarvakāmadā \|\| 41 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

41\. On hearing his words, the eternal goddess, the bestower of all desires, spoke to him in surprise “Hail to you. Let it be so.”

## \|\| 9.3.42 \|\|

*tasmiṃstāṃśca varāndattvā pārvatīparameśvarau \|*  
*tatraivāṃtaśca dadhatuḥ kṛtvā kṛṣṇasya satkṛpām \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. After granting Kṛṣṇa these boons and blessing him mercifully Pārvatī and Śiva vanished there itself.

## \|\| 9.3.43 \|\|

*kṛṣṇaḥ kṛtārthamātmānamamanyata munīśvaraḥ \|*  
*upamanyormunarāśu prāpāśramamanuttamam \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. O great sage, Kṛṣṇa, felt as if he had achieved his purpose. Immediately he went to the excellent hermitage of the sage Upamanyu.

## \|\| 9.3.44 \|\|

*praṇamya śirasā tatra taṃ muniṃ keśihā tataḥ \|*  
*tayā vṛttaṃ ca tasmai tatsamācaṣṭopamanyave \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Bowing his head to the sage, Kṛṣṇa, the slayer of Keśin, narrated all the details to Upamanyu.

## \|\| 9.3.45 \|\|

*sa ca taṃ prāha ko'nyassyāccharvāddevājjanārddana \|*  
*mahādānapatirloke krodhe vā'tīva dussahaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

He said:—

45\. “O Janārdana, except lord Śiva who else can there be the lord of great gifts or very unbearable if infuriated?

## \|\| 9.3.46 \|\|

*jñāne tapasi vā śauryye sthairyye vā pada eva ca \|*  
*śṛṇu śaṃbhostu govinda devaiśvaryyaṃ mahāyaśāḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. O Kṛṣṇa of great fame, listen to the lordly glory of Śiva about his perfect knowledge, austerity, heroism and fortitude during dangers.

## \|\| 9.3.47 \|\|

*tacchrutvā śraddhayā yukto'bhavacchaṃbhostu bhaktimān \|*  
*papraccha śivamāhātmyaṃ sa taṃ prāha munīśvaraḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. On hearing that, Kṛṣṇa became endowed with faith and devotion to Śiva. He asked him about Śiva’s glory. The great sage narrated thus.

## \|\| 9.3.48 \|\|

*upamanyuruvāca \|*  
*bhagavāñśaṃkaraḥ pūrvaṃ brahmaloke mahātmanā \|*  
*stuto nāmasahasreṇa daṇḍinā brahmayoginā \|\| 48 \|\|*

Upamanyu said:—

48\. Formerly in the region of Brahmā lord Śiva was eulogised with his thousand names by the noble-souled Brahmā bearing the staff.

## \|\| 9.3.49 \|\|

*sāṃkhyāḥ paṭhaṃti tadgītaṃ vistīrṇaṃ ca nighaṃṭavat \|*  
*durjñānaṃ mānuṣāṇāṃ tu stotraṃ tatsarvakāmadam \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. The Sāṃkhyas read that song of prayer as it were a big lexicon. That hymn bestows all desires on men though inscrutable to them.

## \|\| 9.3.50 \|\|

*smarannityaṃ śaṃkaraṃ tvaṃ gaccha kṛṣṇa gṛhaṃ sukhī \|*  
*bhaviṣyasi sadā tāta śivabhaktagaṇāgraṇīḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. O Kṛṣṇa, return home happily remembering Śiva constantly. O dear, you will always be the foremost among Śiva’s devotees.

## \|\| 9.3.51 \|\|

*ityuktastaṃ namaskṛtya vāsudevo munīśvaram \|*  
*manasā saṃsmarañśaṃbhuṃ keśavo dvārakāṃ yayau \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Thus permitted by him, Keśava the son of Vasudeva made obeisance to the sage and returned to Dvārakā mentally remembering Śiva.

## \|\| 9.3.52 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ kṛṣṇassamārādhya śaṃkaraṃ lokaśaṃkaram \|*  
*kṛtārtho'bhūnmuniśreṣṭha sarvājeyo'bhavattathā \|\| 52 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

52\. O great sage, Kṛṣṇa became contented and invincible after propitiating Śiva, the benefactor of the worlds.

## \|\| 9.3.53 \|\|

*tathā dāśarathī rāmaśśivamārādhya bhaktitaḥ \|*  
*kṛtārtho'bhūnmuniśreṣṭha vijayī sarvato'bhavat \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Similarly, O great sage, Rāma, the son of Daśaratha, devoutly propitiated Śiva and became contented and victorious everywhere.

## \|\| 9.3.54 \|\|

*tapastaptvā'tivipulaṃ purā rāmo girau mune \|*  
*śivāddhanuśśaraṃ cāpaṃ jñānaṃ vai paramuttamam \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. O sage, after performing a great penance on the mountain, Rāma secured from Śiva a bow and an arrow and the excellent knowledge.

## \|\| 9.3.55 \|\|

*rāvaṇaṃ sagaṇaṃ hatvā setuṃ baddhvāṃbhasāṃnidhau \|*  
*sītāṃ prāpya gṛhaṃ yāto bubhuje nikhilāṃ mahīm \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. He built the bridge on the ocean, killed Rāvaṇa with his followers, regained Śītā, returned home and enjoyed the whole earth.

## \|\| 9.3.56-57 \|\|

*tathā ca bhārgavo rāmo hyārādhya tapasā vibhum \|*  
*nirīkṣya duḥkhitaśśarvātpitaraṃ kṣatriyairhatam \|\| 56 \|\|*

*tīkṣṇaṃ sa paraśuṃ lebhe nirdadāha ca tena tān \|*  
*trissaptakṛtvaḥ kṣatrāṃśca prasannātparameśvarāt \|\| 57 \|\|*

56-57. Similarly Paraśurāma, who was distressed on seeing his father killed by the Kṣatriyas, propitiated lord Śiva by his penance. From the delighted lord Śiva he secured a sharp-edged axe. With that he exterminated the Kṣatriyas twenty-one times.

## \|\| 9.3.58 \|\|

*ajeyaścāmaraścaiva so'dyāpi tapasāṃnidhiḥ \|*  
*liṃgārcanarato nityaṃ dṛśyate siddhacāraṇaiḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Even today Siddhas and Cāraṇas sec this storehouse of penance deathless and invincible engaged in worshipping the phallic image.

## \|\| 9.3.59 \|\|

*mahendraparvate rāmaḥ sthitastapasi tiṣṭhati \|*  
*kalpāṃte punarevāsāvṛṣisthānamavāpsyati \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Rāma stays on the mountain Mahendra and performs penance. At the end of the Kalpa he will attain the region of the sage.

## \|\| 9.3.60-61 \|\|

*asitasyānujaḥ pūrvaṃ pīḍayā kṛtavāṃstapaḥ \|*  
*mūlagrāheṇa viśvasya devalo nāma tāpasaḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

*purandareṇa śaptastu tapasvī yaśca susthiram \|*  
*adharmyaṃ dharmamala malliṃgamāradhya kāmadam \|\| 61 \|\|*

60-61. The ascetic Devala, younger brother of Asita, harassed the universe formerly by clutching at its root. He was then cursed by the lord, destroyer of cities. He performed penance by propitiating the phallic image that bestows all desires and removes the past evil.

## \|\| 9.3.62-63 \|\|

*cākṣuṣasya manoḥ putro mṛgo'bhūttu marusthale \|*  
*vasiṣṭhaśāpādgṛtsamado daṇḍakāraṇya ekalaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

*hṛdaye saṃsmanbhaktyā pravaṇena yutaṃ śivam \|*  
*tasmānmṛtyumukhākāro gaṇo mṛgamukho'bhavat \|\| 63 \|\|*

62-63. Gṛtsamada, son of Cākṣuṣamanu became a deer in the desert in the Daṇḍaka forest by the curse of Vasiṣṭha. Alone he roamed about, devoutly remembering in his heart, Śiva along with the Praṇava oṃ. After his death he became a deer-faced Gaṇa.

## \|\| 9.3.64 \|\|

*ajarāmaratāṃ nītastīrtvā śāpaṃ punaśca saḥ \|*  
*śaṃkareṇa kṛtaḥ prītyā nityaṃ lambodarānugaḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Thus passing through the curse he was made free from death and old age by Śiva and lovingly appointed as a permanent follower of Gaṇeśa.

## \|\| 9.3.65-66 \|\|

*gārgyāya pradadau śarvo mokṣaṃ ca bhuvi durlabham \|*  
*kāmacārī mahākṣetraṃ kālajñānaṃ maharddhimat \|\| 65 \|\|*

*catuṣpādaṃ sarasvatyāḥ pāraṃgatvaṃ ca śāśvatam \|*  
*na tulyaṃ ca sahasraṃ tu putrāṇāṃ pradadau śivaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

65-66. To Gārgya Śiva granted salvation rare in the world, the ability to go as he pleased in holy centres, the knowledge of all time coupled with prosperity, and permanent mastery of the words of the four Vedas. Śiva granted him a thousand peerless sons too.

## \|\| 9.3.67 \|\|

*vedavyāsaṃ tu yogīndraṃ putraṃ tuṣṭaḥ pināka dhṛk \|*  
*parāśarāya ca dadau jarāmṛtyuvivarjitam \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. The satisfied lord Śiva granted to Parāśara, a powerful Yogin as the son who was free from old age and death and was known as Vedavyāsa.

## \|\| 9.3.68 \|\|

*māṃḍavyaśśaṃkaraṇaiva jīvaṃ dattvā visarjitaḥ \|*  
*varṣāṇāṃ daśa lakṣāṇi śūlāgrā davaropitaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. Māṇḍavya who had been fixed to a sake for a million years was released by Śiva and blessed with longevity.

## \|\| 9.3.69-77 \|\|

*daridro brāhmaṇaḥ kaścinnikṣipya guruveśmani \|*  
*putraṃ tu gālavaṃ yaśca pūrvamāsīdgṛhāśramī \|\| 69 \|\|*

*gupto vā muniśālāyāṃ bhikṣurāyāti tadgṛham \|*  
*bhāryyāmuvāca yaḥ kaścidavaśyaṃ nirdhano yataḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

*sa tu vācyo bhavatyā ca na dṛśyaṃta iti priyaḥ \|*  
*atitherāgatasyāpi kiṃ dāsyāmi gṛhe vasan \|\| 71 \|\|*

*kadācidatithiḥ kaścitkṣuttṛṣākṣāmatarṣitaḥ \|*  
*tāmuvāca sa bhartā te kva gataśceti taṃ ca sā \|\| 72 \|\|*

*prāha bhartā madīyastu sāṃprataṃ na ca dṛśyate \|*  
*sa ṛṣistāmuvācedaṃ jñātvā divyena cakṣuṣā \|\| 73 \|\|*

*gṛhasthitaḥ praticchannastatraiva sa mṛto dvijaḥ \|*  
*viśvāmitrasyanujñātastatputro gālavastathā \|\| 74 \|\|*

*gṛhamāgatya mātussa śrutvā śāpaṃ sudāruṇam \|*  
*ārādhya śaṃkaraṃ devaṃ pūjāṃ kṛtvā tu śāṃbhavīm \|\| 75 \|\|*

*gṛhādasau viniṣkrāṃtassaṃsmarañśaṃkaraṃ hṛdā \|*  
*atha taṃ tanayaṃ dṛṣṭvā pitā taṃ prāha sāñjalim \|\| 76 \|\|*

*mahādevaprasādācca kṛtakṛtyo'smi kṛtyataḥ \|*  
*dhanavānputravāṃścaiva mṛto'haṃ jīvitaḥ punaḥ \|\| 77 \|\|*

69-77. Formerly there was a poor brahmin householder. He had kept his son Gālava in the house of his preceptor. Whenever any mendicant visited his house this brahmin hid himself. He used to tell his wife, “No doubt I am a householder. But what can I offer to a guest? If any poor man comes you shall tell him “My husband is not here.” It happened once that a guest oppressed by thirst and hunger visited the house. He asked the lady “Where is your husband? Where has he gone?” She replied “My husband is not here.” Knowing the truth by his divine vision the sage said, “He is hidden inside the house.” The brahmin died in his hiding place. When permitted by his preceptor Viśvāmitra, the son of the brahmin, Gālava returned home and heard from his mother the terrible calamity that had overtaken them. He propitiated lord Śiva and performed Śiva’s worship. When he went out of his house remembering Śiva in his heart, he saw his father alive again who on seeing the son standing with palms joined in reverence said—“Thanks to the favour of lord Śiva I am blessed and satisfied. I have been resuscitated to life again and also blessed with wealth”.

## \|\| 9.3.78 \|\|

*iti vaḥ kathitamaśeṣaṃ nāhaṃ śaktaḥ samāsato vyāsāt \|*  
*vaktuṃ śaṃbhośca guṇāñśeṣasyāpi na mukhāni syuḥ \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. Thus everything has been narrated to you. I am really not competent to express the qualities of Śiva in brief or in detail. Even Śeṣa’s tongues may not be competent for the task.

## Chapter 4 - The exhibition of Siva’s spell

## \|\| 9.4.1 \|\|

*munaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*tātatāta mahābhāga dhanyastvaṃ hi mahāmate \|*  
*adbhuteyaṃ kathā śaṃbhoḥ śrāvitā parabhaktidā \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O dear, O fortunate one, you are blessed, O dear one of great intellect. This wonderful narrative of Śiva that bestows great devotion has been narrated.

## \|\| 9.4.2 \|\|

*punarbrūhi kathāṃ śaṃbhorvyāsa praśnānusārataḥ \|*  
*sarvajñastvaṃ vyāsaśiṣyaḥ śivatattvavicakṣaṇaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Mention again the story of Śiva in accordance with Vyāsa’s enquiry. You are omniscient. You are the disciple of Vyāsa and an expert in Śiva’s principles.

## \|\| 9.4.3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evameva gururvyāsaḥ pṛṣṭavānme'jasaṃbhavam \|*  
*sanatkumāraṃ sarvajñaṃ śivabhaktaṃ munīśvaram \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. It was in this very same manner that my preceptor Vyāsa asked Sanatkumāra, son of Brahmā, who was an omniscient great sage and a devotee of Śiva.

## \|\| 9.4.4 \|\|

*vyāsa uvāca \|*  
*sanatkumāra sarvajña śrāviteyaṃ śubhā kadhā \|*  
*śaṃkarasya maheśasya nānālīlāvihāriṇaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

Vyāsa said:—

4\. O omniscient Sanatkumāra, this auspicious story of Śiva, the great lord indulging in different sports has been narrated to you.

## \|\| 9.4.5 \|\|

*punarbrūhi mahādeva mahimānaṃ viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*śraddhā ca mahatī śrotuṃ mama tāta pravarddhate \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Speak again particularly of the greatness of lord Śiva. O dear, my faith and eagerness to hear the same is great and it increases.

## \|\| 9.4.6 \|\|

*mahimnā yena śaṃbhostu yeye loke vimohitāḥ \|*  
*māyayā jñānamāhṛtya nānālīlāvihāriṇaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Who are the people deluded by the Māyā of Śiva who indulges in great sports? What is that greatness by which their knowledge has been deluded?

## \|\| 9.4.7 \|\|

*\|\| sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu vyāsa mahābuddhe śāṃkarīṃ sukhadāṃ kathām \|*  
*tasyāḥ śravaṇamātreṇa śive bhaktiḥ prajāyate \|\| 7 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

7\. O Vyāsa, of great intellect, listen to the pleasing story of Śiva, the mere hearing of which generates devotion to Śiva.

## \|\| 9.4.8 \|\|

*śivassarveśvaro devassarvātmā sarvadarśanaḥ \|*  
*mahimnā tasya sarvaṃ hi vyāptaṃ ca sakalaṃ jagat \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Lord Śiva is the lord of all, soul of all and the vision of all. The entire universe has been pervaded by his greatness.

## \|\| 9.4.9 \|\|

*śivasyaiva parā mūrtirbrahmaviṣṇvīśvarātmikā \|*  
*sarvabhūtātmabhūtākhyā triliṃgā liṃgarūpiṇī \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. It is the great image of Śiva that manifests as Brahmā. Viṣṇu and Śiva. It is that which has become the soul of all living beings. It is both three-symbolled and symbolless.

## \|\| 9.4.10 \|\|

*devānāṃ yonayaścāṣṭau mānuṣī navamī ca yā \|*  
*tiraścāṃ yonayaḥ paṃca bhavaṃtyevaṃ caturddaśa \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. There are eight classes of gods. Human beings constitute the ninth. There are five types of lower beings. Thus there are fourteen types of living beings.

## \|\| 9.4.11 \|\|

*bhūtā vā vartamānā vā bhaviṣyāścaiva sarvaśa \|*  
*śivātsarve pravartaṃte līyaṃte vṛddhimāgatāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. All living beings past, present and future originate from Śiva, flourish in him and finally merge into him.

## \|\| 9.4.12-13 \|\|

*brahmendropendracandrāṇāṃ devadānavabhoginām \|*  
*gaṃdharvāṇāṃ manuṣyāṇāmanyeṣāṃ vāpi sarvaśaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*baṃdhurmitramathācāryyo rakṣannetā'rthavānguruḥ \|*  
*kalpadrumo'tha vā bhrātā pitā mātā śivo mataḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. Śiva is the kinsman, friend, guide, protector, leader, purposeful preceptor, wish-yielding Kalpa tree, brother, father or mother of Brahmā, Indra, Viṣṇu, moon, the Gods, Dānavas, Serpents, Gandharvas, Human beings and all others.

## \|\| 9.4.14 \|\|

*śivassarvamayaḥ puṃsāṃ svayaṃ vedyaḥ parātparaḥ \|*  
*vaktuṃ na śakyate yaśca paraṃ cānu paraṃ ca yat \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Śiva is identical with all; self-manifest to all men, greater than the greatest. Than him it is impossible to predicate anyother greater thing.

## \|\| 9.4.15 \|\|

*tanmāyā paramā divyā sarvatra vyāpinī mune \|*  
*tadadhīnaṃ jagatsarvaṃ sadevāsuramānuṣam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. O sage, his Māyā is divine and great pervading everything. The entire universe is subservient to it, inclusive of the gods, Asuras and human beings.

## \|\| 9.4.16 \|\|

*kāmena svasahāyena prabalena manobhuvā \|*  
*sarvaḥ pradharṣito vīro viṣṇvādiḥ prabalo'pi hi \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. All heroic beings, even Viṣṇu and mighty people, have been overwhelmed by the mighty Kāma, born of the mind who has no other helper.

## \|\| 9.4.17 \|\|

*śivamāyāprabhāveṇābhūddhariḥ kāmamohitaḥ \|*  
*parastrīdharṣaṇaṃ cakre bahuvāraṃ munīśvara \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. O great sage, Viṣṇu was deluded by Kāma by the power of Śiva’s Māyā. He outraged the modesty of other men’s wives many times.

## \|\| 9.4.18 \|\|

*indrastridaśapo bhūtvā gautamastrīvimohitaḥ \|*  
*pāpaṃ cakāra duṣṭātmā śāpaṃ prāpa munestadā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Indra, the lord of the gods, became fascinated by Gautama’s wife. That vicious one committed sin and so was cursed by the sage.

## \|\| 9.4.19 \|\|

*pāvako'pi jagacchreṣṭho mohitaśśivamāyayā \|*  
*kāmādhīnaḥ kṛto garvāttatastenaiva coddhṛtaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Even the firegod, the most excellent in the universe was deluded by Śiva’s Māyā. Due to his pride he became subservient to lust and was ultimately saved by Śiva.

## \|\| 9.4.20 \|\|

*jagatprāṇo'pi garveṇa mohitaśśivamāyayā \|*  
*kāmena nirjito vyāsaścakre'nyastrīratiṃ purā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The wind-god, the vital air of the universe was deluded by Śiva’s Māyā due to his arrogance. Overwhelmed by Kāma, Vyāsa too, had sexual intercourse with other men’s wives.

## \|\| 9.4.21 \|\|

*caṇḍaraśmistu mārtaṇḍo mohitaśśivamāyayā \|*  
*kāmākulo babhūvāśu dṛṣṭvāśvīṃ hayarūpadhṛk \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The sun of fierce rays, deluded by Śiva’s Māyā became lustful on seeing a mare and assumed the form of a horse.

## \|\| 9.4.22 \|\|

*candraśca mohitaśśambhormāyayā kāmasaṃkulaḥ \|*  
*gurupatnīṃ jahārātha yutastenaiva coddhṛtaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The moon deluded by Śiva’s Māyā became lustful and abducted the wife of Bṛhaspati and had his sexual union with her. But he was finally saved by Bṛhaspati himself.

## \|\| 9.4.23 \|\|

*pūrvaṃ tu mitrāvaruṇau ghore tapasi saṃsthitau \|*  
*mohitau tāvapi munī śivamāyāvimohitau \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Formerly the two gods Mitra and Varuṇa, while performing a severe penance, were fascinated and deluded by Śīva’s Māyā.

## \|\| 9.4.24 \|\|

*urvaśīṃ taruṇīṃ dṛṣṭvā kāmuko saṃbabhūvatuḥ \|*  
*mitraḥ kumbhe jahau reto varuṇo'pi tathā jale \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. On seeing the youthful maiden Ūrvaśī, both of them had the emission of their semen. Mitra deposited his semen in a pot and Varuṇa in water.

## \|\| 9.4.25 \|\|

*tataḥ kumbhātsamutpanno vasiṣṭho mitrasaṃbhavaḥ \|*  
*agastyo varuṇājjāto vaḍavāgnisamadyutiḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Vasiṣṭha was born out of the pot and is called Mitra’s son. Agastya born of Varuṇa has the lustre of the submarine fire.

## \|\| 9.4.26 \|\|

*dakṣaśca mohitaśśaṃbhormāyayā brahmaṇassutaḥ \|*  
*bhrātṛbhissa bhaginyāṃ vai bhoktukāmo'bhavatpurā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Dakṣa, son of Brahmā deluded by Śiva’s Māyā, had a desire for sexual intercourse with his sister along with his other brothers.

## \|\| 9.4.27 \|\|

*brahmā ca bahuvāraṃ hi mohitaśśivamāyayā \|*  
*abhavadbhoktukāmaśca svasutāyāṃ parāsu ca \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Many times, Brahmā, deluded by Śiva’s Māyā, had the desire for sexual union with his own daughter and other women.

## \|\| 9.4.28 \|\|

*cyavano'pi mahāyogī mohitaśśivamāyayā \|*  
*sukanyayā vijahre sa kāmāsakto babhūva ha \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Gyavana the great Yogin. deluded by Śiva’s Māyā, became lustful and had sexual dalliance with Sukanyā.

## \|\| 9.4.29 \|\|

*kaśyapaḥ śivamāyāto mohitaḥ kāmasaṃkulaḥ \|*  
*yayāce kanyakāṃ mohāddhanvano nṛpateḥ purā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Kaśyapa became passionate on being deluded by Śiva’s Māyā. Formerly, out of delusion, he requested king Dhanvan to give his daughter to him.

## \|\| 9.4.30 \|\|

*garuḍaḥ śāṃḍilīṃ kanyāṃ netukāmassumohitaḥ \|*  
*vijñātastu tayā sadyo dagdhapakṣo babhūva ha \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The deluded Garuḍa while abducting the virgin Śāṇḍilī was found out by her and got his wings burnt.

## \|\| 9.4.31 \|\|

*vibhāṃḍako munirnārīṃ dṛṣṭvā kāmavaśaṃ gataḥ \|*  
*ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ sutastasya mṛgyāṃ jātaśśivājñayā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The sage Vibhāṇḍaka became lustful on seeing a woman. At the behest of Śiva, Ṛṣyaśṛṅga was his son born of a hind.

## \|\| 9.4.32-33 \|\|

*gautamaśca muniśśaṃbhormāyāmohitamānasaḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā śāradvatīṃ nagnāṃ rarāma kṣubhitastayā \|\| 32 \|\|*

*retaḥ skannaṃ dadhāra svaṃ droṇyāṃ caiva sa tāpasaḥ \|*  
*tasmācca kalaśājjāto droṇaśśastrabhṛtāṃ varaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. The sage Gautama had his mind deluded by Śiva’s Māyā. On seeing Śāradvatī in the nude he was excited and he indulged in sexual intercourse with her. He collected the semen emitted in a wooden bowl. Droṇa the foremost among marksmen was born of that bowl.

## \|\| 9.4.34 \|\|

*parāśaro mahāyogī mohitaśśivamāyayā \|*  
*matsyodaryā ca cikrīḍe kumāryā dāśakanyayā \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The great yogin Parāśara, deluded by Śiva’s Māyā, indulged in sexual intercourse with Matsyodarī the virgin daughter of a fisherman.

## \|\| 9.4.35 \|\|

*viśvamitro babhūvātha mohitaśśivamāyayā \|*  
*reme menakayā vyāsa vane kāmavaśaṃ gataḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. O Vyāsa, Viśvāmitra became deluded by śiva’s Māyā and lustfully indulged in sexual dalliance with Menakā.

## \|\| 9.4.36 \|\|

*vasiṣṭhena virodhaṃ tu kṛtavānnaṣṭacetanaḥ \|*  
*punaḥ śivaprāsādācca brāhmaṇo'bhūtsa eva vai \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Becoming confounded in thought he rivalled with Vasiṣṭha. But thanks to Śiva’s grace he became a brahmin.

## \|\| 9.4.37 \|\|

*rāvaṇo vaiśravāḥ kāmī babhūva śivamāyayā \|*  
*sītāṃ jahre kubuddhistu mohito mṛtyumāpa ca \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Rāvaṇa, the son of Viśravas, became lustful due to Śiva’s Māyā. The wicked-minded one abducted Sītā, became deluded and courted death.

## \|\| 9.4.38 \|\|

*bṛhaspatirmunivaro mohitaśśivamāyayā \|*  
*bhrātṛpatnyā vaśī reme bharadvājastato'bhavat \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The excellent sage Bṛhaspati deluded by Śiva’s Māyā had sexual intercourse with his brother’s wife and Bharadvāja was born.

## \|\| 9.4.39 \|\|

*iti māyāprabhāvo hi śaṃkarasya mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*varṇitaste mayā vyāsa kimanyacchrotumicchasi \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O Vyāsa, thus the power of the Māyā of Śiva, the great soul has been narrated to you. What else do you wish to hear?

## Chapter 5 - The Great Sins (mahā-pāpa)

## \|\| 9.5.1 \|\|

*vyāsa uvāca \|*  
*ye pāpaniratā jīvā mahānarakahetavaḥ \|*  
*bhagavaṃstānsamācakṣva brahmaputra namo'stu te \|\| 1 \|\|*

Note: Manu differs in regard to the number of sins (pāpa) arising from the misuse of the mind, tongue and body. According to him the sins of the mind are three, those of the speech are four and the those of the body are three (Manu 12.5).

Vyāsa said:—

1\. O holy lord, son of Brahmā, obeisance be to you. Please describe those living beings who are engaged in sins causing their fall into great hells.

## \|\| 9.5.2 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*ye pāpaniratā jīvā mahānarakahetavaḥ \|*  
*te samāsena kathyaṃte sāvadhānatayā śṛṇu \|\| 2 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

2\. I describe to you in brief the living beings who are engaged in sins that cause their fall into great hells. Listen attentively.

## \|\| 9.5.3 \|\|

*parastrīdravyasaṃkalpaścetasā'niṣṭaciṃtanam \|*  
*akāryābhiniveśaśca caturddhā karma mānasam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Pondering over other men’s wives and wealth, wishing for the ill of others mentally, conception of various evil actions and ardent longing for various mean acts, these are the four types of mental activity.

## \|\| 9.5.4 \|\|

*avibaddhapralāpatvamasatyaṃ cāpriyaṃ ca yat \|*  
*parokṣataśca paiśunyaṃ caturddhā karma vācikam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Unconnected jabber, untruthful utterance, displeasing words and backbiting, these are the four types of verbal activity.

## \|\| 9.5.5 \|\|

*abhakṣyabhakṣaṇaṃ hiṃsā mithyākāryya niveśanam \|*  
*parasvānāmupādānaṃ caturddhā karma kāyikam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Eating forbidden food, violence, wild goose chase and pilfering of other’s property, these are the four types of physical activity.

## \|\| 9.5.6 \|\|

*ityetadvā daśavidhaṃ karma proktaṃ trisādhanam \|*  
*asya bhedānpunarvakṣye yeṣāṃ phalamanaṃtakam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Thus there are twelve types of activity intended to achieve three aims. I shall explain their further subdivisions, the results of which are endless and manifold.

## \|\| 9.5.7 \|\|

*ye dviṣaṃti mahādevaṃ saṃsārārṇavatārakam \|*  
*sumahatpātakaṃ teṣāṃ nirayārṇavagāminām \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Very great is the sin of those who hate lord Śiva who enables people to cross the ocean of worldly existence. They are sure to be immersed in the ocean of hell.

## \|\| 9.5.8 \|\|

*ye śivajñānavaktāraṃ nindaṃti ca tapasvinam \|*  
*gurūnpitṝnathonmattāste yāṃti nirayārṇavam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Those who censure the propounder of Śiva’s perfect knowledge, ascetics, preceptors or parents are mad. They fall into the ocean of hell.

## \|\| 9.5.9-10 \|\|

*śivanindā gurornindā śivajñānasya dūṣaṇam \|*  
*devadravyāpaharaṇaṃ dvijadravyavināśanam \|\| 9 \|\|*

*haraṃti ye ca saṃmūḍhāśśivajñānasya pustakam \|*  
*mahāṃti pātakānyāhuranantaphaladāni ṣaṭ \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. The following six are the great sins attended with endless evil results, viz:—censure of Śiva, censure of the preceptor, censure of Śiva’s perfect knowledge, misappropriation of the wealth of the lord, destruction of the wealth of brahmins and the foolish stealing of the sacred text of Śiva’s perfect knowledge.

## \|\| 9.5.11-22 \|\|

*nābhinandaṃti ye dṛṣṭvā śivapūjāṃ prakalpitām \|*  
*na namaṃtyarcitaṃ dṛṣṭvā śivaliṃgaṃ stuvaṃti na \|\| 11 \|\|*

*yatheṣṭaceṣṭā niśśaṃkāssaṃtiṣṭhaṃte ramaṃti ca \|*  
*upacāravinirmuktāśśivāgre gurusannidhau \|\| 12 \|\|*

*sthānasaṃskārapūjāṃ ca ye na kurvaṃti parvasu \|*  
*vidhivadvā gurūṇāṃ ca karmmayogavyavasthitāḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

*ye tyajaṃti śivācāraṃ śivabhaktāndviṣaṃti ca \|*  
*asaṃpūjya śivajñānaṃ ye'dhīyaṃte likhaṃti ca \|\| 14 \|\|*

*anyāyataḥ prayacchaṃti śṛṇvantyuccārayaṃti ca \|*  
*vikrīḍaṃti ca lobhena kujñānaniyamena ca \|\| 15 \|\|*

*asaṃskṛtapradeśeṣu yatheṣṭaṃ svāpayaṃti ca \|*  
*śivajñānakathā''kṣepaṃ yaḥ kṛtvānyatprabhāṣate \|\| 16 \|\|*

*na bravīti ca yaḥ satyaṃ na pradānaṃ karoti ca \|*  
*aśucirvā'śucisthāne yaḥ pravakti śṛṇoti ca \|\| 17 \|\|*

*gurupūjāmakṛtvaiva yaśśāstraṃ śrotumicchati \|*  
*na karoti ca śuśrūṣāmājñāṃ ca bhaktibhāvataḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

*nābhinandaṃti tadvākyamuttaraṃ ca prayacchati \|*  
*gurukarmaṇyasādhyaṃ yattadupekṣāṃ karoti ca \|\| 19 \|\|*

*gurumārttamaśaktaṃ ca videśaṃ prasthitaṃ tathā \|*  
*vairibhiḥ paribhūtaṃ vā yassaṃtyajati pāpakṛt \|\| 20 \|\|*

*tadbhāryyāputramitreṣu yaścāvajñāṃ karoti ca \|*  
*evaṃ suvācakasyāpi gurordharmānudarśinaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*etāni khalu sarvāṇi karmāṇi munisattama \|*  
*sumahatpātakānyāhuśśivanindāsamāni ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

11-22. The following too are great sins and those who commit them are great sinners. Those who do not take delight on seeing a well-arranged worship of Śiva, who do not bow to or eulogise it on seeing his phallic image that is worshipped, those who do not scrub, clean and sanctify the spot of worship during festival days; those who do not duly cooperate with the preceptor in their sacred rites; those who misbehave as they please, play about mischievously and do not render service in front of Śiva or in the presence of the preceptor; those who eschew Śaiva course of conduct and discipline, those who hate Śiva’s devotees; those who begin to study or write about Śiva’s knowledge without worshipping; those who give without justice or justification; those who listen or recite indiscriminately; those who sport about covetously; those who pursue false knowledge or do not have proper rules and regulations; those who lie down and sleep in dirty uncleaned spots; he who abuses Śiva’s story and knowledge and begins to expatiate on other things; he who does not speak the truth; he who does notmake gifts; he who is bodily impure and begins to explain or listen to Śiva’s story in an unclean place; he who begins to listen without worshipping the preceptor; he who does not render service to him or pay heed to his behests with devotion; he who does not support the preceptor’s statement; he who retorts to his preceptor; he who conveniently ignores the most difficult task of his preceptor; he who deserts his preceptor when he is in distress, or when he is unable to maintain the disciple, or when he has gone abroad or when he is attacked by enemies; he who treats with contempt the teacher of virtuous activities and ability to discourse; he who disrespects the teacher’s wife, son or friends. O excellent sage, these activities are as sinful as censure of Śiva.

## \|\| 9.5.23 \|\|

*brahmaghnaśca surāpaśca steyī ca gurutalpagaḥ \|*  
*mahāpātakinastvete tatsaṃyogī ca paṃcamaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The murderer of a brahmin, the addict to wine, the habitual thief, the defiler of the preceptor’s bed and he who associates with these is the great sinner fifth in all.

## \|\| 9.5.24 \|\|

*krodhāllobhādbhayāddveṣādbrāhmaṇasya vadhe tu yaḥ \|*  
*marmāṃtikaṃ mahādoṣamuktvā sa brahmahā bhavet \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. He who kills a brahmin out of fury, greed, fear or hatred or uses heart-rending taunts becomes the slayer of a brahmin.

## \|\| 9.5.25 \|\|

*brāhmaṇaṃ yaḥ samāhūya dattvā yaścādadāti ca \|*  
*nirddoṣaṃ dūṣayedyastu sa naro brahmahā bhavet \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. He who invite? a brahmin and makes him some gift but later on takes it back and criticises him and rebukes him without any fault is the slayer of a brahmin.

## \|\| 9.5.26 \|\|

*yaśca vidyābhimānena nistejayati sudvijam \|*  
*udāsīnaṃ sabhāmadhye brahmahā sa prakīrtitaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. He who, being arrogant of his gift of learning, puts to shame any good brahmin keeping silence and indifferent in the assembly is also a brahmin-slayer.

## \|\| 9.5.27 \|\|

*mithyāguṇairya ātmānaṃ nayatyutkarṣatāṃ balāt \|*  
*guṇānapi nirudvāsya sa ca vai brahmahā bhavet \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. He who pretends to have the qualities he does not have and gains recognition for the same and he who conceals his bad attributes is a brahmin-slayer.

## \|\| 9.5.28 \|\|

*gavāṃ vṛṣābhibhūtānāṃ dvijānāṃ gurupūrvakam \|*  
*yassamācarate vipra tamāhurbrahmaghātakam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. He who hinders cows when bulls mate with them or brahmins when they seek preceptors is called a brahmin-slayer.

## \|\| 9.5.29 \|\|

*devadvijagavāṃ bhūmiṃ pradattāṃ harate tu yaḥ \|*  
*pranaṣṭāmapi kālena tamāhurbrahmaghātakam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. He who forcibly occupies the land given in support of the temples, brahmins or cows, though the lease might have lapsed due to efflux of time, is called a brahmin-slayer.

## \|\| 9.5.30 \|\|

*devadvijasvaharaṇamanyāyenārjitaṃ tu yat \|*  
*brahmahatyāsamaṃ jñeyaṃ pātakaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The misappropriation of the wealth of the deity or a brahmin and wealth earned through injustice js no less than the sin of slaying a brahmin undoubtedly.

## \|\| 9.5.31 \|\|

*adhītya yo dvijo vedaṃ brahmajñānaṃ śivātmakam \|*  
*yadi tyajati yo mūḍhaḥ surāpānasya tatsamam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. If a brahmin learns the Vedas and acquires knowledge of Brahman and Śiva, but eschews it later on he commits a sin equal to that of drinking wine.

## \|\| 9.5.32 \|\|

*yatkiṃciddhi vrataṃ gṛhya niyamaṃ yajanaṃ tathā \|*  
*saṃtyāgaḥ pañcayajñānāṃ surāpānasya tatsamam \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The abandonment of sacred rites, worships and the five daily sacrifices, after performing the same for some time is attended with a sin equal to that of the drinking wine.

## \|\| 9.5.33-35 \|\|

*pitṛmātṛparityāgaḥ kūṭasākṣyaṃ dvijānṛtam \|*  
*āmiṣaṃ śivabhaktānāmabhakṣyasya ca bhakṣaṇam \|\| 33 \|\|*

*vane niraparādhānāṃ prāṇināṃ cāpaghātanam \|*  
*dvijārthaṃ prakṣipetsādhurna dharmārthaṃ niyojayet \|\| 34 \|\|*

*gavāṃ mārge vane grāme yaiścaivāgniḥ pradīyate \|*  
*iti pāpāni ghorāṇi brahmahatyāsamāni ca \|\| 35 \|\|*

33-35. These are terrible sins (pāpa) like the murder of a brahmin:—abandonment of parents, perjury, lying to brahmins, flesh-eating in regard to the devotees of Śiva, eating forbidden food, killing of innocent living beings in the forest, failure to use for charitable purpose the funds left by good men for the sake of brahmins and committing of arson in the forest or village or in the pathway of cows.

## \|\| 9.5.36-40 \|\|

*dīnasarvasvaharaṇaṃ narastrīgajavājinām \|*  
*gobhūrajatavastrāṇāmauṣadhīnāṃ rasasya ca \|\| 36 \|\|*

*candanāgarukarpūrakastūrīpaṭṭavāsasām \|*  
*vikrayastvavipattau yaḥ kṛto jñānād dvijātibhiḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

*hastanyāsāpaharaṇaṃ rukmasteyasamaṃ smṛtam \|*  
*kanyānāṃ varayogyānāmadānaṃ sadṛśe vare \|\| 38 \|\|*

*putramitrakalatreṣu gamanaṃ bhaginīṣu ca \|*  
*kumārīsāhasaṃ ghoramadyapastrīniṣevaṇam \|\| 39 \|\|*

*savarṇāyāśca gamanaṃ gurubhāryyāsamaṃ smṛtam \|*  
*mahāpāpāni coktāni śṛṇu tvamupapātakam \|\| 40 \|\|*

36-40. The following sins are equal to the theft of gold:—The forfeiture of the entire property of a poor man; the selling of these by a brahmin knowingly done except in a grave emergency—man, woman, elephant, horse, cow, land, silver, cloth, medicinal herbs, juices (or quicksilver) sandal paste, Aguru, camphor, musk, silk garments etc. and misappropriation of deposits kept in trust. The following are sins on a par with that of defiling teacher’s bed—the non-disposal of marriageable daughters by giving them in marriage to deserving hushands, having sexual intercourse with the wives of sons and friends or with the sisters, raping virgins; cohabitation with an intoxicated woman or a woman of one’s own caste. These are the great sins (mahā-pāpa) I have mentioned. Now listen to the minor sins.

## Chapter 6 - Different types of sins (pāpa)

## \|\| 9.6.1 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*dvijadravyāpaharaṇamapi dāyavyatikramaḥ \|*  
*atimāno'tikopaśca dāṃbhikatvaṃ kṛtaghnatā\|\| 1 \|\| \|\| \|*  
*atyaṃtaviṣayāsaktiḥ kārpaṇyaṃ sādhumatsaraḥ \|*  
*paradārābhigamanaṃ sādhukanyāsudūṣaṇam \|\| 2 \|\|*

Note: For the comparison of the Principal and subsidiary sins as mentioned in the previous and present chapters, see Manu Ch. 11. The treatment of the subject in ŚP is more elaborate.

Sanatkumāra said:—

1\. Removal of a brahmin’s money, transgressor of the rules of inheritance, too much of arrogance, great fury, false prestige, ingratitude.

## \|\| 9.6.2 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*dvijadravyāpaharaṇamapi dāyavyatikramaḥ \|*  
*atimāno'tikopaśca dāṃbhikatvaṃ kṛtaghnatā\|\| 1 \|\| \|\| \|*  
*atyaṃtaviṣayāsaktiḥ kārpaṇyaṃ sādhumatsaraḥ \|*  
*paradārābhigamanaṃ sādhukanyāsudūṣaṇam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Debauchery, miserliness, malice towards good men illicit approach to another man’s wife, defiling the virgins of good men.

## \|\| 9.6.3 \|\|

*parivittiḥ parivettā ca yayā ca parividyate \|*  
*tayordānaṃ ca kanyāyāstayoreva ca yājanam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3 Parivitti (the elder brother who is not married but who has allowed the younger brother to marry); Parivettṛ (the younger brother who marries thus); Giving daughters to these two or allowing them to officiate in sacrifices.

## \|\| 9.6.4 \|\|

*śivāśramatarūṇāṃ ca puṣpārāmavināśanam \|*  
*yaḥ pīḍāmāśramasthānāmācaredalpikāmapi \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. The spoilation of flowers and trees around the temple of Śiva; even the slightest injury to the people in the hermitage.

## \|\| 9.6.5 \|\|

*sabhṛtyaparivārasya paśudhānyadhanasya ca \|*  
*kupyadhānyapaśusteyamapāṃ vyāpāvanaṃ tathā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The theft of cattle, grain, wealth etc. of the family of servants, theft of base metals, food-grains and milch cattle, dirtying of waters.

## \|\| 9.6.6 \|\|

*yajñārāmataḍāgānāṃ dārāpatyasya vikrayam \|*  
*tīrthayātropavāsānāṃ vratopanayakarmmiṇām \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Selling of sacrificial parks and ponds, wives and children, pilgrimages and fasts, sacred rites and investiture with sacred thread.

## \|\| 9.6.7 \|\|

*strīdhanānyupajīvaṃti strībhirapyantanirjitāḥ \|*  
*arakṣaṇaṃ ca nārīṇāṃ māyayā strīniṣevaṇam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Dependence on dowry, servitude to women, nonprotection of the womenfolk, carrying on affairs with women through fraudulent means.

## \|\| 9.6.8 \|\|

*kālāgatāpradānaṃ ca dhānyavṛddhyupasevanam \|*  
*niṃditācca dhanādānaṃ paṇyānāṃ kūṭa jīvanam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Non-return of time-barred debts, taking grain as interest, acceptance of monetary gift from a despicable person, deceitful life through merchandise.

## \|\| 9.6.9 \|\|

*viṣamāraṇyapatrāṇāṃ satataṃ vṛṣavāhanam \|*  
*uccāṭanābhicāraṃ ca dhānyādānaṃ bhiṣakkriyā \|\| 9 \|\|*
