*virājamasṛjadviṣṇussa sṛṣṭaḥ puruṣo virāṭ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25 With his greatness he persuaded the universe and created the people. Viṣṇu created the Virāṭ (the cosmic being). Thus the Puruṣa Virāṭ was created.

## \|\| 9.29.26 \|\|

*dvitīyaṃ taṃ manuṃ viddhi manorantarameva ca \|*  
*sa vairājaḥ prajāssarvāssasarja puruṣaḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Know that he was another Manu that began the age. That lord, Puruṣa, created all the subjects out of Vairāja.

## \|\| 9.29.27 \|\|

*nārāyaṇavisargasya prajāstasyāpyayonijaḥ \|*  
*āyuṣmānkīrtimāndhanyaḥ prajāvāṃścābhavattataḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The creations of Viṣṇu were not born of womb. They were long-lived, famous, blessed and possessed of progeny.

## \|\| 9.29.28 \|\|

*ityevamādisargaste varṇito munisattama \|*  
*ādisargaṃ viditvaivaṃ yatheṣṭāṃ prāpnuyādgatim \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O excellent sage, thus the primeval creation has been narrated to you on knowing which one attains his cherished goal.

## Chapter 30 - Description of Creation

## \|\| 9.30.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*saṃsṛṣṭāsu prajāsveva āpavo'tha prajāpratiḥ \|*  
*lebhe vai puruṣaḥ patnīṃ śatarūpāmayo nijām \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. When the subjects were created the patriarch Āpava as Puruṣa acquired as his wife Śatarūpā who was not born of womb.

## \|\| 9.30.2 \|\|

*āpavasya mahimnā tu divamāvṛtya tiṣṭhataḥ \|*  
*dharmeṇaiva mahātmā sa śatarūpāpyajāyata \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. With his greatness Āpava enveloped the heaven and stood virtuously. He the noble soul became Śatarūpā thus.

## \|\| 9.30.3 \|\|

*sā tu varṣaśataṃ taptvā tapaḥ paramaduścaram \|*  
*bhartāraṃ dīptatapasaṃ puruṣaṃ pratyapadyata \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. She performed a penance for a hundred years and attained the Puruṣa of illumined penance as her husband.

## \|\| 9.30.4 \|\|

*sa vai svāyaṃbhuvo jajñe puruṣo manurucyate \|*  
*tasyaikasaptatiyugaṃ manvaṃtaramihocyate \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. The Puruṣa who was born is called Svāyambhuva Manu. His age is said to be seventy-one Yugas.

## \|\| 9.30.5 \|\|

*vairājātpuruṣādvīrā śatarūpā vyajāyata \|*  
*priyavratottānapādau vīrakāyāmajāyatām \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The intelligent Śatarūpā bore the Vairāja Puruṣa two sons Priyavrata and Uttānapāda of heroic prowess.

## \|\| 9.30.6 \|\|

*kāmyā nāma mahābhāgā kardamasya prajāpateḥ \|*  
*kāmyāputrāstrayastvāsansamrāṭsākṣiraviṭprabhuḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The blessed lady Kāmyā, wife of the patriarch Kardama, gave birth to three sons Samrāṭ, Sākṣi, and Aviṭprabhu.

## \|\| 9.30.7 \|\|

*uttānapādo'janayatputrāñchakrasamānprabhuḥ \|*  
*dhruvaṃ ca tanayaṃ divyamātmānaṃdasuvarcasam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Lord Uttānapāda begot sons equal to Indra. He begot another son Dhruva of divine nature who had the excellent lustre and the inner bliss.

## \|\| 9.30.8 \|\|

*dharmasya kanyā suśroṇī sunītirnāma viśrutā \|*  
*utpannā cāpi dharmmeṇa dhuvasya jananī tathā \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Dharma’s daughter, the virtuously born beautiful woman named Sunīti was the mother of Dhruva.

## \|\| 9.30.9 \|\|

*dhruvo varṣasahasrāṇi trīṇi divyāni kānane \|*  
*tapastepe sa bālastu prārthayansthānamavyayam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Even as a child Dhruva performed penance for three thousand divine years wishing for an imperishable status.

## \|\| 9.30.10 \|\|

*tasmai brahmā dadau prītassthānamātmasamaṃ prabhuḥ \|*  
*acalaṃ caiva puratassaptarṣīṇāṃ prajāpatiḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Lord Brahmā, being delighted gave him a stable position as his own, facing the seven sages.

## \|\| 9.30.11-12 \|\|

*tasmātpuṣṭiśca dhānyaśca dhruvātputrau vyajāyatām \|*  
*puṣṭirevaṃ samutthāyāḥ pañcaputrānakalmaṣān \|\| 11 \|\|*

*ripuṃ ripuṃjayaṃ vipraṃ vṛkalaṃ vṛṣatejasam \|*  
*riporevaṃ ca mahiṣī cākṣuṣaṃ sarvatodiśam \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12 Two sons, Puṣṭi and Dhānya were born of Dhruva. Puṣṭi begot of Samutthā five pious sons viz Ripu, Ripuñjaya, Vipra, Vṛkala and Vṛṣatejas. The queen of Ripu gave birth to Cākṣuṣa Manu.

## \|\| 9.30.13-16 \|\|

*ajījanatpuṣkariṇyāṃ varuṇaṃ cākṣuṣo manuḥ \|*  
*manorajāyanta daśa naḍvalāyāṃ mahaujasaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

*kanyāyāṃ hi muniśreṣṭha vaiśyajanma prajāyateḥ \|*  
*pururmāsaḥ śatadyumnastapasvī satyavitkaviḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*agniṣṭomo'tirātraścātimanyussuyaśā daśa \|*  
*pūrorajanayatputrānṣaḍāgneyī mahāprabhān \|\| 15 \|\|*

*aṅgaṃ sumanasaṃ khyātiṃ sṛtimaṃgirasaṃ gayam \|*  
*aṅgātsunīthā bhāryyā vai venamekamasūyata \|\| 16 \|\|*

13-16. Cākṣuṣa Manu begot Varuṇa of Puṣkariṇī. O excellent sage, from Manu of great prowess, in Naḍalā the daughter of the patriarch Vaisyajanma (?) were born ten sons viz Puru, Māsa, Satadyumna, Tapasvin, Satyavit, Kavi, Agniṣṭoma, Atirātra, Atimanyu and Suyaśas. To Puru, Āgneyī bore six sons of great lustre viz Aṅga, Sumanas, Khyāti, Sṛti, Aṅgiras and Gaya. Sunīthā, wife of Aṅga, bore him a son Vena.

## \|\| 9.30.17 \|\|

*apacāreṇa venasya kopasteṣāṃ mahānabhūt \|*  
*huṃkāreṇaiva taṃ jaghnurmunayo dharmatatparāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Due to the misbehaviour of Vena the pious sagos became very furious and killed him by very huṅkāra.

## \|\| 9.30.18 \|\|

*atha prajārthamṛṣayaḥ prārthitāśca sunīthayā \|*  
*sārasvatāstadā tasya mamaṃthurdakṣiṇaṃ karam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Sārasvata Sages were requested by Sunīthā for the birth of a son. They then churned and pressed his right hand.

## \|\| 9.30.19 \|\|

*venasya pāṇau mathite saṃbabhūva tataḥ pṛthuḥ \|*  
*sa dhanvī kavacī jātastejasādityasannibhaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. When the hand of Vena was churned Pṛthu was born. He had a bow and a coat of mail even as he was born and was equal to the sun in brilliance.

## \|\| 9.30.20 \|\|

*avatārasya viṣṇorhi prajāpālanahe tave \|*  
*dharmasaṃrakṣaṇārthāya duṣṭānāṃ daṃḍahetave \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. He was indeed an incarnation of Viṣṇu, for protecting the subjects, for the maintenance of virtue and for punishing the wicked.

## \|\| 9.30.21 \|\|

*pṛthurvainyastadā pṛdhvīmarakṣatkṣatrapūrvajaḥ \|*  
*rājasūyābhiṣiktānāmādyassa vasudhāpatiḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Pṛthu, son of Vena, the ancestor of all Kṣatriyas protected the earth. He was the first lord of Earth, the first among those anointed in the Rājasūya sacrifice.

## \|\| 9.30.22 \|\|

*tasmāccaiva samutpannau nipuṇau sūtamāgadhau \|*  
*teneyaṃ gaurmuniśreṣṭha dugdhā sarvahitāya vai \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The two efficient persons Śūta and Māgadha were born of him. O great Sage, by him the earth in the form of cow was milked for the common welfare.

## \|\| 9.30.23 \|\|

*sarveṣāṃ vṛttidaścābhūddevarṣisura rakṣasām \|*  
*manuṣyāṇāṃ viśeṣeṇa śatayajñakaro nṛpaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Performing a hundred sacrifices he as a king become the provider of livelihood to all the gods, sages and Rākṣasas and to men in particular.

## \|\| 9.30.24 \|\|

*pṛthoḥ putrau tu jajñāte dharmajñau bhuvi pārthivau \|*  
*vijitāśvaśca haryakṣo mahāvīrau suviśrutau \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Two virtuous princes, Vivitāsva and Haryakṣa were born to Pṛthu. They were great heroes who had become very famous.

## \|\| 9.30.25 \|\|

*śikhaṃḍinī cājanayatputraṃ prācīnabarhiṣam \|*  
*prācīnāgrāḥ kuśāstasya pṛthivītalacāriṇaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Śikhaṇḍinī gave birth to a son Prācīnabarhis. While he roamed on the earth Kuśa grasses had their tips towards the East.

## \|\| 9.30.26 \|\|

*samudratanayā tena dharmatassuvivāhitā \|*  
*reje'dhikataraṃ rājā kṛtadāro mahāprabhuḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. The daughter of the lord of the Ocean was duly married by him. The great lord, shone all the more when he had a wife.

## \|\| 9.30.27 \|\|

*samudratanayāyāstu daśa prācīnabarhiṣaḥ \|*  
*babhūvustanayā divyā bahuyajñakarasya vai \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Ten sons were born to Prācīnabarhis the great sacrificer, of his wife Samudratanayā.

## \|\| 9.30.28 \|\|

*sarve prācetasā nāmnā dhanurvedasya pāragāḥ \|*  
*apṛthagdharmācaraṇāste'tapyaṃta mahattapaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. They were Prācetas by name. They had mastered the science of archery. Together they practised virtue and performed penance.

## \|\| 9.30.29 \|\|

*daśavarṣasahasrāṇi samudrasalileśayāḥ \|*  
*rudragītaṃ japaṃtaśca śivadhyānaparāyaṇāḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. They lay sunk in the waters of the ocean for ten thousand years, repeating the Japa of Rudragīta and meditating on Śiva.

## \|\| 9.30.30 \|\|

*tapaścaratsu pṛthivyāmabhavaṃśca mahīruhāḥ \|*  
*arakṣyamāṇāyāṃ pṛthvyāṃ babhūvātha prajākṣayaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. While they were performing penance trees grew on the earth. When the Earth stood unprotected there was a great destruction of subjects.

## \|\| 9.30.31 \|\|

*tāndṛṣṭvā tu nivṛttāste tapaso labdhasadvarāḥ \|*  
*cukrudhurmuniśārdūla dagdhukāmā stapobalāḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. O great king, after attaining boons when they returned they saw the trees. They were furious and wanted to burn them by the power of penance.

## \|\| 9.30.32 \|\|

*prācetasā mukhebhyaste prāsṛjannagnimārutau \|*  
*vṛkṣānunmūlya vāyustānadahaddhavyavāhanaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The Prācetasas created fire and wind from their mouths. The wind uprooted the trees and the fire burnt them.

## \|\| 9.30.33 \|\|

*vṛkṣakṣayaṃ tato dṛṣṭvā kiṃciccheṣeṣu śākhiṣu \|*  
*upagamyābravīdetānrājā somaḥ pratāpavān \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. On seeing the destruction of trees and observing that only a few trees were left, the valorous king Soma approached them and said.

## \|\| 9.30.34 \|\|

*soma uvāca \|*  
*kopaṃ yacchata rājānassarve prācīnabarhiṣaḥ \|*  
*anubhūtānukanyeyaṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ varavarṇinī \|\| 34 \|\|*

Soma said:—

34\. O Prācinabarhiṣas, O kings, subdue your anger. This daughter of the trees, Anubhūtā, is a woman of fair complexion.

## \|\| 9.30.35 \|\|

*bhaviṣyaṃ jānatā sā tu dhṛtā garbheṇa vai mayā \|*  
*bhāryyā vo'stu mahābhāgāssomavaṃśavivarddhinī \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. O fortunate ones, she was conceived by me in my womb as I knew the future. Let her be your wife and make the race of the moon flourish.

## \|\| 9.30.36 \|\|

*asyāmutpatsyate vidvāndakṣo nāma prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*sṛṣṭikartā mahātejā brahmaputraḥ purātanaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The patriarch Dakṣa will be born of her. He, the ancient son of Brahmā, will become a creator of great brilliance.

## \|\| 9.30.37 \|\|

*yuṣmākaṃ tejasārddhena mama cānena tejasā \|*  
*brahmatejomayo bhūpaḥ prajā saṃvarddhayiṣyati \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. With half your brilliance, and with half my brilliance this king full of Brahma’s brilliance will make the subjects flourish.

## \|\| 9.30.38 \|\|

*tatassomasya vacanājjagṛhuste pracetasaḥ \|*  
*bhāryyāṃ dharmeṇa tāṃ prītyā vṛkṣajāṃ varavarṇinīm \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Then at the instance of Soma the Pracetasas lovingly took the fair-complexioned lady, daughter of trees, as their wife.

## \|\| 9.30.39 \|\|

*tebhyastasyāstu saṃjajñe dakṣo nāma prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*so'pi jajñe mahātejāssomasyāṃśena vai mune \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. To her, through them was born the patriarch Dakṣa. O sage, that boy of great brilliance was born of the moon’s parts as well.

## \|\| 9.30.40 \|\|

*acarāṃśca carāṃścaiva dvipado'tha catuṣpadaḥ \|*  
*saṃsṛjya manasā dakṣo maithunīṃ sṛṣṭimārabhat \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. After creating mentally the mobile and immobile beings, the bipeds and the quadrupeds, Dakṣa began creation though copulatory process.

## \|\| 9.30.41 \|\|

*vīraṇasya sutāṃ nāmnā vīraṇīṃ sa prajāpateḥ \|*  
*upayeme suvidhinā sudharmeṇa pativratām \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. In accordance with the Śāstric injunctions, he virtuously married the daughter of Vīraṇa the partriarch, the chaste lady Vīraṇī.

## \|\| 9.30.42 \|\|

*haryyaśvānayutaṃ tasyāṃ sutānpuṇyānajījanat \|*  
*te viraktā babhūvuśca nāradasyopadeśataḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. He begot of her ten thousand sons Haryaśvas. At the instance of Nārada they became detached from the world.

## \|\| 9.30.43 \|\|

*tacchutvā sa punardakṣassubalāśvānajījanat \|*  
*nāmatastanayāṃstasyāṃ sahasraparisaṃkhyayā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. On hearing of it Dakṣa again begot of her a thousand sons Subalāśvas.

## \|\| 9.30.44 \|\|

*te'pi bhrātṛpathā yātāstanmunerupadeśataḥ \|*  
*nāgamanpitṛsānnidhyaṃ viraktā bhikṣumārgiṇaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Thanks to the instructions of the same sage they too followed the footsteps of their brothers. Unattached and following the path of mendicants they never returned to their parents.

## \|\| 9.30.45 \|\|

*tacchrutvā śāpamākruddho munaye dussahaṃ dadau \|*  
*kutracinna labhasveti saṃsthitiṃ kalahapriya \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. On hearing of it he became very furious and cursed the sage thus—O wretch, fond of quarrels, you will never stay permanently anywhere.

## \|\| 9.30.46 \|\|

*sāṃtvito'tha vidhātrā hi sa paścādasṛjatstriyaḥ \|*  
*mahājvālāsvarūpeṇa guṇaiścāpi munīśvaraḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Consoled by Brahmā, O great sage, he afterwards created women endowed with qualities and in the form of great flames.

## \|\| 9.30.47-48 \|\|

*dadau sa daśa dharmāya kaśyapāya trayodaśa \|*  
*dve caivaṃ brahmaputrāya dve caivāṅgirase tadā \|\| 47 \|\|*

*dve kṛśāśvāya viduṣe munaye munisattama \|*  
*śiṣṭāssomāya dakṣo'pi nakṣatrākhyā dadau prabhuḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

47-48. He gave ten daughters to Dharma and thirteen to Kaśyapa, two to Brahmaputra and two to Aṅgiras and two to the learned sage Kṛśāśva. O excellent sage, the remaining twenty-seven daughters, named after the constellations lord Dakṣa gave to Soma.

## \|\| 9.30.49 \|\|

*tābhyo dakṣasya putrībhyo jātā devāsurādayaḥ \|*  
*bahavastanayā khyātāstaissarvaiḥ pūritaṃ jagat \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Asuras and others were born of the daughters of Dakṣa and had become famous. The universe was filled with them.

## \|\| 9.30.50 \|\|

*tataḥ prabhṛti viprendra prajā maithunasaṃbhavāḥ \|*  
*saṃkalpāddarśanātsparśātpūrveṣāṃ sṛṣṭirucyate \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. O great brahmin, the subjects thereafter were born of sexual union. The creation prior to this was through mental concepts, visualisation and touch.

## \|\| 9.30.51 \|\|

*\|\| śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*aṃguṣṭhādbrahmaṇo jajñe dakṣaścoktastvayā purā \|*  
*kathaṃ prācetasatvaṃ hi punarlebhe mahātapāḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

Śaunaka said:—

51\. Formerly it was said by you that Dakṣa was born of the thumb of Brahmā. How then did he of great penance become the son of Prācetasa again?

## \|\| 9.30.52 \|\|

*etaṃ me saṃśayaṃ sūta pratyākhyātuṃ tvamarhasi \|*  
*citrametatsa somasya kathaṃ śvaśuratāṃ gataḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O Sūta, it behoves you to clear this doubt of mine. This is also surprising how he attained the status of of the father-in-law of Soma.

## \|\| 9.30.53 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*utpattiśca nirodhaśca nityaṃ bhūteṣu vartate \|*  
*kalpekalpe bhavaṃtyete sarve dakṣādayo mune \|\| 53 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

53\. Birth and destruction happen everyday among the mortals. O sage, in every Kalpa these, Dakṣa and others are born again.

## \|\| 9.30.54 \|\|

*imāṃ visṛṣṭiṃ dakṣasya yo vidyātsacarācarām \|*  
*prajāvānāyuṣā pūrṇassvargaloke mahīyate \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. He who knows this mode of creation of Dakṣa of the mobile and immobile beings becomes endowed with progeny and longevity. After death he is honoured in the heavenly region.

## Chapter 31 - Description of Creation (2)

## \|\| 9.31.1 \|\|

*śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*devānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca gandharvoragarakṣasām \|*  
*sṛṣṭiṃ tu vistareṇemāṃ sūtaputra vadāśu me \|\| 1 \|\|*

Śaunaka said:—

1\. O son of Sūta, tell me quickly about the details of the creation of the gods, Dānavas, Gandharvas, serpents and Rākṣasas.

## \|\| 9.31.2 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*yadā na vavṛdhe sā tu vīraṇasya prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*sutāṃ sutapasā yuktāmāhvayatsargakāraṇāt \|\| 2 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

2\. When the creation did not multiply, the patriarch asked the daughter of Vīraṇa, endowed with penance, for the purpose of creation

## \|\| 9.31.3 \|\|

*sa maithunena dharmeṇa sasarja vividhāḥ prajāḥ \|*  
*tāḥ śṛṇu tvaṃ mahāprājña kathayāmi samāsataḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. He created different people by the process of copulation. O intelligent one, listen to them. I shall explain in brief.

## \|\| 9.31.4 \|\|

*tasyāṃ putrasahasrāṇi vīriṇyāṃ paṃca vīryavān \|*  
*āśritya janayāmāsa dakṣa eva prajāpatiḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Uniting with her, the powerful patriarch begot of Vīriṇī five thousand sons.

## \|\| 9.31.5-6 \|\|

*etānsṛṣṭāṃstu tāndṛṣṭvā nāradaḥ prāha vai muniḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ sa tu samutpanno nāradaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*śrutavānvā kaśyapādvai puṃsāṃ sṛṣṭirbhaviṣyati \|*  
*dakṣasyeva duhitṛṣu tasmāttānabravīttu saḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. Nārada, son of Brahmā, had heard at the Satra that the creation can take place only through Kaśyapa in the daughters of Dakṣa. So, on seeing the created sons, the sage Nārada addressed them thus.

## \|\| 9.31.7 \|\|

*ajānataḥ kathaṃ sṛṣṭiṃ bāliśā vai kariṣyatha \|*  
*diśaṃ kāṃcidajānaṃtastasmādvijñāya tāṃ bhuvam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. “You do not know any direction in the world. Without knowing that what can you create? You are very childish. Proceed only after knowing the world,”

## \|\| 9.31.8 \|\|

*ityuktāḥ prayayussarve āśāṃ vijñātumojasā \|*  
*tadaṃtaṃ na hi saṃprāpya na nivṛttāḥ piturgṛham \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Thus addressed by him they all proceeded to know the direction by means of their power. Without being able to reach their end they never returned to their father’s house.

## \|\| 9.31.9 \|\|

*tajjñātvā janayāmāsa punaḥ paṃcaśatānsutān \|*  
*tānuvāca punasso'pi nāradassarvadarśanaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. On knowing that he created five hundred sons, Nārada, the visualiser of all, spoke to them also.

## \|\| 9.31.10 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*bhuvo mānamajānaṃtaḥ kathaṃ sṛṣṭiṃ kariṣyatha \|*  
*sarve hi bāliśāḥ kiṃ hi sṛṣṭikartuṃ samudyatāḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

Nārada said:—

10\. How can you create without knowing the extent of the world? All of you are childish. What creation do you propose to make?

## \|\| 9.31.11 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*te'pi tadvacanaṃ śrutvā niryātāssarvatodiśam \|*  
*subalāśvā dakṣasutā haryaśvā iva te purā \|\| 11 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

11\. On hearing his words, Subalāśvas, sons of Dakṣa, as did the Haryaśvas formerly, started in quest of all directions.

## \|\| 9.31.12 \|\|

*anaṃtaṃ puṣkaraṃ prāpya gatāste'pi parābhavam \|*  
*adyāpi na nivartaṃte samudrebhya ivāpagāḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. After reaching Puṣkara and attaining despair due to the endlessness of the world they do not return even now at the rivers from the ocean.

## \|\| 9.31.13 \|\|

*tadāprabhṛti vai bhrātā bhrāturanveṣaṇe rataḥ \|*  
*prayāto naśyati mune tanna kāryyaṃ vipaścitā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Ever since, O sage, if a brother goes in quest of a brother he perishes. That should not be done by any learned man.

## \|\| 9.31.14 \|\|

*tāṃścāpi naṣṭānvijñāya putrāndakṣaḥ prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*sa ca krodhā ddadau śāpaṃ nāradāya mahātmane \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Realising that his son? had perished, the patriarch Dakṣa cursed the noble soul Nārada in anger.

## \|\| 9.31.15 \|\|

*kutracinna labhasveti saṃsthitiṃ kalahapriya \|*  
*tava sānnidhyato loke bhavecca kalahassadā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. “O wretch, fond of quarrels, you will never stay anywhere permanently. Quarrel will always occur among the people where you happen to be present.”

## \|\| 9.31.16 \|\|

*sāṃtvito'tha vidhātrā hi sa dakṣastu prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*kanyāḥ ṣaṣṭyasṛjatpaścādvīriṇyāmiti naḥ śrutam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. We have heard that Dakṣa the patriarch was consoled by Brahmā afterwards and that he begot of Vīriṇī sixty daughters.

## \|\| 9.31.17 \|\|

*dadau sa daśa dharmāya kaśyapāya trayodaśa \|*  
*saptaviṃśati somāya catasro'riṣṭanemine \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. He gave ten of them to Dharma; thirteen to Kaśyapa; twentyseven to the moon and four to Ariṣṭanemi.

## \|\| 9.31.18 \|\|

*dve caivaṃ brahmaputrāya dve caivāṅgirase tadā \|*  
*dve kṛśāśvāya viduṣe tāsāṃ nāmāni me śṛṇu \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Two to the son of Brahmā, two to Aṅgiras and two to the learned Kṛśāśva. Now listen to their names.

## \|\| 9.31.19-20 \|\|

*aruṃdhatī vasuryyāmirlambā bhānurmarutvatī \|*  
*saṃkalpā ca muhūrtā ca saṃdhyā viśvā ca vai mune \|\| 19 \|\|*

*dharmapatnyo mune tvetāstāsvapatyāni me śṛṇu \|*  
*viśvedevāstu viśvāyāssādhyānsādhyā vyajāyata \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. O sage, the wives of Dharma are Arundhatī, Vasu, Yāmi, Lambā, Bhānu, Marutvatī, Saṅkalpā, Muhūrtā, Sādhyā and Viśvā. Now listen to their children. Viśvedevas are the sons of Viśvā, and Sādhyā gave birth to Sādhyas.

## \|\| 9.31.21 \|\|

*marutvatyāṃ marutvaṃto vasostu vasavastathā \|*  
*bhānostu bhānavassarve muhūrtāyāṃ muhūrtajāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Marutvats were born of Marutvatī, Vasus were born of Vasu; the suns were born of Bhānu and the Muhūrtajas were born of Muhūrtā.

## \|\| 9.31.22 \|\|

*lambāyāścaiva ghoṣo'tha nāgavīthī ca yāmijā \|*  
*pṛthivī viṣamastasyāmarundhatyāmajāyata \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Ghoṣa was born of Lambā; Nāgavīthī was born of Yāmi and Pṛthivīviṣama was born of Arundhatī.

## \|\| 9.31.23 \|\|

*saṃkalpāyāstu satyātmā jajñe saṃkalpa eva hi \|*  
*ayādayā vasoḥ putrā aṣṭau tāñchṛṇu śaunaka \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Saṅkalpa of the nature of Truth was born of Saṅkalpā. Aya and others were born of Vasu. They are eight. O Śaunaka, listen to them.

## \|\| 9.31.24 \|\|

*ayo dhuvaśca somaśca dharaścaivānilo'nalaḥ \|*  
*pratyūṣaśca prabhāsaśca vasavā'ṣṭā ca nāmataḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Aya, Dhruva, Soma, Dhara, Anila, Anala, Pratyūṣa and Prabhāsa are the eight Vasus by name.

## \|\| 9.31.25 \|\|

*ayasya putro vaitaṇḍaḥ śramaḥ śāṃto munistathā \|*  
*dhruvasya putro bhagavānkālo lokabhāvanaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Aya’s sons are Vaitaṇḍa, Śrama, Śānta and Muni. Dhruva’s son was lord Kāla influencing the worlds.

## \|\| 9.31.26 \|\|

*somasya bhagavānvarcā varcasvī yena jāyate \|*  
*dharasya putro draviṇo hutahavyavahastathā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Soma’s son was lord Varcas imparting lustre to others. Dhara’s sons were Draviṇa and Hutahavyavaha.

## \|\| 9.31.27-28 \|\|

*manoharāyāśśiśiraḥ prāṇo'tha ramaṇastathā \|*  
*anilasya śivā bhāryyā yasyāḥ putrāḥ purojavaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

*avijñātagatiścaiva dvau putrāvanilasya tu \|*  
*agniputraḥ kumārastu śarastambe śriyāvṛte \|\| 28 \|\|*

27-28. Manoharā’s sons were Śaśira, Prāṇa and Ramaṇa. Anila’s wife was Śivā whose sons were Purojava and Avijñātagati. Anila had only two sons. Kumāra was the son of Agni in the grove of Śara grass surrounded by glory.

## \|\| 9.31.29 \|\|

*tasya śākho viśākhaśca naigameyaśca pṛṣṭhataḥ \|*  
*apatyaṃ kṛttikānāṃ tu kārtikeya iti smṛtaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. His sons were Śākha, Viśākha and Naigameya. The son of Kṛttikās was called Kārttikeya.

## \|\| 9.31.30 \|\|

*pratyūṣasya tvabhūtputra ṛṣirnāmnā tu devalaḥ \|*  
*dvau putrau devalasyāpi prajāvantau manīṣiṇau \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Pratyūṣa’s son was Devala a sage. Devala had two sons who too had children and who were intelligent.

## \|\| 9.31.31-32 \|\|

*bṛhaspate tu bhaginī varastrī brahmacāriṇī \|*  
*yogasiddhā jagatkṛtsnaṃ samaṃtādvyacarattadā \|\| 31 \|\|*

*prabhāsasya tu sā bhāryyā vasūnāmaṣṭamasya ca \|*  
*viśvakarmā mahābhāga tasya jajñe prajāpatiḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. Bṛhaspati’s sister was a woman of excellence, well-versed in Yoga and she traversed the entire world maintaining celibacy. She became the wife oí Prabhāsa the eighth of Vasus. O fortunate one, Viśvakarman the patriarch was born of him.

## \|\| 9.31.33 \|\|

*kartā śilpasahasrāṇāṃ tridaśānāṃ ca vārddhakiḥ \|*  
*bhūṣaṇānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ kartā śilpavatāṃ varaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. He was the creator of thousands of skilled arts. He is the architect of the gods. He was maker of all sorts of ornaments. He was the foremost among artisans and craftsmen.

## \|\| 9.31.34 \|\|

*yassarvāsāṃ vimānāni devatānāṃ cakāra ha \|*  
*manuṣyāścopajīvanti yasya śilpaṃ mahātmanaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. He built the aerial chariots of all gods. He is a great Ātman whose craft is emulated and made use of by men too.

## \|\| 9.31.35-36 \|\|

*matāṃtaramāha \|*  
*raivato'jo bhavo bhīmo vāma ugro vṛṣākapiḥ \|*  
*ajaikapādahirbudhnyo bahurūpo mahāniti \|\| 35 \|\|*

*sarūpāyāṃ prasūtasya striyāṃ rudraśca koṭiśaḥ \|*  
*tatraikādaśamukhyāstu tannāmāni mune śṛṇu \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. According to another version his wife Sarūpā gave birth to crores of Rudras such as Raivata, Aja, Bhava, Bhīma, Vāma, Ugra, Vṛṣākapi, Ajaikapād, Ahirbudhnya, Bahurūpa, Mahat etc. Eleven of them are important. O sage, listen to their names.

## \|\| 9.31.37-38 \|\|

*ajaikapādahirbudhnyastvaṣṭā rudraśca vīryavān \|*  
*haraśca bahurūpaśca tryambakaścāparājitaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

*vṛṣākapiśca śambhuśca kapardī raivatastathā \|*  
*ekādaśaite kathitā rudrāstribhuvaneśvarāḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

37-38. These eleven are the Rudras, lords of the three worlds viz:—Ajaikapād, Ahirbudhnya, Tvaṣtṛ, the powerful Rudrahara of many forms, Tryambaka, Aparājita, Vṛṣākapi, Śambhu, Kapardin and Raivata.

## \|\| 9.31.39 \|\|

*śataṃ tvevaṃ samākhyātaṃ rudrāṇāmamitaujasām \|*  
*śṛṇu kaśyapapatnīnāṃ nāmāni munisattama \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Thus a hundred Rudras of immense prowess have been mentioned to you. Now listen to the names of Kaśyapa’s wives, O excellent sage.

## Chapter 32 - Description of Creation (3): The family of Kaśyapa

## \|\| 9.32.1-2 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*aditirditiśca surasāriṣṭelā danureva ca \|*  
*surabhirvinatā celā tāmrā krodhavaśā tathā \|\| 1 \|\|*

*kadūrmuniśca viprendra tāsvapatyāni me śṛṇu \|*  
*pūrvamanvaṃtare śreṣṭhe dvādaśāsansurottamāḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1-2. O brahmin, they are Aditi, Surasā, Ariṣṭelā, Danu, Surabhi, Vinatā, Ilā, Tāmrā, Krodhavaśā, Kadru and Muni. Now listen to the names of their sons. In the previous excellent Manvantara twelve excellent gods were born.

## \|\| 9.32.3-6 \|\|

*tuṣitā nāma te'nyonyamūcurvaivasvateṃtare \|*  
*upasthite suyaśasaścākṣuṣasyāṃtare manoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*hitāya sarvalokānāṃ samāgamya parasparam \|*  
*āgacchatastu tānūcuraditiṃ ca praviśya vai \|\| 4 \|\|*

*manvaṃtare prasūyāmassatāṃ śreyo bhaviṣyati \|*  
*evamuktāstu te sarve cākṣuṣasyāntare manoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*mārīcātkaśyapājjātāste'dityāṃ dakṣakanyayā \|*  
*tatra viṣṇuśca śakraśca jajñāte punareva hi \|\| 6 \|\|*

3-6. They are Tuṣitas. During the intervening period of the Vaivasvata and Cākṣuṣa Manvantaras they consulted one another for the welfare of the worlds. After meeting together they spoke—“We will enter Aditi and be born. This will happen for the welfare of the gods.” After conferring they were born of Aditi daughter of Dakṣa and Kaśyapa son of Marīci. Viṣṇu and Indra were born again.

## \|\| 9.32.7-9 \|\|

*aryamā caiva dhātā ca tvaṣṭā pūṣā tathaiva ca \|*  
*vivasvānsavitā caiva mitrāvaruṇa eva ca \|\| 7 \|\|*

*aṃśo bhagaścātitejā ādityā dvādaśa smṛtāḥ \|*  
*pūrvamāsanye tuṣitāssurāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*puraiva tasyāṃtare tu ādityā dvādaśa smṛtāḥ \|*  
*iti proktāni kramaśo'dityapatyāni śaunaka \|\| 9 \|\|*

7-9. They along with Aryaman, Dhātṛ, Tvaṣṭṛ, Pūṣan, Vivasvat, Savitṛ, Mitrāvaruṇa, Aṃśa, Bhaga and Atitejas are the twelve Ādityas. Those who were born before as Tuṣitas were born again as the twelve Ādityas in the Cākṣuṣa Manvantara, O Śaunaka thus the names of Aditi’s sons have been mentioned to you.

## \|\| 9.32.10 \|\|

*saptaviṃśati yāḥ proktāssomapatnyo'tha suvratāḥ \|*  
*tāsāmapatyānyabhavandīptayo'mitatejasaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Dīptis of immeasurable splendour became the children of the twenty-seven women of sacred rites and the wives of Soma.

## \|\| 9.32.11-12 \|\|

*ariṣṭanemipatnīnāmapatyānīha ṣoḍaśa \|*  
*bahuputrasya viduṣaścatasro yāssutāḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

*kṛśāśvasya tu devarṣe devapraharaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*bhārmyāyāmarciṣi mune dhūmrakeśastathaiva ca \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. The children of Ariṣṭanemi’s wives were sixteen in number. “O celestial sage, the learned Kṛśāśva had many sons. Four of them are known as Devapraharaṇa. O sage, in his wife Arcis, Dhūmrakeśa too was born.

## \|\| 9.32.13 \|\|

*svadhā satī ca dve patnyau svadhā jyeṣṭhā satī parā \|*  
*svadhāsūta pitṝnvedamatharvāṅgirasaṃ satī \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Two wives were Svadhā and Satī of whom Svadhā was the elder and Satī the younger. Svadhā gave birth to the Pitṛs and Satī to the Atharvāṅgirasaveda.

## \|\| 9.32.14 \|\|

*ete yugasahasrāṃte jāyaṃte punareva hi \|*  
*sarvadevanikāyāśca trayastriṃśattu kāmajāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. At the end of a thousand Yugas these will be born again. All the groups of the gods are thirty-three.

## \|\| 9.32.15 \|\|

*yathā sūryyasya nityaṃ hi udayāstamayāviha \|*  
*evaṃ devānikāste ca saṃbhavaṃti yugeyuge \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Just as the sun rises and sets every day so also the groups of gods are born in every Yuga.

## \|\| 9.32.16 \|\|

*dityāṃ babhūvatuḥ putrau kaśyapāditi naḥ śrutam \|*  
*hiraṇyakaśipuścaiva hiraṇyākṣaśca vīryavān \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. We have heard that Hiraṇyakaśipu and Hiraṇyākṣa were born as the sons of Diti and Kaśyapa.

## \|\| 9.32.17 \|\|

*siṃhikā hyabhavatkanyā vipracitteḥ parigrahaḥ \|*  
*hiraṇyakaśipoḥ putrāścatvāraḥ prathitaujasaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Siṃhikā their daughter became the wife of Vipracitti. The four sons of Hiraṇyakaśipu were famous for their strength and prowess.

## \|\| 9.32.18 \|\|

*anuhrādaśca hrādaśca saṃhrādaścaiva vīryavān \|*  
*prahrādaścānujastatra viṣṇubhaktivicāradhīḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. They are Anuhrāda, Hrāda, Saṃhrāda and Prahlāda. The youngest Prahlāda was a devotee of Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 9.32.19 \|\|

*anuhrādasya sūryāyāṃ pulomā mahiṣastathā \|*  
*hrādasya dhamanirbhāryāsūta vātāpimilvalam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Pūloman and Mahiṣa were the sons of Anuhrāda born of Sūryā. Hrāda’s wife Dhamani gave birth to Vātāpi and Ilvala.

## \|\| 9.32.20 \|\|

*saṃhrādasya kṛtirbhāryāsūtaḥ paṃcajanaṃ tataḥ \|*  
*virocanastu prāhrādirdevyāstasyābhavadbaliḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Saṃhrāda’s wife Kṛti gave birth to Pañcajana. Virocana was the son of Prahlāda. Bali was born of his queen Devī.

## \|\| 9.32.21-22 \|\|

*baleḥ putraśataṃ tvāsīdaśanāyāṃ munīśvara \|*  
*balirāsīnmahāśaivaḥ śivabhaktiparāyaṇaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*dānaśīla udāraśca puṇyakīrti tapāḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*tatputro bāṇanāmā yatso'ṣi śaivavarassudhīḥ \|*  
*yassaṃtoṣya śivaṃ samyaggāṇapatyamavāpa ha \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. O great sage, Bali begot hundred sons of his wife Aśanā. He was a great devotee of Śiva. He engaged himself in the activities of devotion to Śiva. He was charitably inclined and liberal-minded. He attained meritorious fame and austerity. His son Bāṇa was foremost among the devotees of Śiva. By propitiating Śiva he attained the leadership of Gaṇas.

## \|\| 9.32.23 \|\|

*sā kathā śrutapūrvā te bāṇasya hi mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*kṛṣṇaṃ yassamare vīrassuprasannaṃ cakāra ha \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The story of Bāṇa has already been heard by you. In the battle Kṛṣṇa was delighted with him.

## \|\| 9.32.24-25 \|\|

*hiraṇyākṣasutāḥ paṃca paṃḍitāstu mahābalāḥ \|*  
*kukuraḥ śakuniścaiva bhūtasaṃtāpanastathā \|\| 24 \|\|*

*mahānādaśca vikrāṃtaḥ kālanābhastathaiva ca \|*  
*ityuktā ditiputrāśca danoḥ putrānmune śṛṇu \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25 Hiraṇyākṣa’s five sons were strong and learned. They were Kukura, Śakuni, Bhūta Santāpana, Mahānāda and Kālanābha. Thus the sons of Diti have been mentioned. O sage, now listen to the names of the sons of Danu.

## \|\| 9.32.26-28 \|\|

*abhavandanuputrāśca śataṃ tīvraparākramāḥ \|*  
*ayomukhaśśaṃbaraśca kapolo vāmanastathā \|\| 26 \|\|*

*vaiśvānaraḥ pulomā ca vidrāvaṇamahāśirau \|*  
*svarbhānurvṛṣaparvā ca vipracittiśca vīryavān \|\| 27 \|\|*

*ete sarve danoḥ putrāḥ kaśyapādanujajñire \|*  
*eṣāṃ putrāñcchṛṇu mune prasaṃgādvacmi te'nagha \|\| 28 \|\|*

26-28. Danu’s sons were hundred in number and were of severe exploits. Ayomukha, Śambara, Kapola, Vāmana, Vaiśvānara, Puloman, Vidrāvaṇa, Mahāśira, Svarbhānu, Vṛṣaparvan and the powerful Vipracitti—these were the sons of Danu begot by Kaśyapa. O sage, now listen to the names of their sons. O sinless one, incidentally I shall mention a few of them.

## \|\| 9.32.29-30 \|\|

*svabhārnostu prabhā kanyā pulomnastu śacī sutā \|*  
*upadānavī hayaśirā śarmmiṣṭhā vārṣaparvaṇī \|\| 29 \|\|*

*pulomā pulomikā caiva vaiśvānarasute ubhe \|*  
*bahvapatye mahāvīryye mārīcestu parigrahaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. Prabhā was the daughter of Svarbhānu. Śacī was the daughter of Puloman. So also Upadānavī, Hayaśirā and Śarmiṣṭhā were daughters of Vṛṣaparvan. Pulomā and Pulomikā were the daughters of Vaiśvānara. They were the wives of Kaśyapa son of Marīci and had many heroic sons.

## \|\| 9.32.31 \|\|

*tayoḥ putrasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭirdānavanandanāḥ \|*  
*marīcirjanayāmāsa mahatā tapasānvitaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Kaśyapa, son of Marīci, endowed with great penance, begot of them sixty thousand sons delighting the Dānavas.

## \|\| 9.32.32 \|\|

*paulomāḥ kālakhaṃjāśca dānavānāṃ mahābalā \|*  
*avadhyā devatānāṃ ca hiraṇyapuravāsinaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Kālakhañjas, the powerful sons of Pulomā, lived in Hiraṇyapura. They could not be killed either by the Dānavas or by the gods.

## \|\| 9.32.33 \|\|

*pitāmahaprasādena ye hatāḥ savyasācinā \|*  
*siṃhikāyāmathotpannā vipracittessutāstathā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Vipracitti’s sons, born of Siṃhikā, were later killed by Arjuna by the grace of Brahmā.

## \|\| 9.32.34 \|\|

*daityadānavasaṃyogājjātāstīvraparākramāḥ \|*  
*saiṃhikeyā iti khyātāstrayodaśa mahābalāḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Due to the intermixture of Daityas and Dānavas these sons became valorous and famous as Saiṃhikeyas. They are thirteen in number.

## \|\| 9.32.35-36 \|\|

*rāhuḥ śalyo subalino balaścaiva mahābalaḥ \|*  
*vātāpirnamuciścaivāthelvalaḥ svasṛpastathā \|\| 35 \|\|*

*ajiko narakaścaiva kālanābhastathaiva ca \|*  
*śaramāṇaśśarakalpaśca ete vaṃśavivarddhanāḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. They were the very powerful Rāhu and Śalya, Bala, Mahābala, Vātāpi, Namuci, Ilvala, Svasṛpa, Ajika, Naraka, Kālanābha, Śaramāṇa and Śarakalpa. These increased their races.

## \|\| 9.32.37 \|\|

*eṣāṃ putrāśca pautrāśca danuvaṃśavivarddhanāḥ \|*  
*bahavaśca samudbhūtā vistaratvānna varṇitāḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Many sons and grandsons were born of them increasing the race of Danu. Being innumerable they are not mentioned in detail.

## \|\| 9.32.38 \|\|

*saṃhrādasya tu daiteyā nivātakavacāḥ kule \|*  
*utpannā marutastasmiṃstapasā bhāvitātmanaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. In the family of Saṃhrāda the Daiteyas Nivātakavacas and Maruts were born and they were purified by penance.

## \|\| 9.32.39-40 \|\|

*ṣaṇmukhādyā mahāsattvāstāmrāyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ \|*  
*kākī śyenī ca bhāsī ca sugrīvī ca śukī tathā \|\| 39 \|\|*

*gṛddhrikāśvī hyulūkī ca tāmrā kanyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ \|*  
*kākī kākānajanayadulūkī pratyulūkakān \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. Ṣaṇmukha and others are glorified as the sons of Tāmrā. Kākī, Śyenī, Bhāsī, Sugrīvī, Śukī, Gṛdhṛkā, Aśvī, and Ulūkī were the daughters of Tāmrā. Kākī gave birth to crows and Ulūkī to owls.

## \|\| 9.32.41 \|\|

*śyenī śyenāṃstathā bhāsī bhāsā ngṛddhī tu gṛdhrakān \|*  
*śukī śukānajanayatsugrīvī śubhapakṣiṇaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Śyenī gave birth to vultures, Bhāsī to Bhāsas Gṛdhrī to Gṛdhrakas. Śukī gave birth to parrots and Sugrīvī to auspicious birds.

## \|\| 9.32.42 \|\|

*aśvānuṣṭrāngardabhāṃśca tāmrā ca kaśyapapriyā \|*  
*janayāmāsa cetyevaṃ tāmrāvaṃśāḥ prakīrtitāḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Tāmrā the beloved of Kaśyapa gave birth to horses, camels and donkeys. Thus the family of Tāmrā has been described to you.

## \|\| 9.32.43 \|\|

*vinatāyāśca putrau dvāvaruṇo garuḍastathā \|*  
*suparṇaḥ patatāṃ śreṣṭho nāruṇassvena karmaṇā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Vinatā had two sons Aruṇa and Garuḍa. The latter was the most excellent among birds. He was terrible by virtue of his activities.

## \|\| 9.32.44-47 \|\|

*surasāyāssahasraṃ tu sarpāṇāmamitaujasām \|*  
*anekaśirasāṃ teṣāṃ khecarāṇāṃ mahātmanām \|\| 44 \|\|*

*yeṣāṃ pradhānā rājānaḥ śeṣavāsukitakṣakāḥ \|*  
*airāvato mahāpadmaḥ kaṃbalāśvatarāvubhau \|\| 45 \|\|*

*ailāputrastathā padmaḥ karkoṭakadhanaṃjayau \|*  
*mahānīlamahākarṇau dhṛtarāṣṭro balāhakaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

*kuharaḥ puṣpadantaśca durmukhāssumukhastathā \|*  
*bahuśaḥ khararomā ca pāṇirityevamādayaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

44-47. Surasā gave birth to a thousand serpents of immeasurable power. They had many hoods. They were powerful enough to traverse the sky, the important among them were the kings Śeṣa, Vāsuki and Takṣaka, Airāvata, Mahāpadma, Kambala and Aśvatara. The sons of Ailā were Padma, Karkoṭaka, Dhanañjaya, Mahānīla, Mahākarṇa, Dhṛtarāṣṭra, Balāhaka, Kuhara, Puṣpadanta, Durmukha, Sumukha, Khararoman, Kharapāṇi and many others.

## \|\| 9.32.48 \|\|

*gaṇāḥ krodhavaśāyāśca tasyāssarve ca daṃṣṭriṇaḥ \|*  
*aṃḍajāḥ pakṣiṇo'bjāśca varāhyāḥ paśavo matāḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Krodhavaśā gave birth to many who. were the fanged animals and birds both of land and water. Varā’s sons were animals.

## \|\| 9.32.49 \|\|

*anāyuṣāyāḥ putrāśca paṃcāśacca mahābalāḥ \|*  
*abhavanbalavṛkṣau ca vikṣaro'tha bṛhaṃstathā \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Anāyuṣā’s sons were fifty. They were very strong. The chief amongst them were Bala, Vṛtta, Vikṣara and Bṛhan.

## \|\| 9.32.50-51 \|\|

*śaśāṃstu janayāmāsa surarabhirmahiṣāṃstathā \|*  
*ilā vṛkṣāṃllatā vallīstṛṇajātīstu sarvaśaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

*khaśā tu yakṣarakṣāṃsi munirapsarasastathā \|*  
*ariṣṭāsūta sarpāṃśca prabhāvairmānavottamān \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. Surabhi gave birth to rabbits and buffaloes; Ilā to trees, creepers, spreading vines and all kinds of grass; Khaśā gave birth to Yakṣa and Rākṣasas, Muni gave birth to the celestial damsels. Ariṣṭā gave birth to serpents more excellent than human beings in their power.

## \|\| 9.32.52 \|\|

*ete kaśyapadāyādāḥ kīrtitāste munīśvara \|*  
*yeṣāṃ putrāśca pautrāśca śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O great sages, thus the descendants of Kaśyapa have been explained to you. They had hundreds of sons and grandsons.

## Chapter 33 - Description of Creation (4)

## \|\| 9.33.1-5 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*eṣa manvantare tāta sargassvārociṣe smṛtaḥ \|*  
*vaivasvate tu mahati vāruṇe vitate kratau \|\| 1 \|\|*

*juhvānasya brahmaṇo vai prajāsarga ihocyate \|*  
*pūrvaṃ yānatha brahmarṣīnutpannānsapta mānasān \|\| 2 \|\|*

*putrānvai kalpayāmāsa svayameva pitāmahaḥ \|*  
*teṣāṃ virodho devānāṃ dānavānāṃ mahānṛṣe \|\| 3 \|\|*

*diti vinaṣṭaputrā tu kaśyapaṃ samupasthitā \|*  
*sa kaśyapaḥ prasannātmā samgayagārādhitastayā \|\| 4 \|\|*

*vareṇacchaṃdayāmāsa sā ca vavre varaṃ tadā \|*  
*putramindravadhārthāya samarthamamitaujasam \|\| 5 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1-5. Such is the creation as mentioned in the Manvantara, Svārociṣa, O dear. Now I shall describe the creation as evolved by Brahman while offering oblations in the elaborate Vāruṇa sacrifice in the great Vaivasvata Manvantara. All the Brahmarṣis born in the beginning, Pitāmaha made his mental sons. When a great fight ensued between the gods and the Dānavas, Diti lost all her sons. She approached Kaśyapa and propitiated him. The delighted Kaśyapa asked her to choose a boon as she pleased. She chose the boon that she be favoured with a son of immeasurable power capable of slaying Indra.

## \|\| 9.33.6 \|\|

*sa tasyai ca varaṃ prādātprārthitaṃ sumahātapāḥ \|*  
*brahmacaryyādiniyamaṃ prāha caiva śataṃ samāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. He granted her the boon of her choice. The sage of great penance advised her celibacy and other restraints for a hundred years.

## \|\| 9.33.7 \|\|

*dhārayāmāsa garbhaṃ tu śucissā varavarṇinī \|*  
*brahmacaryyādiniyamaṃ ditirdadhre tathaiva vai \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The pious fair-complexioned lady conceived. She began to observe celibacy and other restraints.

## \|\| 9.33.8 \|\|

*tatastvādhāya so'dityāṃ garbhaṃ taṃ śaṃsitavrataḥ \|*  
*jagāma kaśyapastaptuṃ tapassaṃhṛṣṭamānasaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. After impregnating Diti, Kaśyapa of noble sacred rites went away satisfied for the performance of penance.

## \|\| 9.33.9 \|\|

*tasyāścaivāṃtaraṃ prepsusso'bhavatpākaśāsanaḥ \|*  
*ūnavarṣe śate cāsyā dadarśāntarameva saḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Indra waited for finding out a weak point in her. He found one such when the period of hundred years was short by a year.

## \|\| 9.33.10 \|\|

*akṛtvā pādayoḥ śaucaṃ ditirarvākśirāstadā \|*  
*nidrāmāhārayāmāsa bhāvino'rthasya gauravāt \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Due to the gravity of the event destined to happen, once Diti did not wash her feet. She went to sleep with head hanging down.

## \|\| 9.33.11 \|\|

*etasminnantare śakrastasyāḥ kukṣiṃ praviśya saḥ \|*  
*vajrapāṇistu taṃ garbhaṃ saptadhā hi nyakṛntata \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. In the meantime Indra entered her belly with the thunderbolt. He cut off the foetus into seven pieces.

## \|\| 9.33.12 \|\|

*sa pāṭyamāno garbho'tha vajreṇa praruroda ha \|*  
*rudantaṃ saptadhaikaikaṃ mārodīriti tānpunaḥ \|*  
*cakarta vajrapāṇistānneva mamrustathāpi te \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. While being split into seven the foetus began to cry in seven different tones. Indra told the foetus “Do not cry, Do not cry” repeatedly. He cut them again but they did not die.

## \|\| 9.33.13 \|\|

*te tamūcuḥ pātyamānāssarve prāṃjalayo mune \|*  
*no jighāṃsasi kiṃ śakra bhrātaro marutastava \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. O sage, being felled down they told him with palms joined in reverence. “O Indra, why do you wish to kill us? We are your brothers, the Maruts.”

## \|\| 9.33.14 \|\|

*iṃdreṇa svīkṛtāste hi bhrātṛtve sarva eva ca \|*  
*tatyajurddaityabhāvaṃ te viprarṣe śaṃkarecchayā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O brahmin sage, at the will of Śiva, they eschewed their Daitya nature. They were accepted by Indra with due fraternity.

## \|\| 9.33.15 \|\|

*maruto nāma te devā babhūvustu mahābalāḥ \|*  
*khagā ekonapaṃcāśatsahāyā vajrapāṇinaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. In the name of Maruts they became gods of great prowess. Thus traversing the sky the forty-nine Maruts became assistants of Indra.

## \|\| 9.33.16 \|\|

*teṣāmeva pravṛddhānāṃ hariḥ prādātprajāpatiḥ \|*  
*kramaśastāni rājyāni pṛthupūrvaṃ śṛṇuṣva tat \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. When they flourished, Viṣṇu the patriarch, offered them kingdoms beginning with Pṛthu. Listen to that.

## \|\| 9.33.17 \|\|

*ariṣṭapuruṣo vīraḥ kṛṣṇo jiṣṇuḥ prajāpatiḥ \|*  
*parjanyastu dhanādhyakṣastasya sarvamidaṃ jagat \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Ariṣṭa was the heroic person. Kṛṣṇa the patriarch was ever victorious. Parjanya was the presiding deity of clouds. This entire universe belongs to him.

## \|\| 9.33.18 \|\|

*bhūtasargamimaṃ samyagavocaṃ te mahāmune \|*  
*vibhāgaṃ śṛṇu rājyānāṃ kramaśastaṃ bruve'dhunā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O great sage, I have explained the creation of living beings to you. Listen to the division of kingdoms. I shall explain that duly now.

## \|\| 9.33.19 \|\|

*abhiṣicyādhirājye tu pṛthuṃ vainyaṃ pitāmahaḥ \|*  
*tataḥ krameṇa rājyāni vyādeṣṭumupacakrame \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. After crowning Pṛthu the son of Vena in the emperorship, Brahmā began to assign the kingdoms in due order.

## \|\| 9.33.20 \|\|

*dvijānāṃ vīrudhāṃ caiva nakṣatragrahayostathā \|*  
*yajñānāṃ tapasāṃ caiva somaṃ rājye'bhyaṣecayat \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. He crowned Soma in the kingdom of birds, creepers, stars, planets, sacrifices and austerities.

## \|\| 9.33.21 \|\|

*apāṃ tu varuṇaṃ rājye rājñāṃ vaiśravaṇaṃ prabhum \|*  
*ādityānāṃ tathā viṣṇuṃ vasūnāmatha pāvakam \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. He crowned Varuṇa in the kingdom of waters; Vaiśravaṇa as the lord of kings; Viṣṇu as the lord of Āditvas and Pāvaka as the lord of Vasus.

## \|\| 9.33.22 \|\|

*prajāpatīnāṃ dakṣaṃ tu marutāmatha vāsavam \|*  
*daityānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca prahlādamamitaujasam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He crowned Dakṣa as the lord of patriarchs, Indra as the lord of Maruts, and Prahlāda of great strength as the lord of Daityas and Dānavas.

## \|\| 9.33.23 \|\|

*vaivasvataṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca yamaṃ rājye'bhyaṣecayat \|*  
*mātṝṇāṃ ca vratānāṃ ca mantrāṇāṃ ca tathā gavām \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. He crowned Vaivasvata Yama in the kingdom of Pitṛs, Mātṛs, Vratas, Mantras and cows.

## \|\| 9.33.24 \|\|

*yakṣāṇāṃ rākṣasānāṃ ca pārthivānāṃ tathaiva ca \|*  
*sarvabhūtapiśācānāṃ giriśaṃ śūlapāṇinam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. He crowned the trident-bearing Śiva, in the kingdom of Yakṣas, Rākṣasas, kings, goblins and ghosts.

## \|\| 9.33.25 \|\|

*śailānāṃ himavantaṃ ca nadīnāmatha sāgaram \|*  
*mṛgāṇāmatha śārdūlaṃ govṛṣaṃ tu gavāmapi \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. He crowned Himavat as the lord of mountains, the ocean of rivers, the tiger of deer and Bull as the lord of cows.

## \|\| 9.33.26 \|\|

*vanaspatīnāṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ vaṭaṃ rājye'bhyaṣecayat \|*  
*iti dattaṃ prajeśena rājyaṃ sarvatra vai kramāt \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. He crowned the banyan tree in the kingdom of plants and trees. Thus the kingdom was assigned everywhere by the lord of subjects.

## \|\| 9.33.27 \|\|

*pūrvasyāṃ diśi putraṃ tu vairājasya prajāpateḥ \|*  
*sthāpayāmāsa sarvātmā rājye viśvapatirvibhuḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The lord of the universe, the soul of all, established the son of patriarch Vairāja in the kingdom in the east.

## \|\| 9.33.28 \|\|

*tathaiva muniśārdūla kardamasya prajāpateḥ \|*  
*dakṣiṇasyāṃ tathā putraṃ sudhanvānamacīklṛpat \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Similarly, O great king, he established Sudhanvan, son of Kardama, the patriarch, in the kingdom in the south.

## \|\| 9.33.29 \|\|

*paścimāyāṃ diśi tathā rajasaḥ putramacyutam \|*  
*ketumantaṃ mahātmānaṃ rājānaṃ vyādiśatprabhuḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The lord directed and assigned the unfailing noble-minded son of Rajas, Ketumat as the king in the kingdom in the west.

## \|\| 9.33.30 \|\|

*tathā hiraṇyaromāṇaṃ parjanyasya prajāpateḥ \|*  
*udīcyāṃ diśi rājānaṃ durdharṣaṃ so'bhyaṣecayat \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. He crowned the indefatigable son of Parjanya the patriarch, Hiraṇyaroman, in the kingdom in the north.

## \|\| 9.33.31 \|\|

*tasya vistāramākhyātaṃ pṛthorvenyasya śaunaka \|*  
*mahardhye tadadhiṣṭhānaṃ purāṇe parikīrtitam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. O Śaunaka, the details of Pṛthu the son of Vena have been narrated to you. This base is glorified as the most ancient spot of great prosperity.

## Chapter 34 - The enumeration of Manvantaras

## \|\| 9.34.1 \|\|

*\|\| śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*manvaṃtarāṇi sarvāṇi vistareṇānukīrtaya \|*  
*yāvaṃto manavaścaiva śrotumicchāmi tānaham \|\| 1 \|\|*

Śaunaka said:—

1\. Please describe in detail all the Manvantaras. I wish to hear of all the Manus too.

## \|\| 9.34.2-3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*svāyaṃbhuvo manuścaiva tatasstvārociṣastathā \|*  
*uttamastāmasaścaiva raivataścākṣuṣastathā \|\| 2 \|\|*

*ete ca manavaḥ ṣaṭ te saṃproktā munipuṃgava \|*  
*vaivasvato muniśreṣṭha sāṃprataṃ manurucyate \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

2-3. O great sage, six Manus have already been mentioned to you viz. Svāyambhuva, Svārociṣa, Uttama, Tāmasa, Raivata and Cākṣuṣa. The present Manu is called Vaivasvata, O great sage.

## \|\| 9.34.4-6 \|\|

*sāvarṇiśca manuścaiva tato raucyastathā paraḥ \|*  
*tathaiva brahmasāvarṇiścatvāro manavastathā \|\| 4 \|\|*

*tathaiva dharmasāvarṇī rudrasāvarṇireva ca \|*  
*devasāvarṇirākhyātaṃ iṃdrasāvarṇireva ca \|\| 5 \|\|*

*atītā vartamānāśca tathaivānāgatāśca ye \|*  
*kīrtitā manavaścāpi mayaivaite yathā śrutāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

4-6. The Manus of future are Sāvarṇi, Raucya, Brahmasāvarṇi, Dharmasāvarṇi, Rudrasāvarṇi, Devasāvarṇi and Indrasāvarṇi. All these constitute the Manus of the past, present and future. These have been enumerated by me as I have heard.

## \|\| 9.34.7 \|\|

*mune caturdaśaitāni trikālānugatāni te \|*  
*proktāni nirmitaḥ kalpo yugasāhasraparyyayaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O sage, these fourteen Manvantaras extending over the period past, present and the future have been mentioned to you. A Kalpa consisting of a thousand Yugas is constituted by these.

## \|\| 9.34.8 \|\|

*ṛṣīṃsteṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi puttrāndevagaṇāṃstathā \|*  
*śṛṇu śaunaka suprītyā kramaśastānyaśasvinaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. O Śaunaka, listen. I shall explain the sages, their sons and the gods; all are famous. Listen with pleasure.

## \|\| 9.34.9 \|\|

*marīciratrirbhagavānaṅgirāḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ \|*  
*pulastyaśca vasiṣṭhaśca saptaite brahmaṇassutāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Marīci, Atri, the holy lord Aṅgiras, Pulaha, Kratu, Pulastya and Vasiṣṭha—these seven are Brahmā’s sons.

## \|\| 9.34.10 \|\|

*uttarasyāṃ diśi tathā mune saptarṣa yastathā \|*  
*yāmā nāma tathā devā āsansvāyaṃbhuveṃtare \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O sage, the seven sages are in the northern quarter. In the Svāyambhuva Manvantara there were the gods named Yāmas.

## \|\| 9.34.11-12 \|\|

*āgnīdhraścāgnibāhuśca medhā medhātithirvasuḥ \|*  
*jyotiṣmāndhṛtimānhavyaḥ savanaśśubhra eva ca \|\| 11 \|\|*

*svāyaṃbhuvasya putrāste manordaśa mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*kīrtitā muniśārdūla tatrendro yajña ucyate \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. The ten noble-souled sons of Svāyambhuva Manu were—Āgnīdhra, Agnibāhu, Medhā, Medhātithi, Vasu, Jyotiṣmat, Dhṛtimat, Havya, Savana and Śubhra. O great sage, the Indra there was Yajña.

## \|\| 9.34.13 \|\|

*prathamaṃ kathitaṃ tāta divyaṃ manvataraṃ tathā \|*  
*dvitīyaṃ te pravakṣyāmi tannibodha yathātatham \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Thus O dear, the first divine Manvantara has been mentioned to you. I shall explain the second one. Know as it is.

## \|\| 9.34.14-15 \|\|

*ūrjastaṃbhaḥ parastaṃbha ṛṣabho vasumāṃ stathā \|*  
*jyotiṣmāndyutimāṃścaiva rociṣmānsaptamastathā \|\| 14 \|\|*

*ete maharṣayo jñeyāstatrendro rocanastathā \|*  
*devāśca tuṣitā nāma smṛtāḥ svārociṣeṃ'tare \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. In the second Svārociṣa Manvantara these must be known as the great sages, viz.—Ūrjastambha, Parastambha, Ṛṣabha, Vasumat, Jyotiṣmat, Dyutimat and the seventh Rociṣmat. Then the Indra was Rocana. The gods were known as Tuṣitas.

## \|\| 9.34.16-17 \|\|

*harighnassukṛtirjyotirayomūrtirayasmayaḥ \|*  
*prathitaśca manasyuśca nabhassūryastathaiva ca \|\| 16 \|\|*

*svārociṣasya putrāste manordaśamahātmanaḥ \|*  
*kīrtitā muniśārdūla mahāvīryaparākramāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. O great sage, the ten noble-minded sons of Svārociṣa Manu, all of great heroism and exploits were Harighna, Sukṣti, Jyotis, Ayomūrti, Ayasmaya, Prathita, Manasyu, Nabha and Sūrya.

## \|\| 9.34.18 \|\|

*dvitīyametatkathitaṃ mune manvantaraṃ mayā \|*  
*tṛtīyaṃ tava vakṣyāmi tannibodha yathātatham \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O sage, the second Manvantara has been mentioned by me. I shall explain the third one. Know it factually.

## \|\| 9.34.19-20 \|\|

*vasiṣṭhaputrāḥ saptāsanvāsiṣṭhā iti viśrutāḥ \|*  
*hiraṇyagarbhasya sutā ūrjā nāma mahaujasaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*ṛṣayo'tra samākhyātāḥ kīrtyamānānnibodha me \|*  
*auttameyā ṛṣiśreṣṭha daśaputrā manoḥ smṛtāḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. Vasiṣṭha’s seven sons famous as Vāsiṣṭhas and Hiraṇyagarbha’s sons of great splendour known as Ūrjas are mentioned as the sages. Uttama the Manu had ten sons, O great sage.

## \|\| 9.34.21-22 \|\|

*iṣa ūrjita ūrjaśca madhurmādhava eva ca \|*  
*śuciśśukravahaścaiva nabhaso nabha eva ca \|\| 21 \|\|*

*ṛṣabhastatra devāśca satyaveda śrutādayaḥ \|*  
*tatrendrassatyajinnāma trailokyādhipatirmune \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. They were Iṣa, Ūrjita, Ūrja, Madhu, Mādhava Śuci, Śukravaha, Nabhasa, Nabha and Ṛṣabha. The gods were Satyavedaśruta and others. O sage, the Indra then was Satyajit. He was the ruler of the three worlds.

## \|\| 9.34.23 \|\|

*tṛtīyametatparamaṃ manvataramudāhṛtam \|*  
*manvataraṃ caturthaṃ te kathayāmi mune śṛṇu \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The great third Manvantara has been explained. O sage, listen. I shall mention the fourth Manvantara.

## \|\| 9.34.24 \|\|

*gārgyaḥ pṛthustathā vāgmī jayo dhātā kapīnakaḥ \|*  
*kapīvānsaptaṛṣayaḥ satyā devagaṇāstathā \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. The seven sages were Gārgya, Pṛthu, Vāgmin, Janya, Dhātā, Kapīnaka and the seventh Kapīvat. The groups of gods were Satyas.

## \|\| 9.34.25-26 \|\|

*tatreṃdrastriśikho jñeyo manuputrānmune śṛṇu \|*  
*dyūtipotassautapasyastamaśśūlaśca tāpanaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

*taporatirakalmāṣo dhanvī khaḍgī mahānṛṣiḥ \|*  
*tāmasasya smṛtā ete daśa putrā mahāvratāḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

25-26. The Indra there was Triśikha. O sage, listen to the names of Manu’s sons. The ten sons of great rites of the Manu Tāmasa were Dyutipota, Sautapasya, Tapaḥśūla, Tāpana, Taporati, Akalmāsa, Dhanvin, Khaḍgin, Mahat and Ṛṣi.

## \|\| 9.34.27 \|\|

*tāmasasyāṃtaraṃ caiva mano me kathitaṃ tava \|*  
*caturthaṃ pañcamaṃ tāta śṛṇu manvaṃtaraṃ param \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The fourth Manvantara of Tāmasa Manu has been mentioned to you. O dear, now listen to the great fifth Manvantara.

## \|\| 9.34.28-29 \|\|

*devabāhurjayaścaiva munirvedaśirāstathā \|*  
*hiraṇyaromā parjanya ūrdhvabāhuśca somapāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

*satyanetraratāścānye ete saptarṣayo'pare \|*  
*devāśca bhūtarajasastapaḥprakṛtayastathā \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. Devabāhu, Jaya, the sage Vedaśiras, Hiraṇyaroman, Parjanya, Ūrdhva Bāhu, Somapa—These and others constituted the seven sages. They were engaged in truth. The gods were Bhūtarajasas of the nature of performing penance.

## \|\| 9.34.30 \|\|

*tatreṃdro vibhunāmā ca trailokyādhipatistathā \|*  
*raivatākhyo manustatra jñeyastāmasasodaraḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Indra was named Vibhu and he was the lord of the three worlds. The name of the Manu was Raivata. He shall be known as the brother of Tāmasa.

## \|\| 9.34.31 \|\|

*arjunaḥ paṃktiviṃdhyo vā dayāyāsta nayā mune \|*  
*mahatā tapasā yuktā merupṛṣṭhe vasaṃti hi \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. O sage, Arjuna, Paṅktivindhya and others shall be known as his sons. They are endowed with great penance. They stay on the mountain Meru.

## \|\| 9.34.32 \|\|

*ruceḥ prajāpatiḥ putro raucyo nāma manuḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*bhūtyā cotpādito devyāṃ bhautyo nāmābhavatsutaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Raucya son of Ruci the patriarch was the Manu. His son born of the queen Bhūti was Bhautya.

## \|\| 9.34.33 \|\|

*anāgatāśca saptaite kalpe'sminmanavassmṛtāḥ \|*  
*anāgatāśca saptaiva smṛtā divi maharṣayaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The future Manus in this Kalpa will be seven. The future great sages will also be seven in each Manvantara in the heaven.

## \|\| 9.34.34 \|\|

*rāmo vyāsastathātreyo dīptimānsu bahuśrutaḥ \|*  
*bharadvājastathā drauṇiraśvatthāmā mahādyutiḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Rāma, Vyāsa, Ātreya, Dīptimat, Subahuśruta, Bharadvāja, and the lustrous Aśvatthāman son of Droṇa will be seven sages in this Manvantara.

## \|\| 9.34.35-37 \|\|

*gautamasyātmajaścaiva śaradvān gautamaḥ kṛpaḥ \|*  
*kauśiko gālavaścaiva ruruḥ kaśyapa eva ca \|\| 35 \|\|*

*ete sapta mahātmāno bhaviṣyā munisattamāḥ \|*  
*devāścānāgatāstatra trayaḥ proktāssvayaṃbhuvā \|\| 36 \|\|*

*marīceścaiva putrāste kaśyapasya mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*teṣāṃ virocanasuto baliriṃdro bhaviṣyati \|\| 37 \|\|*

35-37. Śaradvat son of Gautama, Gautama, Kṛpa, Kauśika, Gālava, Rum and Kaśyapa will be future seven sages. The future gods are three in number as mentioned by Svayambhu. They are the sons of the noble-souled Kaśyapa the son of Marīci. Bali the son of Virocana will become their Indra.

## \|\| 9.34.38-39 \|\|

*viṣāṃṅgaścāvanīvāṃśca sumaṃto dhṛtimānvasuḥ \|*  
*sūriḥ surākhyo viṣṇuśca rājā sumatireva ca \|\| 38 \|\|*

*sāvarṇeśca manoḥ putrā bhaviṣyā daśa śaunaka \|*  
*ihāṣṭamaṃ hi kathitaṃ navamaṃ cāntaraṃ śṛṇu \|\| 39 \|\|*

38-39. O Śaunaka, the future ten sons of Sāvarṇi the Manu will be Viṣāṅga, Avanīvat, Sumanta, Dhṛtimat, Vasu, Sūri, Sura, Viṣṇu, Rājā and Sumati. Thus the eighth Manvantara has been mentioned. Now listen to the ninth Manvantara.

## \|\| 9.34.40-42 \|\|

*prathamaṃ dakṣasāvarṇi pravakṣyāmi manuṃ śṛṇu \|*  
*medhātithiśca paulastyo vasuḥ kaśyapa eva ca \|\| 40 \|\|*

*jyotiṣmānbhārgavaścaiva dhṛtimānaṃgirāstathā \|*  
*savanaścaiva vāsiṣṭha ātreyo havya eva ca \|\| 41 \|\|*

*pulahassapta ityete ṛṣayo rauhiteṃtare \|*  
*devatānāṃ gaṇāstatra traya eva mahāmune \|\| 42 \|\|*

40-42. I shall mention Dakṣasāvarṇi Manu at the outset. Listen. Medhātithi, Paulastya, Vasu, Kaśyapa, Jyotiṣmat, Bhārgava, Dhṛtimat, Aṅgiras, Savana son of Vasiṣṭha, Havya son of Atri, and Pulaha—these seven are the sages in the Rauhita Manvantara. O great sage, the groups of gods are three.

## \|\| 9.34.43-44 \|\|

*dīkṣāputrasya putrāste rohitasya prajāpateḥ \|*  
*dhṛṣṭaketurdīptaketuḥ paṃcahasto nirākṛtiḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*pṛthuśravā bhūridyumno ṛcīko bṛhato gayaḥ \|*  
*prathamasya tu sāvarṇernava putrā mahaujasa \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. They are the sons of the patriarch Rohita, son of Dakṣa. These are the nine sons of great strength of the first Sāvarṇi—Dhṛṣṭaketu, Dīptaketu, Pañcahasta, Nirākṛti, Pṛthuśravas, Bhūridyurana, Ṛcīka, Bṛhata and Gaya.

## \|\| 9.34.45-47 \|\|

*daśame tvatha paryāye dvitīyasyāṃtare manoḥ \|*  
*haviṣmānpulahaścaiva prakṛtiścaiva bhārgavaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

*āyo muktistathātreyo vasiṣṭhaścāvyayassmṛtaḥ \|*  
*paulastyaḥ prayatiścaiva bhāmāraścaiva kaśyapaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

*aṅgirānenasassatyaḥ saptaite paramarṣayaḥ \|*  
*devatānāṃ gaṇāścāpi dviṣimaṃtaśca te smṛtāḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

45-47. In the tenth Manvantara of the second Sāvarṇi the following are the sages, viz. Haviṣmat, Pulaha, Prakṛti, Bhārgava, Āya, Mukti, Ātreya and the imperishable Vasiṣṭha along with the seven great sages viz, Paulastya, Prayati, Bhāmāra, Kaśyapa, Aṅgirā, Anenasa, and Satya. Those known as Dviṣimant are the groups of gods.

## \|\| 9.34.48-49 \|\|

*teṣāmindrassmṛtaḥ śambhustvayameva maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*akṣatvānuttamaujāśca bhūriṣeṇaśca vīryavān \|\| 48 \|\|*

*śatānīko nirāmitro vṛṣaseno jayadrathaḥ \|*  
*bhūridyumnaḥ suvarcārcirdaśa tvete manossutāḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

48-49. Śambhu, Maheśvara himself is as their Indra. The ten sons of the Manu are Akṣatvat, Uttamaujas, Bhūriṣeṇa the powerful, Śatānīka, Nirāmitra, Vṛṣasena, Jayadratha, Bhūridyumna, Suvarcā and Arcis.

## \|\| 9.34.50 \|\|

*ekādaśe tu paryāye tṛtīyasyāṃtare manoḥ \|*  
*tasyāpi sapta ṛṣayaḥ kīrtyamānānnibodha me \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. In the eleventh Manvantara of the third Sāvarṇi there are seven sages. Understand them as they are glorified by me.

## \|\| 9.34.51-52 \|\|

*haviṣmānkaśyapaścāpi vapuṣmāṃścaiva vāruṇaḥ \|*  
*atreyo'tha vasiṣṭhaśca hyanayastvaṃgirāstathā \|\| 51 \|\|*

*cārudhṛṣyaśca paulastyo niḥsvaro'gnistu taijasaḥ \|*  
*saptaite ṛṣayaḥ proktāstrayo devagaṇāssmṛtāḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

51-52. Haviṣmat Kaśyapa, Vapuṣmat Vāruṇa, Ātreya, Vasiṣṭha, Anaya, Aṅgiras, Cārudhṛṣya, Paulastya, Niḥsvara, Taijasa Agni. The first seven are the sages and the last three groups are of gods.

## \|\| 9.34.53-54 \|\|

*brahmaṇastu sutāste hi ta ime vaidhṛtāḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*sarvagaśca suśarmmā ca devānīkastu kṣemakaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

*dṛḍheṣuḥ khaṃḍako darśaḥ kuhurbāhurmanoḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*sāvarṇasya tu pautrā vai tṛtīyasya nava smṛtāḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

53-54. There are the sons of Brahmā known as Vaidhṛtas. The grandsons of Sāvarṇa and the sons of the third Sāvarṇi are—Sarvaga, Suśarman, Devānīka, Kṣemaka, Dṛḍheṣu, Khaṇḍaka, Darśa, Kuhu and Bāha, nine in all.

## \|\| 9.34.55-57 \|\|

*caturthasya tu sāvarṇerṛṣīnsapta nibodha me \|*  
*dyutirvasiṣṭhaputraśca ātreyassutapāstathā \|\| 55 \|\|*

*aṃgirāstapaso mūrtistapasvī kaśyapastathā \|*  
*tapodhanaśca paulastyaḥ pulahaśca taporatiḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

*bhārgavassaptamasteṣāṃ vijñeya tapaso nidhiḥ \|*  
*paṃca devagaṇāḥ proktā mānasā brahmaṇassutāḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

55-57. Now know the seven sages of the fourth Sāvarṇi from me—Dyuti son of Vasiṣṭha, Ātreya of good penance, Aṅgiras an embodied form of penance, Kaśyapa the ascetic. Paulastya the sage, Pulaha interested in penance and Bhārgava the storehouse of penance. The groups of gods are known as five and they are the mental sons of Brahmā.

## \|\| 9.34.58-60 \|\|

*ṛtadhāmā tadindro hi trilokī rājyakṛtsukhī \|*  
*dvādaśe caiva paryāye bhāvye raucyāṃtare mune \|\| 58 \|\|*

*aṃgirāścaiva dhṛtimānpaulastyo havyavāṃstu yaḥ \|*  
*paulahastattvadarśī ca bhārgavaśca nirutsavaḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

*niṣprapaṃcastathātreyo nirdehaḥ kaśyapastathā \|*  
*sutapāścaiva vāsiṣṭhassaptaivaite maharṣayaḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

58-60. The Indra then is Ṛtadhāman. He is the happy ruler of the three worlds. In the future twelfth Manvantara, O, sage, the seven great sages are Dhṛtimat, Aṅgiras, Havyavat Paulastya, Tattvadarśin Paulaha, Nirutsava Bhārgava, Niṣprapañca Ātreya, Nirdeha, Kaśyapa and Sutapa the descendant of Vasiṣṭha.

## \|\| 9.34.61-62 \|\|

*traya eva gaṇāḥ proktā devatānāṃ svayaṃbhuvā \|*  
*divaspatistamindro vai vicitraścitra eva ca \|\| 61 \|\|*

*nayo dharmo dhṛtoṃdhraśca sunetraḥ kṣatravṛddhakaḥ \|*  
*nirbhayassutapā droṇo mano raucyasya te sutāḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

61-62. The groups of gods are three in number as mentioned by Svayambhū. They are: Divaspati Indra, Vicitra and Citra Naya, Dharmadhṛta, Andhra, Sunetra, Kṣatravṛddhaka, Nirbhaya, and Sutapas Droṇa are the sons of Raucya Manu.

## \|\| 9.34.63-65 \|\|

*caturddaśe tu paryāye satyasyaivāṃtare manoḥ \|*  
*āgnīdhraḥ kāśyapaścaiva paulastyo māgadhaśca yaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

*bhārgavo'pyativāhyaśca śucirāṃgirasastathā \|*  
*yuktaścaiva tathātreyaḥ pautro vāśiṣṭha eva ca \|\| 64 \|\|*

*ajitaḥ pulahaścaiva hyaṃtyāssaptarṣayaśca te \|*  
*pavitrāścākṣuṣā devāḥ śucirindro bhaviṣyati \|\| 65 \|\|*

63-65. In the fourteenth Manvantara of Satya Manu the seven sages are Āgnīdhra Kāśyapa, Māgadha, Paulastya Atibāhya Bhārgava, Śuci Aṅgirasa, Yukta Ātreya, Ajita grandson of Vasiṣṭha, and Pulaha. They are the final seven sages. The gods are the holy Cākṣuṣas. Śuci will become Indra.

## \|\| 9.34.66 \|\|

*eteṣāṃ kalya utthāya kīrtanātsukhamedhate \|*  
*atītānāgatānāṃ vai maharṣīṇāṃ naraissadā \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. After getting up early in the morning the repetition of the names of these sages of the past and future increases the happiness of men.

## \|\| 9.34.67-68 \|\|

*devatānāṃ gaṇāḥ proktāśśṛṇu paṃca mahāmune \|*  
*turaṃgabhīrurbudhnaśca tanugro'nūgra eva ca \|\| 67 \|\|*

*atimānī pravīṇaśca viṣṇussaṃkraṃdanastathā \|*  
*tejasvī sabalaścaiva satyastvete manossutā \|\| 68 \|\|*

67-68. O great sage, listen. The groups of gods are five. The sons of this Manu are—Taraṅgabhīru, Budhna, Tanūgra, Anugra, Abhimānin Pravīṇa, Viṣṇu, Saṅkrandana, Tejasvin, and Sabala.

## \|\| 9.34.69 \|\|

*bhaumasyaivādhikāre vai pūrvakalpastu pūryate \|*  
*ityete'nāgatā'tītā manavaḥ kīrtitā mayā \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. The earlier Kalpa is in the authority of Bhauma, Thus I have explained the Manus past and future.

## \|\| 9.34.70-71 \|\|

*uktāssanatkumāreṇa vyāsāyāmitatejasā \|*  
*pūrṇe yugasahasrāṃte paripālyaḥ svadharmataḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

*prajābhistapasā yuktā brahmalokaṃ vrajaṃti te \|*  
*yugāni sapratistvekaṃ sāgrāṇyaṃtaramucyate \|\| 71 \|\|*

70-71. These were mentioned to Vyāsa by Sanatkumāra of great splendour. When the thousand Yugas are complete they, of good penance, return to Brahmaloka when their task of protecting the people is over. Each Manvantara consists of seventy-one Yugas with some period left over.

## \|\| 9.34.72 \|\|

*caturdaśaite manavaḥ kīrtitā kīrtivardhanāḥ \|*  
*manvaṃtareṣu sarveṣu saṃhārāṃte punarbhavaḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. These fourteen Manus are glorified. In each Manvantara there is re-creation after annihilation.

## \|\| 9.34.73 \|\|

*na śakyamantaraṃ teṣāṃ vaktuṃ varṣaśatairapi \|*  
*pūrṇe śatasahasre tu kalpo niḥśeṣa ucyate \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. It is difficult to explain Manvantara even in hundreds of years. The Kalpa that follows a hundred thousand Kalpas is named Niśśeṣa.

## \|\| 9.34.74-75 \|\|

*tatra sarvāṇi bhūtāni dagdhānyādityaraśmibhiḥ \|*  
*brahmāṇamagrataḥ kṛtvā sadādityagaṇairmune \|\| 74 \|\|*

*praviśaṃti suraśreṣṭha hariṃ nārāyaṇaṃ param \|*  
*sraṣṭāraṃ sarva bhūtānāṃ kalpāṃteṣu punaḥpunaḥ \|\| 75 \|\|*

74-75. There all the living beings are burnt by the sun’s rays. O sage, all of them enter Viṣṇu at the end of Kalpas along with the Ādityas. Viṣṇu then creates all living beings. This happens again and again.

## \|\| 9.34.76-77 \|\|

*bhūyopi bhagavān rudrassaṃhartā kāla eva hi \|*  
*kalpāṃte tatpravakṣyāmi manorvaivasvatasya vai \|\| 76 \|\|*

*iti te kathitaṃ sarvaṃ manvaṃtarasamudbhavam \|*  
*visargaṃ puṇyamākhyānaṃ dhanyaṃ kulavivarddhanam \|\| 77 \|\|*

76-77. Lord Rudra annihilates them I shall explain it at the end of Vaivasvata Kalpa. Thus I have mentioned all about the Manvantaras to you. It is a holy narrative conclusive to wealth and increase of the family.

## Chapter 35 - The description of Vaivasvata

## \|\| 9.35.1 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*vivasvānkaśyapājjajñe dākṣāyaṇyāṃ mahāṛṣeḥ \|*  
*tasya bhāryā'bhavatsaṃjñā tvāṣṭrī devī sureṇukā \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. Vivasvata was born of Dakṣa’s daughter and Kaśyapa the great sage. His wife was Saṃjñā, daughter of Tvaṣṭṛ and also known as Sureṇukā.

## \|\| 9.35.2 \|\|

*mune'sahiṣṇunā tena tejasā dussahena ca \|*  
*bhartṛrūpeṇa nātuṣyadrūpa yauvanaśālinī \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Due to the unbearable heat of her husband she in her prime of youth was dissatisfied.

## \|\| 9.35.3 \|\|

*ādityasya hi tadrūpamasahiṣṇustu tejasaḥ \|*  
*dahyamānā tadodvegamakarodvaravarṇinī \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Being scorched and not being able to hear the brilliance of the lustrous sun, the fair-complexioned lady was excited and sorrowful.

## \|\| 9.35.4-5 \|\|

*ṛṣe'syāṃ trīṇyapatyāni janayāmāsa bhāskaraḥ \|*  
*saṃjñāyāṃ tu manuḥ pūrvaṃ śrāddhadevaḥ prajāpatiḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*yamaśca yamunā caiva yamalau saṃbabhūvatuḥ \|*  
*evaṃ hi trīṇyapatyāni tasyāṃ jātāni sūryyataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. O sage, the sun begot three offsprings of her: the patriarch Manu Śrāddhadeva, Yama and Yamunā born as twins.

## \|\| 9.35.6 \|\|

*saṃvartulaṃ tu tadrūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃjñā vivasvataḥ \|*  
*asahaṃtī tataśchāyāmātmanassā' sṛjacchubhām \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. On seeing the brilliance of the sun as unbearable as the fire at the final dissolution she created Chāyā of herself.

## \|\| 9.35.7 \|\|

*māyāmayī tu sā saṃjñāmavocadbhaktitaśśubhe \|*  
*kiṃ karomīha kāryyaṃ te kathayasva śucismite \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. She of illusory form told Saṃjñā: “O sweet-smiled auspicious lady, what shall I do for you, please tell me”.

## \|\| 9.35.8 \|\|

*saṃjñovāca \|*  
*ahaṃ yāsyāmi bhadraṃ te mamaiva bhavanaṃ pituḥ \|*  
*tvayaitadbhavane satyaṃ vastavyaṃ nirvikārataḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

Saṃjñā said:—

8\. “Welfare unto you. I go to my father’s house alone. Indeed you have to stay in this house without aberration.

## \|\| 9.35.9 \|\|

*imau me bālakau sādhū kanyā ceyaṃ sumadhyamā \|*  
*pālanīyāḥ sukhenaiva mama cedicchasi priyam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. These two well-behaved sons and this pretty girl should be happily protected if you wish to please me.”

## \|\| 9.35.10 \|\|

*chāyovāca \|*  
*ākeśagrahaṇāddevi sahiṣye'haṃ suduṣkṛtam \|*  
*nākhyāsyāmi mataṃ tubhyaṃ gaccha devi yathāsukham \|\| 10 \|\|*

Chāyā said:—

10\. “O lady, I shall brook their misdeeds even as much as pulling out my hair. I shall make no complaint to you. O goddess, you can go away happily.”

## \|\| 9.35.11 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktā sā'gamaddevī vrīḍitā sannidhau pituḥ \|*  
*pitrā nirbhartsitā tatra niyuktā sā punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

11\. Ashamed on being addressed thus, the lady went to her father’s house but was rebuked by him. He urged her again and again to return to her husband.

## \|\| 9.35.12 \|\|

*agacchadvaḍavā bhūtvā''cchādyarūpaṃ tatastvakam \|*  
*kuruṃstadottarānprāpya nṛṇāṃ madhye cacāra ha \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. She assumed the form of a mare. Going to Northern Kurus she wandered among the people.

## \|\| 9.35.13 \|\|

*saṃjñāṃ tāṃ tu ravirmatvā chāyāyāṃ susutaṃ tadā \|*  
*janayāmāsa sāvarṇiṃ manuṃ vai savitā kila \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The sun took her for Saṃjñā and begot of her a son the Manu Sāvarṇi.

## \|\| 9.35.14 \|\|

*saṃjñā'nu prārthitā chāyā sā svaputre'pi nityaśaḥ \|*  
*cakārābhyadhikaṃ snehaṃ na tathā pūrvaje sute \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Though requested by Saṃjñā, Chāyā did not love the elder children. She loved her own son more and fondled him always.

## \|\| 9.35.15 \|\|

*anujaścākṣamastattu yamastaṃ naiva cakṣame \|*  
*sa saroṣastu bālyācca bhāvino'rthasya gauravāt \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The younger brother Yama could not hear that. He did not forgive. Ever since childhood he was furious and and ill-tempered due to the gravity of what was destined to happen later.

## \|\| 9.35.16 \|\|

*chāyāṃ saṃtarjayāmāsa yadā vaivasvato yamaḥ \|*  
*taṃ śaśāpa tataḥ krodhācchāyā tu kaluṣīkṛtā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. When Yama, son of Vivasvat threatened Chāyā she became infuriated and cursed him angrily.

## \|\| 9.35.17 \|\|

*caraṇaḥ patatāmeṣa taveti bhṛśaroṣitaḥ \|*  
*yamastataḥ pitussarvaṃ prāṃjaliḥ pratyavedayat \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Due to excessive anger she cursed—“Let your leg fall off”. Yama approached his father with palms joined in reverence and said.

## \|\| 9.35.18-20 \|\|

*bhṛśaṃ śāpa bhayodvignassaṃjñāvākyairviceṣṭitaḥ \|*  
*mātrā snehena sarveṣu vartitavyaṃ suteṣu vai \|\| 18 \|\|*

*snehamasmāsvapākṛtya kanīyāṃsaṃ bibharti sā \|*  
*tasmānmayodyataḥ pādastadbhavān kṣaṃtumarhati \|\| 19 \|\|*

*śaptohamasmi deveśa jananyā tapatāṃvara \|*  
*tava prasādāccaraṇo na patenmama gopate \|\| 20 \|\|*

18-20. “I was agitated due to Chāyā’s words. I am sorry and frightened of the curse. I had only said that a mother shall have impartial and equal love for all her children. She has lost affection for us. She nurtures only the youngest one. Hence I raised my foot. It behoves you to forgive me. O lord of gods, O foremost among the refulgent ones, I have been cursed by my mother. O lord of rays, let not my leg fall off by your grace.”

## \|\| 9.35.21 \|\|

*savitovāca \|*  
*asaṃśayaṃ putra mahadbhaviṣyatyatra kāraṇam \|*  
*yena tvāmāviśatkrodho dharmajñaṃ satyavādinam \|\| 21 \|\|*

The sun said:—

21\. O son, there shall be a great cause for this. That was why you too who know virtue and speak the truth had been infuriated.

## \|\| 9.35.22 \|\|

*na śakyate tanmithyā vai karttuṃ mātṛvacastava \|*  
*kṛmayo māṃsamādāya gamiṣyaṃti mahītale \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. It is not possible to make your mother’s words false. Worms will take away the flesh from your leg and go to the earth.

## \|\| 9.35.23 \|\|

*tadvākyaṃ bhavitā satyaṃ tvaṃ ca trātau bhaviṣyasi \|*  
*kuru tāta na saṃdehaṃ manaścāśvāsya svaṃ prabho \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Her words will come up true and you will also be saved. Have no doubt, O deer, cheer up your mind, O lord.

## \|\| 9.35.24 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā tanayaṃ sūryo yamasaṃjñaṃ munīśvara \|*  
*ādityaścābravīttānttu chāyāṃ krodhasamanvitaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

24\. O great sage, after saying thus to his son Yama, the sun angrily spoke to Chāyā.

## \|\| 9.35.25 \|\|

*sūrya uvāca \|*  
*he priye kumate caṃḍi kiṃ tvayā''caritaṃ kila \|*  
*kiṃ tu me'bhyadhikaḥ sneha etadākhyātumarhasi \|\| 25 \|\|*

The sun said:—

25\. O beloved wife, O evil-minded angry lady, what it is that you have done? Why do you love one son more? You shall tell me.

## \|\| 9.35.26 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*sā ravervacanaṃ śrutvā yathā tathyaṃ nyavedayat \|*  
*nirdagdhā kāmaraviṇā sāṃtvayāmāsa vai tadā \|\| 26 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

26\. On hearing the words of the sun she told him the truth. Scorched by him she consoled him thus.

## \|\| 9.35.27 \|\|

*chāyovāca \|*  
*tavātitejasā dagdhā idaṃ rūpaṃ na śobhate \|*  
*asahaṃtī ca tatsaṃjñā vane vasati śādvale \|\| 27 \|\|*

Chāyā said:—

27\. This fierce form of yours was not pleasant to Saṃjñā. She was scorched by your excessive brilliance. She could not bear it. She now resides on the grassy plain in a forest.

## \|\| 9.35.28 \|\|

*ślāghyā yogabalopetā yogamāsādya gopate \|*  
*anukūlastu deveśa saṃdiśyātmamayaṃ matam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O lord of rays, she is endowed with the power of Yoga. She has resorted to yogic practice. She is praiseworthy. O lord of gods, be favourable to her by sending the message of your opinion.

## \|\| 9.35.29 \|\|

*rūpaṃ nivartayāmyadya tava kāṃtaṃ karomyaham \|*  
*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tacchrutvā'pagataḥ krodho mārtaṇḍasya vivasvataḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. I shall change your form. I shall make it pleasingly brilliant.

## \|\| 9.35.30 \|\|

*bhramimāropya tattejaḥ śātayāmāsa vai muniḥ \|*  
*tato vibhrājitaṃ rūpa tejasā saṃvṛtena ca \|\| 30 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

On hearing this, the sun was appeased.

30\. The sage Tvaṣṭṛ put him on the turner’s lathe and sharpened him further. His blazing form was slightly reduced in brilliance.

## \|\| 9.35.31 \|\|

*kṛtaṃ kāṃtataraṃ rūpaṃ tvaṣṭrā tacchuśubhe tadā \|*  
*tatobhiyogamāsthāya svāṃ bhāryyāṃ hi dadarśa ha \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. When the form was made more pleasingly brilliant by Tvaṣṭṛ it shone splendidly. Then resorting to Yogic practice he saw his wife Saṃjñā.

## \|\| 9.35.32 \|\|

*adhṛṣyāṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ tejasā niyamena ca \|*  
*so'śvarūpaṃ samāsthāya gatvā tāṃ maithunecchayā \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. He assumed the form of a horse and approached her for sexual indulgence with her who could not be overwhelmed by any living being due to her lustre and observance of restraint.

## \|\| 9.35.33 \|\|

*maithunāya viceṣṭaṃtīṃ parapuṃsobhiśaṃkayā \|*  
*mukhato nāsikāyāṃ tu śukraṃ tat vyadadhānmune \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. O sage, in the course of the sexual activity she suspected him to be another man. Hence she received the semen through the mouth into the nostril.

## \|\| 9.35.34 \|\|

*devau tataḥ prajāyetāmaśvinau bhiṣajāṃ varau \|*  
*nāsatyau tau ca dasrau ca smṛtau dvāvaśvināvapi \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Thence were born the twin gods Aśvins, the foremost among physicians. They are known as Nāsatyas and Dasras.

## \|\| 9.35.35 \|\|

*tau tu kāṃtena rūpeṇa darśayāmāsa bhāskaraḥ \|*  
*ātmānaṃ sā tu taṃ dṛṣṭvā prahṛṣṭā patimādarāt \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The sun showed his pleasingly splendid form to them. On seeing her husband she was extremely delighted.

## \|\| 9.35.36 \|\|

*patyā tena gṛhaṃ prāyātsvaṃ satī muditānanā \|*  
*mumudāte'tha tau prītyā daṃpato pūrvatodhikam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The chaste lady returned to the house with her husband with the face beaming with pleasure. The pair rejoiced more than before.

## \|\| 9.35.37 \|\|

*yamastu karmaṇā tena bhṛśaṃ pīḍitamānasaḥ \|*  
*dharmeṇa raṃjayāmāsa dharmarāja imā prajāḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. This incident distressed the mind of Yama. As pious king he gladdened the subjects virtuously.

## \|\| 9.35.38 \|\|

*lebhe sa karmaṇā tena dharmarājo mahādyutiḥ \|*  
*pitṝṇāmādhipatyaṃ ca lokapālatvameva ca \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Yama of great lustre attained the lordship of the names and the guardianship of the quarters.

## \|\| 9.35.39 \|\|

*manuḥ prajāpatistvāsītsāvarṇissa tapodhanaḥ \|*  
*bhāvyaḥ sa karmaṇā tena manossāvarṇikeṃtare \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Sāvarṇi Manu became the patriarch. In the Sāvarṇika Manvantara he will become the future Manu by virtue of his action.

## \|\| 9.35.40-42 \|\|

*merupṛṣṭhe tapo ghoramadyāpi carate prabhuḥ \|*  
*yavīyasī tayoryā tu yamī kanyā yaśasvinī \|\| 40 \|\|*

*abhavatsā saricchreṣṭhā yamunā lokapā vanī \|*  
*manurityucyate loke sāvarṇiriti cocyate \|\| 41 \|\|*

*ya idaṃ janma devānāṃ śṛṇuyāddhārayettu vā \|*  
*āpadaṃ prāpya mucyeta prāpnuyātsumahadyaśaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

40-42. The lord is performing penance even today on the top of Meru. Their younger sister, the famous Yamī, became the most excellent river Yamunā, the sanctifier of all the worlds. He is called Sāvarṇi Manu in the world. He who listens to or retains in memory this origin of the gods attains great fame. Should he suffer from any adversity he will be rid of it.

## Chapter 36 - The description of the nine sons of and the race of Vaivasvata Manu

## \|\| 9.36.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*manorvaivasvatasyāsanputrā vai nava tatsamāḥ \|*  
*paścānmahonnatā dhīrāḥ kṣatradharmaparāyaṇāḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. Nine sons all equal to him were born of Vaivasvata Manu. Later they became very lofty, bold and devoted to the virtues of Kṣatriyas.

## \|\| 9.36.2 \|\|

*ikṣvākuḥ śibinābhāgau dhṛṣṭaḥ śaryātireva ca \|*  
*nariṣyanto'tha nābhāgaḥ karūṣaśca priyavrataḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. They were Ikṣvāku, Śibi, Nābhāga, Dhṛṣṭa, Śaryāti, Nariṣyanta, Nabhaga, Karūṣa and Priyavrata.

## \|\| 9.36.3-5 \|\|

*akarotputtrakāmastu manuriṣṭiṃ prajāpati \|*  
*anutpanneṣu putreṣu tatreṣṭyāṃ munipuṃgavaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*sā hi divyāṃbaradharā divyābharaṇabhūṣitā \|*  
*divyasaṃhananā caivamilā jajñe hi viśrutā \|\| 4 \|\|*

*tāmiḍetyeva hovāca manurdaṇḍadharastathā \|*  
*anugacchatva māmeti tamiḍā pratyuvāca ha \|\| 5 \|\|*

3-5. Desirous of sons, Manu the patriarch performed a sacrifice. O great sage, when no sons were born as a result of the sacrifice, the famous Ilā was born, wearing divine garments, bedecked in divine ornaments and with a divine body. Manu the chastiser addressed her as Ilā and said “Follow me”. Ilā replied to him.

## \|\| 9.36.6 \|\|

*iḍovāca \|*  
*dharmayuktamidaṃ vākyaṃ putrakāmaṃ prajāpatim \|*  
*mitrāvaruṇayoraṃśairjātāsmi vadatāṃ vara \|\| 6 \|\|*

Ilā said:—

6\. She spoke these words to the patriarch desirous of a son—“O foremost among the eloquent, I am born of the parts of Mitra and Varuṇa.

## \|\| 9.36.7-9 \|\|

*tayossakāśaṃ yāsyāmi na me dharme rucirbhavet \|*  
*evamuktvā satī sā tu mitrāvaruṇayostataḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*gatvāṃtikaṃ varārohā prāṃjalirvākyamabravīt \|*  
*aṃśaistu yuvayorjātā manuyajñe mahāmunī \|\| 8 \|\|*

*āgatā bhavatoraṃti brūtaṃ kiṃ karavāṇi vām \|*  
*anyānputrānsṛja vibho tairvaṃśaste bhaviṣyati \|\| 9 \|\|*

7-9. I am approaching them. I shall never be interested in sinful things.” After saying this, the chaste lady approached Mitra and Varuṇa and spoke with palms joined in reverence “O great sages, I am born in the sacrifice of Manu through your parts. I have come to you. Please tell me. What I can do for you. O lords create other sons so that your race may flourish.”

## \|\| 9.36.10 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tāṃ tathāvādinīṃ sādhvīmiḍāṃ manvadhvarodbhavām \|*  
*mitrāvaruṇānāmānau munī ūcaturādarāt \|\| 10 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

10\. When the chaste lady born in the sacrifice of Manu said, the sages Mitra and Varuna spoke thus respectfully.

## \|\| 9.36.11 \|\|

*mitrāvaruṇāvūcatuḥ \|*  
*anena tava dharmajñe praśrayeṇa damena ca \|*  
*satyena caiva suśroṇi prītau dvau varavarṇini \|\| 11 \|\|*

Mitra and Varuṇa said:—

11\. O virtuous beautiful lady of fair complexion, both of us are pleased with your self-control, humility and truth.

## \|\| 9.36.12 \|\|

*āvayostvaṃ mahābhāge khyātiṃ caiva gamiṣyasi \|*  
*manorvaśakaraḥ putrastvameva ca bhaviṣyasi \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. O blessed lady, you will attain our fame. You alone will become the son establishing the family of Manu.

## \|\| 9.36.13 \|\|

*sudyumna iti vikhyātastriṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ \|*  
*jagatpriyo dharmaśīlau manuvaṃśavivarddhanaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. You will be famous in the three worlds as Sudyumna, beloved of the universe, virtuous in conduct and enhancer of the race of Manu.

## \|\| 9.36.14 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*nivṛttā sā tu tacchrutvā gacchaṃtī pituraṃtike \|*  
*budhenāṃtaramāsādya maidhunāyopamaṃtritā \|\| 14 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

14\. On hearing that she returned to her father’s presence. On the way, getting an opportunity, Budha invited her for sexual intercourse.

## \|\| 9.36.15 \|\|

*somaputrāttato jajñe tasyāṃ rājā purūravāḥ \|*  
*putro'tisundaraḥ prājña urvaśī patirunnataḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. King Purūravas was born of her out of the union with Budha. O intelligent one, this son was the handsome and intelligent man who later became the husband of Urvaśī.

## \|\| 9.36.16 \|\|

*janayitvā ca sā tatra purūravasamādarāt \|*  
*putraṃ śivaprasādāttu punassudyumnatāṃ gataḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. After bearing the son Purūravas she became Sudyumna as a result of Śiva’s grace.

## \|\| 9.36.17 \|\|

*sudyumnasya tu dāyādāstrayaḥ paramadhārmikāḥ \|*  
*utkalaśca gayaścāpi vinatāśvaśca vīryavān \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Sudyumna had three very virtuous heirs Utkala, Gaya and the powerful Vinatāśva.

## \|\| 9.36.18 \|\|

*utkalasyotkalā viprā vinatāśvasya paścimāḥ \|*  
*dikpūrvā muni śārdūla gayasya tu gayā smṛtāḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O brahmin, O great sage, Utkala was assigned the country Utkala; Vinatāśva was given the territories on the West and Gaya was given Gayā and the eastern territories.

## \|\| 9.36.19 \|\|

*praviṣṭe tu manau tāta divākaratanuṃ tadā \|*  
*daśadhā tatra tatkṣetramakarotpṛthivīṃ manuḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. O dear, when Manu passed away, the earth was divided into ten regions.

## \|\| 9.36.20-24 \|\|

*ikṣvākuḥ śreṣṭhadāyādo madhyadeśamavāptavān \|*  
*vasiṣṭhavacanādāsītpratiṣṭhānaṃ mahātmanaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*pratiṣṭhāṃ dharmarājyasya sudyumnotha tato dadau \|*
