40\. Different weapons and missiles hurled by the hosts of enemies were rendered futile instantaneously by the power of the goddess.

## \|\| 9.46.41 \|\|

*tato jaghāna sā devī cikṣurapramukhānarīn \|*  
*sagaṇāngadayā bāṇaiḥ śūlaśaktiparaśvadhaiḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Then the goddess hit and struck the hosts of enemies Gikṣura and others with her club, arrows, spear, Śakti and axe.

## \|\| 9.46.42 \|\|

*evaṃ svīyeṣu sainyeṣu hateṣu mahiṣāsuraḥ \|*  
*devīniḥśvāsasaṃbhūtānbhāvayāmāsa tāngaṇān \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. When his armies fell the demon Mahiṣa struggled with the hosts of soldiers that issued from the breath of the goddess.

## \|\| 9.46.43 \|\|

*atāḍayatsaraiḥ kāścitkāścicchṛṅgadvayena ca \|*  
*lāṃgūlena ca tuṇḍena bhinatti sma muhurmuhuḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. He kicked some with the hoofs, hit some with the horns and others with his tail and snout.

## \|\| 9.46.44 \|\|

*itthaṃ devīgaṇā nhatvābhyadhāvatso'surādhipaḥ \|*  
*siṃhaṃ mārayitundevyāstato'sau kupitā'bhavat \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. After slaying the Gaṇas the chief of Asuras rushed at the lion of the goddess in order to kill it. Then she became wrathful.

## \|\| 9.46.45 \|\|

*kopātsopi mahāvīryaḥ khurakuṭṭitabhūtalaḥ \|*  
*śṛṅgābhyāṃ śailamutpāṭya cikṣepa praṇanāda ca \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. He of great virility thrashed the ground with his hoofs, uprooted a mountain with his horns, hurled it and shouted.

## \|\| 9.46.46 \|\|

*vegena viṣvag bhramatā prakṣiptā guravo'drayaḥ \|*  
*ākāśato mahīmadhye nipeturnṛpasattama \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. O excellent king, heavy mountains hurled by him as he rushed all round fell in the battle-ground from the sky.

## \|\| 9.46.47 \|\|

*śṛṃgabhinnāḥ payovāhāḥ khaṇḍaṃ khaṇḍamayāsiṣuḥ \|*  
*lāṃgūlenāhataścābdhirviṣvagudvelamaspadat \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Clouds split by his horns were shattered to pieces. The ocean struck at with his tail throbbed and splashed water beyond the shores all round.

## \|\| 9.46.48 \|\|

*evaṃ kruddhaṃ samālokya mahiṣāsuramambikā \|*  
*vidadhe tadvadhopāyaṃ devānāmabhayaṃkarī \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. On seeing the demon Mahiṣa thus infuriated, Ambikā, the protectress of the gods, became ready to kill him.

## \|\| 9.46.49 \|\|

*tataḥ pāśaṃ samutthāya kṣiptvā tasyoparī śvarī \|*  
*babandha mahiṣaṃ so'pi rūpantatyāja māhiṣam \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. She took up a noose and hurled it at him. She bound the Asura Mahiṣa. Then the demon abandoned his assumed guise.

## \|\| 9.46.50 \|\|

*tataḥ siṃho babhūvāśu māyāvī tacchirombikā \|*  
*yāvadbhinatti tāvatsa khaṅgapāṇirbabhūva ha \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Wielding Māyā, he became a lion. Soon as Ambikā tried to cut off his head he became armed with a sword.

## \|\| 9.46.51 \|\|

*sacarmmāsikaraṃ taṃ ca devī bāṇairatāḍayat \|*  
*tato gajavapurbhūtvā siṃhaṃ ciccheda śuṇḍayā \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. She then struck him with the arrows, as he stood there with the sword and shield. He then became an elephant and struck at the lion with his trunk.

## \|\| 9.46.52 \|\|

*tato'sya ca karaṃ devī cakarta svamahāsinā \|*  
*adhāri ca punā rūpaṃ svakīyaṃ tena rakṣasā \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. The goddess cut off his hand with her sword. The demon then assumed his original form.

## \|\| 9.46.53 \|\|

*tadaiva kṣobhayāmāsa trailokyaṃ sacarācaram \|*  
*tataḥ kruddhā mahāmāyā caṇḍikā mānavikramā \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. He then agitated three worlds including the mobile and immobile beings. Then, Caṇḍikā of great honour and exploit became infuriated.

## \|\| 9.46.54 \|\|

*papau punaḥpunaḥ pānaṃ jahāsodbhrāntalocanā \|*  
*jagarja cāsuraḥ so'pi balavīryamado ddhataḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. She drank the beverage again and again. With eyes rolliug she laughed aloud. The haughty Asura too, proud of his strength and exploit, roared.

## \|\| 9.46.55 \|\|

*tasyā upari cikṣepa śailānutpāṭya so'suraḥ \|*  
*sā ca bāṇāvalīghātaiścūrṇayāmāsa satvaram \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. He uprooted the mountains and hurled them at her. She too reduced them to powder and struck them with arrows.

## \|\| 9.46.56 \|\|

*vāruṇīmadrasaṃ jātamukharāgā''kulendriyā \|*  
*provāca parameśānī meghagaṃbhīrayā girā \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. With her face reddened as a result of the inebriation after drinking wine, and with her senses excited, she spoke in a tone as majestic as the rumbling of the clouds.

## \|\| 9.46.57 \|\|

*\|\| devyuvāca \|*  
*re mūḍha re hataprajña vyartha kiṃ kuruṣe haṭham \|*  
*na madagre'surāḥ kepi sthāsnavo jagatītraye \|\| 57 \|\|*

The goddess said:—

57\. “O stupid fellow, O demon of ruined intellect, why are you stubborn in vain? None of the Asuras in the three worlds can stand before me”.

## \|\| 9.46.58 \|\|

*ṛṣi ruvāca \|*  
*ekamābhāṣya kūrditvā devī sarvakalāmayī \|*  
*padākramyāsuraṃ kaṇṭhe śūlenogreṇa sā'bhinat \|\| 58 \|\|*

The sage said:—

58\. Thus saying the goddess pounced on him, stamped on his neck and pierced him with her fierce spear.

## \|\| 9.46.59 \|\|

*tatastaccaraṇākrāntassa svakīyamukhāttataḥ \|*  
*arddhaniṣkrānta evāsīddevyā vīryeṇa saṃvṛtaḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Pressed with her foot and overwhelmed by her might the Asura seemed as good as half dead.

## \|\| 9.46.60 \|\|

*arddhaniṣkrānta evāsau yudhyamāno mahādhamaḥ \|*  
*mahāsinā śiro bhittvā nyapāti dharaṇītale \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Still he continued to fight. His head was cut off with the sword and he was felled down on the ground.

## \|\| 9.46.61 \|\|

*hāhāśabdaṃ samuccāryyāvāṅmukhāstadgaṇāstataḥ \|*  
*palāyanta raṇādbhītāstrāhitrāhīti vādinaḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Crying “Alas! Alas” loudly, his followers, afraid of the battle fled from the scene shouting “Save us, O save us”.

## \|\| 9.46.62 \|\|

*tuṣṭuvuśca tadā devīmindrādyāḥ sakalāḥ surāḥ \|*  
*gandharvā gītamuccerurnanṛturnartakījanāḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. The god Indra and others, eulogised the goddess. The Gandharvas sang songs; the celestial damsels danced.

## \|\| 9.46.63 \|\|

*evante kathito rājanmahālakṣmyāḥ samudbhavaḥ \|*  
*sarasvatyāstathotpattiṃ śṛṇu susthena cetasā \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. O king, thus the origin of Mahālakṣmī has been narrated to you. Now listen to the origin of Sarasvatī with a calm mind.

## Chapter 47 - Dhūmralocana, Caṇḍa, Muṇḍa and Raktabīja are slain

## \|\| 9.47.1 \|\|

*\|\| ṛṣiruvāca \|*  
*āsīcchumbhāsuro daityo niśuṃbhaśca pratāpavān \|*  
*trailokyamojasā krāntaṃ bhrātṛbhyāṃ sacarācaram \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sage said:—

1\. There was a Daitya Śumbha and another equally powerful Niśumbha. The three worlds including mobile and immobile beings were attacked by the two brothers.

## \|\| 9.47.2 \|\|

*tābhyāmprapīḍitā devā himavantaṃ samāyayuḥ \|*  
*jananīṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ kāmadātrīṃ vavandire \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The gods afflicted and harassed by them went to Himalaya and saluted the mother of all living beings, the bestower of all desires.

## \|\| 9.47.3 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*jaya durge maheśāni jayātmīyajanapriye \|*  
*trailokyatrāṇakāriṇyai śivāyai te namonamaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

The gods said:—

3\. O goddess Durgā, Victory be to you. O, beloved of your kinsmen, be victorious. Obeisance to you, the cause of the protection of the three worlds.

## \|\| 9.47.4 \|\|

*namo muktipradāyinyai parāmbāyai namonamaḥ \|*  
*namaḥ samastasaṃsārotpattisthityantakārike \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Obeisance to you, the bestower of salvation, obeisance to the great mother, the cause of the creation, sustenance and annihilation of the world.

## \|\| 9.47.5 \|\|

*kālikārūpasaṃpanno namaskārākṛte namaḥ \|*  
*chinnamastāsvarūpāyai śrīvidyāyai namostu te \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. O goddess of the form of Kālikā, Tārā. Chinnamastā, Śrīvidyā, Obeisance to you.

## \|\| 9.47.6 \|\|

*bhuvaneśi namastubhyaṃ namaste bhairavākṛte \|*  
*namostu bagalāmukhyai dhūmāvatyai namonamaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. O goddess of the worlds, of the form of Bhairava, obeisance to you. Obeisance to Bagalāmukhī (stork-faced). Obeisance, to Dhūmāvatī.

## \|\| 9.47.7 \|\|

*namastripurasundaryyai mātaṅgayai te namonamaḥ \|*  
*ajitāyai namastubhyaṃ vijayāyai namonamaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Obeisance to Tripurasundarī, to Mātaṅgī, to Ajitā (the unconquered) and to Vijayā.

## \|\| 9.47.8 \|\|

*jayāyai maṃgalāyai te vilāsinyai namonamaḥ \|*  
*dogdhrīrūpe namastubhyaṃ namo ghorākṛte'stu te \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Obeisance to Jayā, Maṅgalā Vilāsinī, to you of the form of milking maid and of terrible form.

## \|\| 9.47.9 \|\|

*mano'parājitākāre nityākāre namonamaḥ \|*  
*śaraṇāgatapālinyai rudrāṇyai te namonamaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Obeisance to you, O goddess, of the form of the unconquered, of permanent form, the protectress of those who seek refuge in you.

## \|\| 9.47.10 \|\|

*namo vedāntavedyāyai namaste paramātmane \|*  
*anantakoṭibrahmāṇḍanāyikāyai namonamaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Obeisance to the one knowable through Vedānta; obeisance to you the great soul, the heroine of infinite worlds of this universe.

## \|\| 9.47.11 \|\|

*iti devaiḥ stutā gaurī prasannā varadā śivā \|*  
*provāca tridaśānsarvānyuṣmābhiḥ stūyate'tra kā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Eulogised thus by the gods the delighted goddess the bestower of boons, spoke, to the gods—“Who is being eulogised by you here?”

## \|\| 9.47.12 \|\|

*tato gaurītanorekā prādurāsītkumārikā \|*  
*sovāca miṣatāṃ teṣāṃ śivaśaktiṃ parādarāt \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Then a virgin came out of the body of the goddess. Even as they were winking she spoke to the goddess with great reverence.

## \|\| 9.47.13 \|\|

*stotraṃ me kriyate mātaḥ samastaiḥ svargavāsibhiḥ \|*  
*niśuṃbhaśuṃbhadaityābhyāṃ prabalābhyāṃ prapīḍitaiḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. “O mother, this eulogy is addressed to me by the heaven-dwellers harassed by the powerful Daityas Śumbha and Niśumbha.”

## \|\| 9.47.14 \|\|

*śarīrakośādyattasyā nirgatā tena kauśikī \|*  
*nāmnā sā gīyate sākṣācchuṃbhāsuranibarhiṇī \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Since she emerged out of the inner vestures of her body she is called Kauśikī. The destroyer of the Asura Śumbha is being sung in that name.

## \|\| 9.47.15 \|\|

*caivogratārikā proktā mahogratārikāpi ca \|*  
*prādurbhūtā yataḥ sā vai mātaṃgītyucyate bhuvi \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. She alone is said to be Ugratārikā and great Ugratārikā. She is called Mātaṅgī after the source from which she manifested herself.

## \|\| 9.47.16 \|\|

*babhāṣe nikhilāndevānyūyaṃ tiṣṭhata nirbhayāḥ \|*  
*kāryaṃ vaḥ sādhayiṣyāmi svatantrāhaṃ vināśrayam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. She addressed the gods—“All of you stay here fearlessly. I shall carry out your task without any strain.

I am independent”.

## \|\| 9.47.17 \|\|

*ityuktvā sā tadā devī tarasāntarhitā'bhavat \|*  
*cāṇḍamuṇḍau tu tāndevīmadrāṣṭāṃ sevakau tayoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. After saying this, the goddess vanished. Caṇḍa and Muṇḍa—the attendants of Śumbha and Niśumba saw the goddess.

## \|\| 9.47.18 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā manoharaṃ tasyā rūpaṃ netrasukhāvaham \|*  
*petatustau dharāmadhye naṣṭasaṃjñau vimohitau \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. On seeing her beautiful form pleasing to the eyes they were fascinated. They lost their consciousness and fell on the ground.

## \|\| 9.47.19 \|\|

*gatvā vyājahratuḥ sarvaṃ rājñe vṛttāntamāditaḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭā kācinmayā pūrvā nārī rājanmanoramā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Returning to their king they narrated the details to him “O king, a certain beautiful lady hitherto unseen has been seen by us.

## \|\| 9.47.20 \|\|

*himavacchikhare ramye saṃsthitā siṃhavāhinī \|*  
*samantāddevakanyābhiḥ sevitā baddhapāṇibhiḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Seated on a lion, on the beautiful peak of the Himavat she was served by the virgins of the gods with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 9.47.21 \|\|

*kurute pādasaṃvāhaṃ kācitsaṃskurute kacān \|*  
*pāṇisaṃvāhanaṃ kācitkācinnetrāñjanaṃ nyadhāt \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. They massaged her feet and hands, decorated her tresses, and applied collyrium to her eyes.

## \|\| 9.47.22 \|\|

*kācid gṛhītvā hastenādarśaṃ darśayate mukham \|*  
*nāgavallīṃ dadātyekā lavaṃgailādisaṃyutām \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. A certain damsel held out the mirror to her face. Another handed over the betel-leaf along with cloves and cardamom.

## \|\| 9.47.23 \|\|

*patadgrahaṃ kare kṛtvā sthitā kācitsakhī puraḥ \|*  
*bhūṣayatyakhilāṃgāni kācidbhūṣāmbarādibhiḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Some picked up the fallen dice and stood in front of her. Some bedecked her body with ornaments and clothes.

## \|\| 9.47.24 \|\|

*kadalīstaṃbhajaṃghoruḥ kīranāsā'hidaurlatā \|*  
*raṇanmañjīracaraṇā ramyamekhalayā yutā \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Her calves and thighs are like the stem of the plantain tree. Her nose is like that of a parrot. Her arms are like serpents and creepers. She wears a beautiful girdle. The anklets round her feet are jingling.

## \|\| 9.47.25 \|\|

*lasatkastūrikāmodamuktāhāracalastanī \|*  
*graiveyakalasadgrīvā lalantīdāmamaṇḍitā \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Pearl necklaces heave over her breasts rendered fragrant with the musk. Necklaces shine round her neck. She was sporting about bedecked in chaplets.

## \|\| 9.47.26 \|\|

*arddhacandradharā devī maṇikuṇḍaladhāriṇī \|*  
*ramyaveṇirviṃśālākṣī locanatrayabhūṣitā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. She has semi-circular marks on her forehead. She wears car-rings set with gems. Her plaited hair are pretty. The wide three eyes bedeck her face.

## \|\| 9.47.27 \|\|

*sākṣarā mālikopetā paṇirājitakaṃkaṇā \|*  
*svarṇormikāṃgulirbhrājatpārihāryyalasatkarā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. She is imperishable, possessed of garlands. Bangles shine in her hands. There are gold rings round her fingers. Shining bracelets make her hands dazzle.

## \|\| 9.47.28 \|\|

*śubhavastrāvṛtā gaurī padmāsanavirājitā \|*  
*kāśmīrabindutilakā candrālaṃkṛtamastakā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. She is clad in white garments. She shines in her lotus-pose with the marks of saffron on her forehead which is also bedecked with the moon.

## \|\| 9.47.29 \|\|

*taḍiddyutirmahāmūlyāmbara colonnamatkucā \|*  
*bhujairaṣṭābhiruttuṃgairdhārayantī varāyudhān \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. She has the brilliance of lightning. She wears precious garments. Her breasts are elevated within her bodice. She holds excellent weapons with her eight uplifted arms.

## \|\| 9.47.30 \|\|

*tādṛśī nāsurī nāgī na gandharvī na dānavī \|*  
*vidyate triṣu lokeṣu yādṛśī sā manoramā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. There is no lady among the Asuras, Nāgas, Gandharvas or Dānavas in the three worlds as beautiful as she.

## \|\| 9.47.31 \|\|

*tasmātsaṃbhogayogyatvaṃ tasyāstvayyeva śobhate \|*  
*nārīratnaṃ yataḥ sā vai puṃratnaṃ ca bhavānprabho \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Hence the fitness for sexual indulgence with her rests only in you since she is a gem among ladies and O lord, you are a gem among men.

## \|\| 9.47.32 \|\|

*ityuktaṃ caṇḍamuṇḍābhyāṃ niśamya sa mahāsuraḥ \|*  
*dūtaṃ sugrīvanāmānaṃ preṣayāmāsa tāṃ prati \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. On hearing the words of Caṇḍa and Muṇḍa that great demon Śumbha sent a messenger Sugrīva to her.

## \|\| 9.47.33 \|\|

*gaccha dūta tuṣārādrau tatrāste kāpi sundarī \|*  
*sā netavyā prayatnena kathayitvā vaco mama \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. “O messenger, there is a certain beautiful lady on the snow-capped mountain. Mentioning these words of mine she shall be strenuously brought here”.

## \|\| 9.47.34 \|\|

*iti vijñāpitastena sugrīvo dānavottamaḥ \|*  
*gatvā himācalaṃ prāha jagadambāṃ maheśvarīm \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Thus urged by him, the excellent Dānava Sugrīva went to Himācala and spoke to the great goddess, the mother of the universe.

## \|\| 9.47.35 \|\|

*dūta uvāca \|*  
*devi śuṃbhāsuro daityo niśuṃbhastasya cānujaḥ \|*  
*vikhyātastriṣu lokeṣu mahā balaparākramaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

The messenger said:—

35\. O gentle lady, the Daitya Śumbha and his brother Niśumbha are very strong and valorous. They are famous in the three worlds.

## \|\| 9.47.36 \|\|

*cārohaṃ preṣitastena sannidhinte samāgamam \|*  
*sa yajjagau sureśāni tatsamākarṇayādhunā \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. O goddess, I am a messenger of Śumbha. I have come with his message to which you will listen please.

## \|\| 9.47.37 \|\|

*indrādīnsamare jitvā teṣāṃ ratnānyapāharam \|*  
*devabhāgaṃ svayaṃ bhuñje yāge dattaṃ surādibhiḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. “I have defeated Indra and others in the battle and taken away their jewels. I enjoy the share of the gods offered to them in the sacrifices.

## \|\| 9.47.38 \|\|

*strīratnaṃ tvāmahaṃ manye sarvaratnopari sthitam \|*  
*sā tvaṃ mamānujaṃ māṃ vā bhajatātkāmajai rasaiḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. You are a jewel among women, more precious than ether jewels. Resort to me or to my younger brother, exhibiting your loving emotion.

## \|\| 9.47.39 \|\|

*iti dūtoktamākarṇya vacanaṃ śuṃbhabhāṣitam \|*  
*jagāda sā mahāmāyā bhūteśaprāṇavallabhā \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. On hearing the message of Śumbha conveyed by the messenger the Mahāmāyā, the beloved consort of Śiva, spoke.

## \|\| 9.47.40-41 \|\|

*\|\| devyuvāca \|*  
*satyaṃ vadasi bho dūta nānṛtaṃ kiṃciducyate \|*  
*parantvekā kṛtā pūrvaṃ pratijñā tānnibodha me \|\| 40 \|\|*

*yo me darpaṃ vidhunute yo māṃ jayati saṃgare \|*  
*utsahe tamahaṃ kartuṃ patiṃ nānyamiti dhruvam \|\| 41 \|\|*

The goddess said:—

40-41. O messenger, you speak the truth, not the lie. Bat I have taken a vow that I shall endeavour to make one alone as my husband who can shake off my pride, who can conquer me in the battle. None else.

## \|\| 9.47.42 \|\|

*sa tvaṃ kathaya śuṃbhāya niśuṃbhāya vaco mama \|*  
*yathā yuktaṃ bhavedevaṃ vidadhātu tathā'tra saḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Hence you convey my words to Śumbha and Niśumbha. Let either do whatever is proper in this matter.

## \|\| 9.47.43 \|\|

*itthaṃ devīvacaḥ śrutvā sugrīvo nāma dānavaḥ \|*  
*rājñe vijñāpayāmāsa gatvā tatra savistaram \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. On hearing these words of the goddess the Asura Sugrīva returned to his king and acquainted him with every detail.

## \|\| 9.47.44 \|\|

*atha dūtoktamākarṇya śuṃbho bhairavaśāsanaḥ \|*  
*dhūmrākṣaṃ prāha sakrodhaḥ senānyaṃ balināṃ varam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. On hearing the report of the messenger Śumbha of stern rule angrily said to Dhūmrākṣa, the foremost of his generals.

## \|\| 9.47.45-46 \|\|

*he dhūmrākṣa tuṣārādrau vartate kāpi sundarī \|*  
*tāmānaya drutaṃ gatvā yathā yāsyati sātra vai \|\| 45 \|\|*

*tasyā ānayane bhītirna kāryyā'surasattama \|*  
*yuddhaṃ kāryaṃ prayatnena yadi sā yoddhumicchati \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. O Dhūmrākṣa, there is a beautiful lady on the snow-capped Himalaya mountain. Go quickly and fetch her here. O excellent Asura, do not be afraid of this expedition. If she wishes to fight, you shall fight strenuously.

## \|\| 9.47.47 \|\|

*evaṃ vijñāpito daityo dhūmralocanasaṃjñakaḥ \|*  
*gatvā himācalaṃ prāha bhuvaneśīmumāṃśajām \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Thus directed the Daitya Dhūmrākṣa went to Himālaya and spoke to the goddess who was a part of Umā.

## \|\| 9.47.48 \|\|

*bharturmamāntikaṃ gaccha nocettvāṃ ghātayāmyaham \|*  
*puṣṭyā'surāṇāṃ sahitaḥ sahasrāṇāṃ nitaṃbini \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. “O lady, approach my lord. Otherwise I shall kill you. I am accompanied by sixty-thousand Asuras”

## \|\| 9.47.49 \|\|

*devyuvāca \|*  
*daityarāṭpreṣito vīra haṃsi cetkiṃ karomi te \|*  
*parantvasādhyaṃ gamanaṃ manye saṃgrāmamantarā \|\| 49 \|\|*

The goddess said:—

49\. “O hero, yon are commissioned by the king of Daityas. If you kill me what can I do for you? But I consider it impossible to go without a fight.”

## \|\| 9.47.50 \|\|

*ityuktastāmanvadhāvaddānavo dhūmralocanaḥ \|*  
*huṃkāroccāraṇenaiva tandadāha maheśvarī \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Thus addressed, the Dānava Dhūmrākṣa rushed at her but was burnt with a mere Huṅkāra uttered by her.

## \|\| 9.47.51 \|\|

*tataḥ prabhṛti sā devī dhūmāvatyucyate bhuvi \|*  
*ārādhitā svabhaktānāṃ śatruvarganikartinī \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Since then, that goddess is called Dhūmāvatī in the world. When propitiated she destroys hosts of enemies of her devotees.

## \|\| 9.47.52 \|\|

*dhūmrākṣe nihate devyā vāhanenātikopinā \|*  
*carvitāstadgaṇāssarve'palāyantāvaśeṣitāḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. After Dhūnirākṣa was killed, his army was crunched and munched by the infuriated lion. Those who were spared fled away.

## \|\| 9.47.53 \|\|

*itthaṃ devyā hataṃ daityaṃ śrutvā śuṃbhaḥ pratāpavān \|*  
*cakāra bahulaṃ kopaṃ sandaṣṭoṣṭhapuṭadvayaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. On hearing that the Daitya was killed by the goddess, the valorous Śumbha was angry and bit his lips.

## \|\| 9.47.54 \|\|

*caṇḍaṃ muṃḍaṃ raktabījaṃ praiṣayatkramato' surān \|*  
*tepi cājñāpitā daityā yayuryatrāmbikā sthitā \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. He sent the Asuras—Caṇḍa, Muṇḍa and Raktabīja in order. The Daityas, thus ordered, went to the spot where Ambikā was stationed.

## \|\| 9.47.55 \|\|

*siṃhārūḍhā bhagavatīmaṇimādibhirāśritām \|*  
*bhāsayaṃtī diśo bhāsā dṛṣṭvocurddānavarṣabhāḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. On seeing the goddess seated on a lion, possessed of Aṇimā and other Siddhis and dazzling the quarters with her brilliance, the leading Dānavas said.

## \|\| 9.47.56 \|\|

*he devi tarasā mūlaṃ yāhi śuṃbhaniśuṃbhayoḥ \|*  
*anyathā ghātayiṣyāmaḥ sagaṇāṃ tvāṃ savāhanām \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. “O lady, hasten to approach Śumbha and Niśumbha. Otherwise we shall kill you along with your Gaṇas and your lion.

## \|\| 9.47.57 \|\|

*vṛṇīṣva taṃ patiṃ vāme lokapālādibhiḥ stutam \|*  
*prapatsyase mahānaṃdaṃ devānāmapi durlabham \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. O lady, choose him as your husband. He is eulogised by the guardians of the quarters and others. You will attain a great bliss rare to attain by the gods.”

## \|\| 9.47.58 \|\|

*ityuktamākalayyāmbā smayitvā parameśvarī \|*  
*udājahāra sā devī sūnṛtaṃ rasavadvacaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. On hearing these words the goddess Ambā smiled and spoke interesting and truthful words.

## \|\| 9.47.59 \|\|

*devyuvāca \|*  
*advitīyo maheśānaḥ parabrahma sadāśivaḥ \|*  
*yattattvanna vidurvedā viṣṇvādīnāṃ ca kā kathā \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Lord Śiva, the great Braḥman, the eternal god is without a second. Even the Vedas do not understand his reality. What then of Viṣṇu and others?

## \|\| 9.47.60 \|\|

*tasyāhaṃ prakṛtiḥ sakṣmā kathamanyaṃ patimvṛṇe \|*  
*siṃhī kāmāturā naiva jambukaṃ vṛṇute kvacit \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. I am His subtle Prakṛti. How can I choose another as my husband? Even if she is overwhelmed by love does a lioness choose a jackal as her mate?

## \|\| 9.47.61 \|\|

*kareṇurgardabhaṃ naiva dvīpinī śaśakaṃ na vā \|*  
*mṛṣā vadata bho daityo mṛtyuvyālaniyaṃtritāḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. A female elephant does not take an ass nor does a leopardess take a hare. O Daityas, overcome by the Serpent of Death you speak in vain.

## \|\| 9.47.62 \|\|

*yūyaṃ prayāta pātālaṃ yudhyadhvaṃ śaktirasti cet \|*  
*iti krodhakaraṃ vākyaṃ śrutvocuste parasparam \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Descend to Pātāla all of you. If you have the power, fight.” On hearing her provocative words the Asuras said to one another.

## \|\| 9.47.63 \|\|

*abalāṃ manasi jñātvā na hanmo bhavatīṃ vayam \|*  
*atho sthiraihi pañcāsye yuddhecchā mānase'sti cet \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. “We do not kill you as you are a woman. But if you desire for battle be ready with your lion.”

## \|\| 9.47.64 \|\|

*teṣāmevaṃ vivadatāṃ kalahaḥ samavarddhata \|*  
*vavṛṣu samare bāṇā ubhayorddalayośśitāḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. While they spoke thus the fight ensued. The arrows rained in the battle., sharp arrows from either side.

## \|\| 9.47.65 \|\|

*evaṃ taiḥ samaraṃ kṛtvā līlayā parameśvarī \|*  
*jaghāna caṇḍamuṇḍābhyāṃ raktabījaṃ mahāsuram \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. Thus fighting with them sportively the goddess slew the Asura along with Caṇḍa and Muṇḍa.

## \|\| 9.47.66 \|\|

*dveṣabuddhiṃ vidhāyāpi tridaśasthitayo'pyamī \|*  
*ante'prāpanparaṃ lokaṃ yaṃllokaṃ yānti tajjanāḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Though they had an inimical feeling these enemies of the gods attained in the end that world which her followers go to.

## Chapter 48 - The manifestation of Sarasvatī

## \|\| 9.48.1-2 \|\|

*rājovāca \|*  
*dhūmrākṣaṃ caṇḍamuṇḍaṃ ca raktabījāsurantathā \|*  
*bhagavannihatandevyā śrutvā śumbhaḥ surārdanaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

*kimakārṣīttato brahmannetanme brūhi sāmpratam \|*  
*śuśrūṣave jagadyoneścaritraṃ pāpanāśanam \|\| 2 \|\|*

The king said:—

1-2. O holy lord, O brahmin, what did Śumbha, the suppressor of the gods, do on hearing that Dhūmrākṣa, Caṇḍa and Raktabīja were killed by the goddess? Please tell me this now. I desire to hear the purifying tale of the goddess, the cause of the universe.

## \|\| 9.48.3 \|\|

*ṛṣiruvāca \|*  
*hatānemāndaityavarānmahāsuro niśamya rājanmahanīyavikramaḥ \|*  
*ajijñapatsvīyagaṇāndurāsadānraṇābhidhoccāraṇajjātasaṃmadān \|\| 3 \|\|*

The sage said:—

3\. O king, on hearing that the leading Daityas had been killed, the great Asura of commendable valour ordered his invincible followers who were thrilled at the very mention of the war.

## \|\| 9.48.4 \|\|

*balānvitāssaṃmilitā mamājñayā jayāśayā kālakavaṃśasaṃbhavāḥ \|*  
*sakālakeyāsuramauryyadaurhṛdāstathā parepyāśu prayāṇayantu te \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. At my behest the Daityas born of the race of Kālaka\[1\] have all assembled here with their armies and along with Kālakeyas,\[2\] Mauryas,\[3\] Daurhṛdas and others. Let them march on with hopes of victory.

## \|\| 9.48.5 \|\|

*niśuṃbhaśuṃbhau ditijānnideśya tānrathādhirūḍhau nirayāṃ babhūvatuḥ \|*  
*balānyanūkhurbalinostayordharādvināśavantaḥ śalabhā ivotthitāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. After ordering the demons, Śumbha and Niśumbha mounted on their chariots and set out. Their armies too followed them like swarms of moths rising up from a mountain definitely for their destruction.

## \|\| 9.48.6 \|\|

*prasādayāmāsa mṛdaṃgamardalaṃ sabherikāḍiṇḍimajharjharānakam \|*  
*raṇasthale saṃjahṛṣū raṇapriyā asupriyāḥ saṃgarataḥ parāyayuḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Mṛdaṅgas, Mardalas, Bherikās, Diṇḍimas, Jharjharas and Ānakas were sounded. The warmongers rejoiced in the battle-ground. Those who wore afraid fled away for their life.

## \|\| 9.48.7 \|\|

*bhaṭāśca te yuddhapaṭāvṛtāstadā raṇasthalīṃ māpurapāpavigrahāḥ \|*  
*gṛhītaśastrāstracayā jigīṣayā parasparaṃ vigrahayanta ulbaṇam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Glad in their martial dress the soldiers came in their healthy spirits to the battleground. Holding various weapons and missiles they teased one another each eager for his victory.

## \|\| 9.48.8 \|\|

*gajādhirūḍhāsturagādhirohiṇo rathādhirūḍhāśca tathāpare'surāḥ \|*  
*alakṣayantaḥ svaparāñjanānmudā'sureśasaṃge samare'bhirebhire \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Soldiers on elephants, looked at the enemy with indifference. In the company of the lord of Asuras they rejoiced in the battle.

## \|\| 9.48.9 \|\|

*dhvaniḥ śataghnī janito muhurmuhurbabhūva tena tridaśāḥ samejitāḥ \|*  
*mahāndhakāraḥ samapadyatāmbare vilokyate no rathamaṇḍalaṃ raveḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The sound arising from the guns rose up repeatedly making the gods tremble. A great darkness enveloped the sky. Even the chariot of the sun was not visible.

## \|\| 9.48.10 \|\|

*padātayo nirva vajurhi koṭiśaḥ prabhūtamānā vijayābhilāṣiṇaḥ \|*  
*rathāśvagā vāraṇagā athāpare'surā nirīyuḥ kati koṭiśo mudā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Foot-soldiers set out in excessive numbers desirous of victory. Soldiers in chariots, on horsebacks, elephants and others set out joyously in crores and crores.

## \|\| 9.48.11 \|\|

*aśukla śailā eva mattavāraṇā atāniṣuścītkṛtiśabdamāhave \|*  
*kramelakāścāpi galadgaladhvaniṃ vitanvate kṣudramahīdharopamāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The rutting elephants like massive black mountains spread their trumpeting sound in the battle-field. Camels resembling small hills produced hoarse sounds from their throats.

## \|\| 9.48.12 \|\|

*hayāśca hreṣanta udagrabhūmijā viśālakaṇṭhābharaṇā gatervidaḥ \|*  
*padāni dantāvalamūrdhni bibhrataḥ suḍiḍyire vyomapathā yathā'vayaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Neighing horses hailing from exalted lands, with big ornaments round their necks, expert in the knowledge of their gaits planted their legs on the heads of elephants and flew like birds.

## \|\| 9.48.13 \|\|

*samīkṣya śatrorbalamitthamāpataccakāra sajyaṃ dhanurambikā tadā \|*  
*nanāda ghaṇṭāṃ ripusādadāyinī jagarja siṃho'pi saṭāṃ vidhūnayan \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. On seeing the army of the enemy advancing thus, Ambikā kept her bow well-strung. She sounded her bell that distressed the enemy. The lion too shook his manes and roared.

## \|\| 9.48.14 \|\|

*tato niśuṃbhastuhinācalasthitāṃ vilokya ramyābharaṇāyudhāṃ śivām \|*  
*giraṃ babhāṣe rasanirbharāṃ parāṃ vilāsanībhāvavicakṣaṇo yathā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Seeing her bedecked in fine ornaments, holding weapons and stationed on the Himalaya mountain Niśumbha spoke words full of sentiments like a man clever in understanding the emotions of beautiful women.

## \|\| 9.48.15 \|\|

*bhavādṛśīnāṃ ramaṇīyavigrahe dunoti kīrṇaṃ khalu mālatīdalam \|*  
*kathaṃ karālāhavamātanoṣyase maheśi tenaiva manojñavarṣmaṇā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. “Even a petal of the Mālatī (Jasmine) flower thrown on the beautiful body of women like you may distress you. O goddess, how will you carry on a terrible war with the self-same handsome body of yours?”

## \|\| 9.48.16 \|\|

*itīrayitvā vacanaṃ mahāsuro babhūva maunī tamuvāca caṃḍikā \|*  
*vṛthā kimātthāsura mūḍha saṃgaraṃ kuruṣva nāgālayamanyathā vraja \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. After saying thus the great Asura became silent. Caṇḍikā spoke to him—“O foolish Asura, why do you prattle in vain? Either fight or else return to Pātāla.

## \|\| 9.48.17 \|\|

*tatotiruṣṭaḥ samare mahārathaścakāra bāṇāvalivṛṣṭimadbhutām \|*  
*ghanāghanāḥ saṃvavṛṣuryathodakaṃ raṇasthale prāvṛḍivāgatā tadā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The heroic demon, becoming infuriated, made a wonderful shower of arrows on the battle-field just as masses of clouds shower water during rains.

## \|\| 9.48.18 \|\|

*śaraiśśitaiśśūlaparaśvadhāyudhaiḥ sabhindipālaiḥ parighaiśśarāsanaiḥ \|*  
*bhuśuṇḍikāprāsakṣuraprasaṃjñakairmahāsibhiḥ saṃyuyudhe madoddhataiḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Along with his arrogant followers the demon fought with sharp weapons as spears, axes, iron clubs, parighas, bows, Bhuśuṇḍikās, javelins, horseshoe-edged arrows and great swords.

## \|\| 9.48.19 \|\|

*vivabhramustatsamare mahāgajā vibhinnakuṃbhāasitādrisannibhāḥ \|*  
*caladbalākādhavalā viketavo visetavaḥ śuṃbhaniśuṃbhaketavaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. In that war great elephants looking like black mountains with foreheads pierced ran here and there. The banners of Śumbha and Niśumbha white like flying cranes fluttered here and there.

## \|\| 9.48.20 \|\|

*vibhinnadehā ditijā jhaṣopamā vikandharā vājigaṇā bhayaṃkarāḥ \|*  
*parāsavaḥ kālikayā kṛtā raṇe mṛgāriṇā cāśiṣatāpare'surā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The demons were shattered by Kālikā like fishes. The dreadful horses were beheaded and killed in the battle. The other demons were devoured by the lion.

## \|\| 9.48.21 \|\|

*visusruvū raktavahāstadantare saricca yāstatra vipupluve hataiḥ \|*  
*kacā bhaṭānāṃ jalanīlikopamāstaduttarīyaṃ sitaphenasaṃnibham \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. In the battlefield streams of blood flowed. The dead soldiers floated. Their tresses of hair resembled the moss. Their upper cloths resembled the white foam.

## \|\| 9.48.22 \|\|

*turaṃgasādī turagādhirohiṇaṃ gajasthitānabhyapatangajāruhaḥ \|*  
*rathī ratheśaṃ khalu pattiraṅghrigānsamapratidvandvikalirmahānabhūt \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. A great fight ensued where soldiers of equal rank fought with one another. The cavalrymen fought their counterpart; the elephant-riders with those on elephants: the charioteers with those on chariots and the footsoldiers with footsoldiers.

## \|\| 9.48.23 \|\|

*tato niśuṃbho hṛdaye vyacintayatkarālakāloyamupāgato'dhunā \|*  
*bhaveddaridro'pi mahādhano mahādhano daridro viparītakālataḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Then Niśumbha thought to himself—“A terrible period has set in now. Even a poor man may become rich and rich man poor if the time is adverse.

## \|\| 9.48.24 \|\|

*jaḍo bhavetsphītamatirmahāmatirjaḍo nṛśaṃso bahumantu saṃstutaḥ \|*  
*parājayaṃ yāti raṇe mahābalā jayaṃti saṃgrāmamukhe ca durbalāḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. A senseless fellow may become intelligent and an intelligent man dull. A wicked man may be praised by the noble. The great and the powerful may be vanquished. The weak may come out victorious in the war.

## \|\| 9.48.25 \|\|

*jayo'jayo vā parameśvarecchayā bhavatyanāyāsata eva dehinām \|*  
*na kālamullaṃghya śaśāka jīvituṃ maheśvaraḥ padmajanī ramāpatiḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Victory or defeat come naturally at the stance of the lord. Neither Śiva nor Brahmā nor Viṣṇu has been able to transgress his law.

## \|\| 9.48.26 \|\|

*upetya saṃgrāmamukhaṃ palāyanaṃ na sādhuvīrā hṛdaye'numanvate \|*  
*paraṃtu yuddhe kathametayā jayo vināśitaṃ me sakalaṃ balaṃ yathā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Heroes do not come to the battlefield for turning back. But how can I attain victory in a battle with this lady who has destroyed my entire host?

## \|\| 9.48.27 \|\|

*iyaṃ hi nūnaṃ surakarma sādhituṃ samāgatā daityabalaṃ ca bādhitum \|*  
*purāṇamūrtiḥ prakṛtiḥ parā śivā na laukikīyaṃ vanitā kadāpi vā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Indeed this lady is the ancient Prakṛti, the great Śivā come here to achieve the task of the gods and harass the army of the Daityas. She can never be an ordinary woman

## \|\| 9.48.28 \|\|

*vadho'pi nārīvihito'yaśaskaraḥ pragīyate yuddharasaṃ lilikṣubhiḥ \|*  
*tathāpyakṛtvā samaraṃ kathaṃ mukhaṃ pradarśayāmo'surarājasannidhau \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. It is inglorious to be killed by a woman or to kill a woman for those who desire to taste the pleasures of war. Still how shall we show our faces to the king of Asuras without fighting?

## \|\| 9.48.29 \|\|

*vicārayitveti mahāratho rathaṃ mahāntamadhyāsya niyantṛcoditam \|*  
*yayau drutaṃ yatra maheśvarāṃganā surāṃganāprārthitayauvanodgamā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. After thinking thus and sitting in a great chariot driven by a charioteer he hastened to the spot where the lady consort of Śiva was present; the goddess whose youth was sought after by the celestial damsels.

## \|\| 9.48.30 \|\|

*avocadenāṃ sa maheśi kiṃ bhavedebhirhatairvetanajīvibhirbhaṭaiḥ \|*  
*tavāsti kāṃkṣā yadi yoddhumāvayostadā raṇaḥ syāddhṛtayuddhasatpaṭaiḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. He addressed her thus—“O goddess, of what avail is it if the mercenary soldiers are killed? If you desire to fight, let both of us clad in martial dress fight with each other.

## \|\| 9.48.31 \|\|

*uvāca kālīṃ prati kauśikī tadā samīkṣyatāmeṣa durāgraho'nayoḥ \|*  
*karoti kālo vipadāgame matiṃ vibhinnavṛttiṃ sadasatpravartakaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The goddess addressed Kālī then—“See the foolish ambition of the two Asuras. Time the instigator of good and bad actions renders the mind work in a different way when adversity is imminent.”

## \|\| 9.48.32 \|\|

*tato niśuṃbho'bhijaghāna caṇḍikāṃ śaraissahasraiśca tathaiva kālikām \|*  
*bibheda bāṇānasurapracoditānsahasrakhaṇḍaṃ svaśarotkaraiḥ śivā \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Then Niśumbha attacked Caṇḍikā as well as Kālī with thousands of arrows. With the volleys of her arrows Śivā split into a thousand pieces the arrows discharged by the Asura.

## \|\| 9.48.33 \|\|

*tataḥ samutthāya kṛpāṇamujjvalaṃ sa carmma kaṇṭhīravamūrdhnyatāḍayat \|*  
*bibheda taṃ cāpi mahāsināmbikā yathā kuṭhāreṇa taruṃ taruśchidaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. He then lifted up his lustrous sword along with the shield and struck the lion on its head. With her great sword she split it too as the woodcutters do to a tree with the axe.

## \|\| 9.48.34 \|\|

*sa bhinnakhaḍgo nicakhāna mārgaṇaṃ parāmbikā vakṣasi so'pi cicchide \|*  
*punastriśūlaṃ hṛdaye'kṣipattadapyacūrṇa yanmuṣṭinipātanena sā \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. When the sword was split he thrust an arrow into her chest. The arrow too was cut. He then hurled the trident which powdered it with her fist.

## \|\| 9.48.35 \|\|

*gadāṃ samādāya punarmahāratho'bhyadhāvadambāṃ maraṇonmukho'suraḥ \|*  
*acūrṇayattāmapi śūladhārayā punastri śūlaṃ vidadāra so'nyayā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Prepared to die the heroic Asura seized a mace and rushed at her. She reduced the mace into powder with the edge of her trident. The demon shattered the trident with another mace.

## \|\| 9.48.36 \|\|

*tatombikā bhīmabhujaṃgamopamaissuradviṣāṃ śoṇitacūṣaṇocitaiḥ \|*  
*niśumbhamātmīyaśilīmukhai śśitairnihatya bhūmīmanayadviṣokṣitaiḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Then she struck Niśumbha with her sharp, terrible serpentine poisonous arrows that were accustomed to drink the blood of Asuras and brought him down to the earth.

## \|\| 9.48.37 \|\|

*nipātite'mānabale'suraprabhuḥ kanīyasi bhrātari roṣapūritaḥ \|*  
*rathasthito bāhubhiraṣṭa bhirvṛto jagāma yatra pramadā maheśituḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. When his younger brother of great honour and strength fell Śumbha was furious. The eight-armed demon seated himself in a chariot and came to the place where Śivā was present.

## \|\| 9.48.38 \|\|

*avādayacchaṃkhamarindamaṃ tadā dhanussvanaṃ cāpi cakāra duḥsaham \|*  
*nanāda siṃho'pi saṭāṃ vidhūnayanbabhūva nādatrayanāditannabhaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. She blew the conch Arindama, produced unbearable bow-twang; the lion shaking its manes roared. The whole sky reverberated with the threefold round.

## \|\| 9.48.39 \|\|

*tato'ṭṭahāsaṃ jagadambikā karodvitatrasustena surārayo'khilāḥ \|*  
*jayeti śabdaṃ jagadustadā surā yadāmbikovāca raṇe sthiro bhava \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Then the mother of the universe laughed boisterously making all the demons tremble with fear. The gods shouted cries of “Victory” when she challenged the Asuras in the battle.

## \|\| 9.48.40 \|\|

*daityarājo mahatīṃ jvalacchikhāṃ mumoca śaktiṃ nihatā ca solkayā \|*  
*bibheda śuṃbhaprahitāñcharācchivā śiveritānsopi sahasradhā śarān \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The king of Daityas hurled a spear of shining flames which was struck down by a meteor. Śivā shattered the arrows discharged by Śumbha. He too split the arrows discharged by Śivā into a thousand pieces.

## \|\| 9.48.41 \|\|

*triśūlamutkṣipya jaghāna caṇḍikā mahāsuraṃ taṃ sa papāta mūrcchitaḥ \|*  
*vibhinnapakṣo hariṇā yathā nagaḥ prakaṃpayan dyāṃ vasudhāṃ sa vāridhim \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. She lifted up her trident and struck the great Asura. He fell down unconscious shaking heaven and earth along with the ocean like a mountain whose wings had been chopped off by Indra.

## \|\| 9.48.42 \|\|

*tato mṛṣitvā triśikhodbhavāṃ vyathāṃ vidhāya bāhūnayutaṃ mahābalaḥ \|*  
*sa kālikāṃ siṃhayutāṃ maheśvarīṃ jaghāna cakrairamarakṣayaṃkaraiḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Suffering the pain arising from the trident, the powerful demon, the suppressor of the gods, created ten thousand hands and struck Kālī and Caṇḍikā along with the lion, by means of his discus.

## \|\| 9.48.43 \|\|

*tadastacakrāṇi vibhidya līlayā triśūlamudgūryya jaghāna sāsuram \|*  
*śivā jagatpāvanapāṇipaṅkajādupāttamṛtyū paramaṃ padaṃ gatau \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Splitting sportively the discus hurled by him, she hurt and struck the Asura with the trident. Thus both of them met their death at the lotus-hands of Śivā that sanctify the universe and attained the great region.

## \|\| 9.48.44 \|\|

*hate tasminmahāvīryye niśuṃbhe bhīmavikrame \|*  
*śuṃbhe ca sakalā daityā viviśurbalisadmani \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. When the powerful Niśumbha, and Śumbha of terrible exploit were killed the Daityas entered Pātāla.

## \|\| 9.48.45 \|\|

*bhakṣitā apare kālīsiṃhādyairamaradviṣaḥ \|*  
*palāyitāstathānye ca daśadikṣu bhayākulāḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Others were devoured by Kālī’s lion. The remaining Asuras being excessively frightened fled in the quarters.

## \|\| 9.48.46 \|\|

*babhūvurmārgavāhinyassaritaḥ svacchapāthasaḥ \|*  
*vavurvātāḥ sukhasparśā nirmalatvaṃ yayau nabhaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The rivers with clear water flowed along their paths; the winds blew very gentle to the touch; the sky became clear.

## \|\| 9.48.47 \|\|

*punaryāgaḥ samārebhe devairbrahmarṣibhistathā \|*  
*sukhinaścābhavansarve mahendrādyā divaukasaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Sacrifices were revived by the gods and the sages, Lord Indra and the gods felt blissful again.

## \|\| 9.48.48-49 \|\|

*pavitraṃ paramaṃ puṇyamumāyāścaritaṃ prabho \|*  
*daityarājavadhopetaṃ śraddhayā yaḥ samabhya set \|\| 48 \|\|*

*sa bhuktvehākhilānbhogāṃstridaśairapi durlabhān \|*  
*paratromālayaṃ gacchenmahāmāyāprasādataḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

48-49. O lord, this story of Umā is holy and meritorious. It describes the destruction of the king of Daityas. He who regularly reads this with faith enjoys all worldly pleasures inaccessible even to the gods and attains the abode of Umā hereafter by the very grace of the goddess.

## \|\| 9.48.50 \|\|

*\|\| ṛṣiruvāca \|*  
*evandevī samutpannā śuṃbhāsuranibarhiṇī \|*  
*proktā sarasvatī sākṣādumāṃśāvirbhavā nṛpa \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Thus was the goddess, slayer of Śumbha born. She is said to be Sarasvatī. O king, she has manifested herself as a part of Umā.

## Chapter 49 - The manifestation of Umā

## \|\| 9.49.1 \|\|

*munaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*umāyā bhuvaneśānyāssūta sarvārthavittama \|*  
*avatāraṃ samācakṣva yato jātā sarasvatī \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O Sūta, the most excellent among the omniscients please mention the incarnation of Umā, the goddess of all the worlds and the mother of Sarasvatī.

## \|\| 9.49.2 \|\|

*yā gīyate parabrahmamūlaprakṛtirīśvarī \|*  
*nirākārāpi sākārā nityā nandamathī satī \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. She is sung as the primordial Prakṛti of the supreme Brahman. She is both possessed and devoid of forms. She is of the form of eternal bliss. She is Satī.

## \|\| 9.49.3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tāpasāḥ śṛṇuta premṇā caritraṃ paramaṃ mahat \|*  
*yasya vijñānamātreṇa naro yāti parāṃ gatim \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. O sages, listen lovingly to the great story, the perfect knowledge of which alone helps man to attain the great goal.

## \|\| 9.49.4 \|\|

*devadānavayoryuddhamekadāsītparasparam \|*  
*mahāmāyāprabhāveṇāmarāṇāṃ vijayo'bhavat \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Once there was a clash between the gods and the Asuras. Thanks to the power of the goddess the gods became victorious.

## \|\| 9.49.5 \|\|

*tato'valiptā amarāssvapraśaṃsāṃ vitenire \|*  
*vayaṃ dhanyā vayaṃ dhanyā kiṃ kariṣyaṃti no'surāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Then the gods were proud and haughty. They boasted—“We are blessed. What can the Asuras do to us

## \|\| 9.49.6 \|\|

*ye prabhāvaṃ samālokyāsmākaṃ paramaduḥsaham \|*  
*bhītā nāgālayaṃ yātā yātayāteti vādinaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Of our unbearable prowess, the Asuras are afraid. They have gone to Pātāla saying, “Run, run”.

## \|\| 9.49.7 \|\|

*aho balamaho tejo daityavaṃśakṣayaṃkaram \|*  
*aho bhāgyaṃ sumanasāmevaṃ sarve'bhyavarṇayan \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Then all of them proclaimed thus: “Wonderful is the strength, splendour and good luck of the gods that has reduced the race of the Daityas.

## \|\| 9.49.8 \|\|

*tata āvirabhūttejaḥ kūṭarūpantadaiva hi \|*  
*adṛṣṭapūrvaṃ taddṛṣṭvā vismitā abhavansurāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Then a mass of splendour appeared, at the unprecedented sight of which the gods were surprised.

## \|\| 9.49.9 \|\|

*kimidaṃ kimidaṃ ceti ruddhakaṇṭhāssamabruvana \|*  
*ajānantaḥ paraṃ śyāmānu bhāvaṃ mānabhañjanam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Ignorant of the real glory and the intention of the goddess to quell their arrogance, the Asuras said with choking throats “What is this? What is this?”

## \|\| 9.49.10 \|\|

*tata ājñāpayaddevāndevānāmadhināyakaḥ \|*  
*yāta yūyaṃ parīkṣadhvaṃ yāthātathyena kinviti \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Then the lord Indra ordered the gods—“Go ye all and examine it and ascertain what it is.”

## \|\| 9.49.11 \|\|

*surendraprerito vāyurmahasaḥ sannidhiṃ gataḥ \|*  
*kastvaṃ bhoriti sambodhyāvocadenaṃ ca tanmahaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Urged by Indra, the wind-god approached splendour and addressed it, “Who are you?” The splendour too put the same question to him.

## \|\| 9.49.12 \|\|

*iti pṛṣṭastadā vāyurmahasātigarīyasā \|*  
*vāyurasmi jagatprāṇassābhimāno'bravīdidam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Thus asked by the big mass of splendour the wind-god replied arrogantly.—“I am Vāyu the vital breath of the universe.”

## \|\| 9.49.13 \|\|

*jaṃgamājaṃgamaṃ sarvamotaprotamidaṃ jagat \|*  
*mayyeva nikhilādhāre cālayāmyakhilaṃ jagat \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Everything in the universe, mobile or immobile, is woven like warp and woof into me. I am the support of everything. I move the entire universe.”

## \|\| 9.49.14 \|\|

*tadovāca mahātejaḥ śakto'si yadi cālane \|*  
*dhṛtametattṛṇaṃ vāyo cālayasva nijecchayā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Then the great splendour said:—“If you are competent to move, O wind-god, please move this blade of grass I have set before you. Move it as you please.”

## \|\| 9.49.15 \|\|

*tataḥ sarvaprayatnenākarodyatnaṃ sadāgatiḥ \|*  
*na cacāla yadā sthānāttadāsau lajjito'bhavata \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Then wind-god put forth all his efforts. But the blade of grass did not stir from its position. Then he was put to shame.

## \|\| 9.49.16 \|\|

*tūṣṇīṃ bhūtvā tato vāyurjagāmendraṃ sabhāṃ prati \|*  
*kathayāmāsa tad vṛttaṃ svakīyābhibhavānvitam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The wind-god went silently to the assembly of Indra. There he narrated the details of his discomfiture.

## \|\| 9.49.17 \|\|

*sarveśatvaṃ vayaṃ sarve mṛṣaivātmani manmahe \|*  
*na pārayāmahe kiṃcidvi dhātuṃ kṣudravastvapi \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. “False is our pride that we are the lords. In fact we are helpless, insignificant creatures.”

## \|\| 9.49.18 \|\|

*tataśca preṣayāmāsa marutvānsakalānsurān \|*  
*na śekuste yadā jñātuṃ tadendraḥ svayamabhyagāt \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Then Indra sent all the gods. When they could not realise anything Indra himself went there.

## \|\| 9.49.19 \|\|

*maghavantamathāyāntaṃ dṛṣṭvā tejotiduḥsaham \|*  
*babhūvāntarhitaṃ sadyo vismito'bhūcca vāsavaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. On seeing Indra come, the unbearable splendour vanished immediately. Indra was surprised.

## \|\| 9.49.20 \|\|

*caritramīdṛśaṃ yasya tameva śaraṇaṃ śraye \|*  
*iti saṃcintayāmāsa sahasrākṣaḥ punaḥpunaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Then Indra thought to himself again and again—“I seek refuge in him alone whose conduct is such as this.”

## \|\| 9.49.21-22 \|\|

*etasminnaṃtare tatra nirvyājakaruṇātanuḥ \|*  
*teṣāmanugrahaṃ kartuṃ hartuṃ garvaṃ śivāṃganā \|\| 21 \|\|*

*caitraśuklanavamyāṃ tu madhyāhnasthe divākare \|*  
*prādurāsīdumā devī saccidānandarūpiṇī \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. In the meantime, in order to bless them and remove their arrogance, Śivā, the embodied form of undr ceitful merry, existence, knowledge and bliss, manifested herself on the ninth day of the bright half of the month Caitra when the sun was in the middle of the day.

## \|\| 9.49.23 \|\|

*mahomadhye virājantī bhāsayantī diśo rucā \|*  
*bodhayantī surānsarvānbrahmaivāhamiti sphuṭam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Shining among the mass of splendour she brightened the quarters with her brilliance. She enlightened the gods, saying “I alone am Brahman.”

## \|\| 9.49.24 \|\|

*caturbhirdadhatī hastairvarapāśāṃkuśābhayān \|*  
*śrutibhissevitā ramyā navayauvanagarvitā \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. In her four hands she held boons, noose, goad and the mystic gesture of protection. She was served by the Vedas and looked beautiful and proud of her blooming youth.

## \|\| 9.49.25 \|\|

*raktāmbaraparīdhānā raktamālyānulepanā \|*  
*koṭikaṃdarppasaṃkāśā candrakoṭisamaprabhā \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. She wore red garments and red garlands. Red sandal paste was smeared over her body. She was as dazzling as a crore of cupids. Her lustre wṅs that of a crorc moons.

## \|\| 9.49.26 \|\|

*vyājahāra mahāmāyā sarvāntaryyāmirūpiṇī \|*  
*sākṣiṇī sarvabhūtānāṃ parabrahmasvarūpiṇī \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. The great Māyā, in the form of the immanent soul of all, the cosmic witness of all living beings, and the great Brahman, spoke.

## \|\| 9.49.27 \|\|

*umovāca \|*  
*na brahmā na surārātirna purārātirīśvaraḥ \|*  
*madagre garvituṃ kiṃcitkā kathānyasuparvaṇām \|\| 27 \|\|*

Umā said:—

27\. Neither Brahma nor Viṣṇu nor Śiva the slayer of the Tripura demon can bluff before me. What about the other gods?

## \|\| 9.49.28 \|\|

*paraṃ brahma paraṃ jyotiḥ praṇavadvandvarūpiṇī \|*  
*ahamevāsmi sakalaṃ madanyo nāsti kaścana \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The great Brahman, the great light in the form of the two Praṇavas, I alone am. I am all. There is none other beside me.

## \|\| 9.49.29 \|\|

*nirākārāpi sākārā sarvatattvasvarūpiṇī \|*  
*apratarkyaguṇā nityā kāryakāraṇarūpiṇī \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Though devoid of form I possess forms. I constitute all principles. I am eternal and my attributes cannot be disputed. I am both the cause and the effect.

## \|\| 9.49.30 \|\|

*kadāciddayitākārā kadācitpuruṣākṛtiḥ \|*  
*kadācidubhayākārā sarvākārāhamīśvarī \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Sometimes I have the form of a woman, sometimes of a man. Sometimes both. I am goddess assuming all forms.

## \|\| 9.49.31 \|\|

*virañciḥ sṛṣṭikartāhaṃ jagatpātāhamacyutaḥ \|*  
*rudraḥ saṃhārakartāhaṃ sarvaviśvavimohinī \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. I am the creator, the protector and the destroyer of the world. I am the enchantress of the universe.

## \|\| 9.49.32 \|\|

*kālikā kamalāvāṇī mukhāssarvā hi śaktayaḥ \|*  
*madaṃśādeva saṃjātāstathemāssakalāḥ kalāḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. All the Śaktis—Kālī, Lakṣmī and Sarasvatī as well as others are born of my parts. So also the arts originate from me.

## \|\| 9.49.33 \|\|

*matprabhāvājjitāssarve yuṣmābhirdditinandanāḥ \|*  
*tāmavijñāya māṃ yūyaṃ vṛthā sarveśamāninaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Due to my power alone the demons are conquered by you. Without knowing me of this nature you bluff as the lords of all.

## \|\| 9.49.34 \|\|

*yathā dārumayīṃ yoṣāṃ nartayatyaindrajālikaḥ \|*  
*tathaiva sarvabhūtāni nartayāmyahamīśvarī \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Just as a magician makes the wooden doll of a woman dance so also I the goddess make all living beings dance.

## \|\| 9.49.35 \|\|

*madbhayādvāti pavanaḥ sarvaṃ dahati havyabhuk \|*  
*lokapālāḥ prakurvaṃti svasvakarmāṇyanāratam \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Being afraid of me the wind blows, the fire-god burns and the guardians of the quarters carry on their duties.

## \|\| 9.49.36 \|\|

*kadāciddevavargāṇāṃ kadācidditijanma nām \|*  
*karomi vijayaṃ samyaksvatantrā nijalīlayā \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Independent that I am, I sportively bestow victory on the gods sometimes and on the Daityas sometimes.

## \|\| 9.49.37 \|\|

*avināśi paraṃ dhāma māyātītaṃ parātparam \|*  
*śrutayo varṇayante yatta drūpantu mamaiva hi \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. It is my form that is described in the Vedas as indestructible, the great abode, the one beyond Māyā and that which is greater than the greatest.

## \|\| 9.49.38 \|\|

*saguṇaṃ nirguṇaṃ ceti madrūpaṃ dvividhaṃ matam \|*  
*māyāśabalitaṃ caikaṃ dvitīyantadanāśritam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. My form is two-fold—Saguṇa and Nirguṇa. One is mixed with Māyā and the other is free from it.

## \|\| 9.49.39 \|\|

*evaṃ vijñāya māṃ devāssvaṃ svaṃ garvaṃ vihāya ca \|*  
*bhajata praṇayopetāḥ prakṛtiṃ māṃ sanātanīm \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O gods, endowed with devotion, realise this and eschew your arrogance, worship me, the eternal Prakṛti.

## \|\| 9.49.40 \|\|

*iti devyā vacaḥ śrutvā karuṇāgarbhitaṃ surāḥ \|*  
*tuṣṭuvuḥ parameśānīṃ bhaktisaṃnatakandharāḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. On hearing the merciful words of the goddess, the gods eulogised her, with their shoulders drooping with devotion.

## \|\| 9.49.41 \|\|

*kṣamasva jagadīśāni prasīda parameśvari \|*  
*maivaṃ bhūyātkadācinno garvo mātarddayāṃ kuru \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. O goddess of the universe, forgive us. O goddess, he pleased. O mother, let us not be arrogant again. Please be merciful.

## \|\| 9.49.42 \|\|

*tataḥprabhṛti te daivā hitvā garvaṃ samāhitāḥ \|*  
*umāmārādhayāmāsuryathāpūrvaṃ yathāvidhi \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Since then the gods began to propitiate her as before. They eschewed their arrogance and became pure in mind.

## \|\| 9.49.43 \|\|

*iti vaḥ kathito viprā umāprādurbhavo mayā \|*  
*yasya śravaṇamātreṇa paramaṃ padamaśnute \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. O Brahmins, thus I have narrated to you the manifestation of Umā. By only hearing this men attain the great region.

## Chapter 50 - The incarnation of Śatākṣī etc.

## \|\| 9.50.1 \|\|

*munaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*śrotukāmā vayaṃ sarve durgācaritamanvaham \|*  
*aparaṃ ca mahāprājña tattvaṃ varṇaya no'dbhutam \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O intelligent one, all of us are anxious to hear the episode of Durgā every day. Please tell us another wonderful tale of the goddess.

## \|\| 9.50.2 \|\|

*śṛṇvatāntvanmukhāmbhojātkathā nānā sudhopamāḥ \|*  
*na tṛpyati mano'smākaṃ sūta sarvārthavittama \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. O Sūta, the most excellent of the story-tellers, we are not satiated even as we hear different nectarine stories narrated by you.

## \|\| 9.50.3 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*durgamaḥ prathito nāmnā ruruputro mahābalaḥ \|*  
*brahmaṇo varadānena catasro'labhata śrutīḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. The most powerful son of Ruru, famous as Durgama secured the four Vedas as a result of the boon granted by Brahmā.

## \|\| 9.50.4 \|\|

*devājeyabalaṃ cāpi saṃprāpya jagatītale \|*  
*karoti sma bahūtpātāndivi devāścakampire \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Securing this strength that cannot be thwarted even by the gods he wrought havoc on the earth, making the gods tremble in heaven.

## \|\| 9.50.5 \|\|

*sarvā naṣṭeṣu vedeṣu kriyā naṣṭā babhūva ha \|*  
*brāhmaṇāśca durācārā babhūbussasurāstadā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. When the Vedas were destroyed the sacred rites were ruined. Brahmins and the gods fell off from their right conduct.

## \|\| 9.50.6 \|\|

*na dānaṃ na tapo'tyugraṃ na yāgo havanaṃ na hi \|*  
*anāvṛṣṭistato jātā pṛthivyāṃ śatavārṣikī \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. None offered charitable gifts; none performed penances; there was neither worship nor sacrifice. Then a great drought befell the earth extending to a period of a hundred years.

## \|\| 9.50.7 \|\|

*hāhākāro mahānāsīttriṣu lokeṣu duḥkhitāḥ \|*  
*abhavaṃśca janāssarve kṣuttṛḍbhyāṃ pīḍitā bhṛśam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. There was a great hue and cry in all the three worlds. The people felt miserable being afflicted by hunger and thirst.

## \|\| 9.50.8 \|\|

*saritaḥ sāgarāścaiva vāpīkūpasarāṃsi ca \|*  
*nirjalā abhavansarve saṃśuṣkā vṛkṣavīrudhaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Rivers, oceans, tanks, wells and lakes dried. Trees and plants withered.

## \|\| 9.50.9 \|\|

*tato dṛṣṭvā madādduḥkhaṃ prajānāṃ dīnacetasām \|*  
*tridaśāśśaraṇaṃ yātā yogamāyāṃ maheśvarīm \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. On seeing the misery of the distressed subjects the gods sought refuge in the Yogic Māyā, Maheśvarī.

The gods said—

## \|\| 9.50.10 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*rakṣarakṣa mahāmāye svakīyāssakalāḥ prajāḥ \|*  
*kopaṃ saṃhara nūnantvaṃ lokā naṃkṣyaṃti vānyathā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O Mahāmāyā, save all your people. Check your fury or the worlds will be ruined.

## \|\| 9.50.11-12 \|\|

*kathā śuṃbhohato daityo niśuṃbhaśca mahābalaḥ \|*  
*dhūmrākṣaścaṇḍamuṇḍau ca raktabījo mahābalaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

*sa madhuḥ kaiṭabho daityo mahiṣāsura eva ca \|*  
*tathaivāmuṃ kṛpāsindho dīnabandho jahi drutam \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. O ocean of mercy, O supportress of the distressed, kill this demon too just as you have killed Śumbha, Niśumbha. Dhūmrākṣa, Caṇḍa, Muṇḍa, Raktabīja, Madhu, Kaiṭabha and Mahiṣa.

## \|\| 9.50.13 \|\|

*aparādho bhavatyeva bālakānāṃ pade pade \|*  
*sahate ko jano loke kevalaṃ mātaraṃ vinā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Boys are guilty of offence at every step but who on earth brooks it except the mother?

## \|\| 9.50.14 \|\|

*yadāyadābhavadduḥkha devānāṃ brahmaṇāntathā \|*  
*tadātadāvatīryāśu kuruṣe sukhino janān \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. When miseries afflict the gods and the brahmins you incarnate for the welfare of the people.

## \|\| 9.50.15 \|\|

*iti viklavitanteṣāṃ samākarṇya kṛpāmayī \|*  
*anantākṣamayaṃ rūpandarśayāmāsa sāmpratam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. On hearing the distress of the gods the merciful goddess revealed to them her form possessed of many eyes.

## \|\| 9.50.16 \|\|

*dhanurbāṇau tathā padma nānāmūlaphalāni ca \|*  
*caturbhirdadhatī hastaiḥ prasannamukhapaṅkajā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The goddess with her lotus-face beaming with delight held in her four hands the bow, the arrows, the lotus and different fruits and roots.

## \|\| 9.50.17 \|\|

*tato dṛṣṭvā prajāstaptāḥ karuṇāpūritekṣaṇā \|*  
*ruroda nava ghasrāṇi nava rātrīssamākulā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. On seeing her people scorched and distressed, the goddess was dejected. With her eyes full of mercy she cried for nine days and nights.

## \|\| 9.50.18 \|\|

*mocayāmāsa dṛṣṭibhyo vāridhārāḥ sahasraśaḥ \|*  
*tābhiḥ pratarppitā lokā auṣadhyaḥ sakalā api \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The tears took the forms of thousands of water-currents released from her eyes. The worlds, the plants and medicinal herbs were delighted by them.

## \|\| 9.50.19 \|\|

*agādhatoyāssarito babhūvuḥ sāgarā api \|*  
*ruruhurdharaṇīpṛṣṭhe śākamūlaphalāni ca \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. They turned into the rivers and oceans of deep waters. Vegetables, roots and fruits grew on the earth.

## \|\| 9.50.20 \|\|

*vitatāra karasthāni sumanobhyaḥ phalāni ca \|*  
*gobhyastṛṇāni ramyāṇi tathānyebhyo yathārhataḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. To the learned and the gods she distributed the fruits in her hands. To the cows she gave the tender blades of grass and to others the things they deserved.

## \|\| 9.50.21 \|\|

*santuṣṭā abhavansarve sadaiva dvijamānuṣāḥ \|*  
*tato jagāda sā devī kimanyatkaravāṇi vaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. All including the gods, brahmins and men were satisfied. Then the goddess said—“What else shall I do for you?”

## \|\| 9.50.22 \|\|

*sametyocustadā devā bhavatyā toṣitā janāḥ \|*  
*vedāndehi kṛpāṃ kṛtvā durgameṇa samāhṛtām \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The gods then gathered together and said, “The people have been rendered happy and satisfied. O goddess, be pleased and redeem the Vedas taken by Durgama.”

## \|\| 9.50.23 \|\|

*tathāstviti prabhāṣyāha yātayāta nijālayam \|*  
*vitariṣyāmi vo vedānacireṇaiva kālataḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. After saying “So be it” she said. “Go ye all to your abodes. Ere long I shall give you the Vedas.”

## \|\| 9.50.24 \|\|

*tataḥ pramuditā devāssvaṃ svaṃ dhāma samāyayuḥ \|*  
*supraṇamya jagadyoniṃ phullendīvaralocanām \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Then the joyous gods returned to their abodes after bowing to the goddess the cause of the universe with her eyes that resembled full-blown blue lotus.

## \|\| 9.50.25 \|\|

*tataḥ kolāhalo jāto divi bhūmyantarikṣake \|*  
*tacchrutvā rauravaḥ sadyo nyaruṇatsarvataḥ purīm \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Then a great bustle arose in the three regions heaven, earth and sky. On hearing it the son of Ruru besieged the city immediately.

## \|\| 9.50.26 \|\|

*tatastejomayaṃ cakraṃ vidhāya paritaḥ śivā \|*  
*rakṣaṇārthaṃ devatānāṃ svayaṃ tasmādbahirgatā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Then after creating a big circle of splendour all round for the protection of the gods, Śivā herself came out of it.

## \|\| 9.50.27 \|\|

*tataḥ samabhavayuddhaṃ devyā daityasya cobhayoḥ \|*  
*vavṛṣuḥ samare bāṇānniśitānkaṃkaṭacchidaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. A great battle ensued between the goddess and the Daitya. Both showered arrows in the battle, piercing the armour of each other.

## \|\| 9.50.28-29 \|\|

*etasminnaṃtare tasyāḥ śarīrādramyamūrttayaḥ \|*  
*kālī tārā cchinnamastā śrīvidyā bhuvaneśvarī \|\| 28 \|\|*

*bhairavī bagalā dhūmra śrīmattripurasudarī \|*  
*mātaṃgī ca mahāvidyā nirgatā daśa sāyudhāḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. In the meantime ten beautiful forms came out of Kālī Viz Tārā, Chinnamastā, Śrīvidyā, Bhuvaneśvarī, Bhairavī, Bagalā, Dhūmrā, Śrīmattripurasundarī, Mātaṅgī and Mahāvidyā with weapons in their hands.

## \|\| 9.50.30 \|\|

*asaṃkhyātāstato jātā mātaro divyamūrttayaḥ \|*  
*candralekhādharāssarvāssarvā vidyutatsamaprabhāḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Then appeared innumerable mothers of divine forms, wearing the digit of the moon and with a lustre resembling the splendour of lightning.

## \|\| 9.50.31 \|\|

*tato mātṛgaṇairyuddhaṃ prāvartata bhayaṃkaram \|*  
*rauravīyaṃ hatantābhirdalamakṣauhiṇīśatam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Then a terrible combat ensued between the Asuras and the mothers. The army of the son of Ruru, consisting of a hundred Akṣauhiṇīs was killed in the battle.

## \|\| 9.50.32 \|\|

*jaghāna sā tadā daityaṃ durgamaṃ śūladhārayā \|*  
*papāta dharaṇīpṛṣṭhe khātamūladrumo yathā \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Then the goddess struck Durgama with the edge of her trident. He fell on the earth like an uprooted tree.

## \|\| 9.50.33 \|\|

*itthaṃ hatvā tadā daityaṃ durgamāsuranāma kam \|*  
*ādāya caturo vedāndadau devebhya īśvarī \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Thus after killing him the goddess redeemed the four Vedas and gave them to the gods.

## \|\| 9.50.34 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*asmadarthaṃ tvayā rūpamanantākṣimayaṃ dhṛtam \|*  
*munayaḥ kīrtayiṣyanti śatākṣīntvāmato'mbike \|\| 34 \|\|*

The gods said:—

34\. O Ambikā, for our welfare that form of infinite eyes was assumed by you. Hence sages will glorify you as Śatākṣī.

## \|\| 9.50.35 \|\|

*ātmadehasamudbhūtaiḥ śākairlokā mṛtā yataḥ \|*  
*śākaṃbharīti vikhyātantatte nāma bhaviṣyati \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Since the worlds were sustained by you with vegetables sprouting from your body, you will be famous as Śākambharī.

## \|\| 9.50.36 \|\|

*durgamākhyo mahādaityo hato yasmāttataḥ śive \|*  
*durgāṃ bhagavatīṃ bhadrāṃ vyāhariṣyaṃti mānavāḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. O Śivā, since you killed the Daitya Durgama you will be known as Durgā.

## \|\| 9.50.37 \|\|

*yoganidre namastubhyaṃ namaste'stu mahābale \|*  
*namo jñānaprade tubhyaṃ viśvamātre namo namaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. O Yogic slumber, O Mahābalā, O bestower of knowledge, O the mother of the universe, Obeisance be to you.

## \|\| 9.50.38 \|\|

*tattvamasyādivākyairyā bodhyate parameśvarī \|*  
*anantakoṭibrahmāṇḍanāyikāyai namo namaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Obeisance to the heroine of infinite universes known as the supreme goddess in the Mahāvākyas as Tattvamasi.

## \|\| 9.50.39 \|\|

*vāṅmanaḥkāyaduṣprāpāṃ sūryacandrāgnilocanām \|*  
*stotuṃ na śaknumo mātastva tprabhāvābudhā vayam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O Mother, we who are unaware oi your power and greatness, cannot adequately eulogise you who are inaccessible through words, mind or body and whose eyes are the sun and the moon.

## \|\| 9.50.40 \|\|

*mādṛśānamarāndṛṣṭvā kaḥ kuryādīdṛśīndayām \|*  
*varjayitvā sureśānīṃ śatākṣī mātaraṃ vinā \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Who else will bestow mercy on seeing us the gods except the great goddess Śatākṣī the mother?

## \|\| 9.50.41 \|\|

*trilokī nābhibhūyeta bādhābhiśca nirantaram \|*  
*evaṃ kāryastvayā yatno'smākaṃ vairivināśanam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Effort shall be made by you so that the three worlds will not be pestered continuously with obstacles and our enemies shall be destroyed.

## \|\| 9.50.42 \|\|

*devyuvāca \|*  
*vatsāndṛṣṭvā yathā gāvo vyagrā dhāvanti satvaram \|*  
*tathaiva bhavato dṛṣṭvā dhāvāmi vyākulā satī \|\| 42 \|\|*

The gods said:—

42\. Just as the cows run about in excitement quickly on seeing their calves in distress, so also I, Satī, am excited on seeing you in distress and run about.

## \|\| 9.50.43 \|\|

*mama yuṣmānapaśyantyā paśyantyā bālakāniva \|*  
*api prāṇānprayacchantyāḥ kṣaṇa eko yugāyate \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Even a moment appears to be a Yuga if I do not see you since I look at you as my own children for whom I am ready even to lay down my life.

## \|\| 9.50.44 \|\|

*kāpi cintā na karttavyā yuṣmābhirbha ktiśālibhiḥ \|*  
*bhavatyāṃ mayi tiṣṭhantyāṃ saṃharantyāṃ nijāpadaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. No worry or anxious thought need be entertained by you endowed with devotion as long as I stand by you destroying your distress.

## \|\| 9.50.45 \|\|

*yathā pūrvaṃ hatā daityā haniṣyāmi tathā'surān \|*  
*saṃśayo nātra karttavyassatyaṃsatyaṃ bravīmyaham \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. In the manner I have killed the Daityas I shall kill the Asuras. You need not entertain any doubt in this regard. Truth, I am speaking the truth.

## \|\| 9.50.46-47 \|\|

*yadā śuṃbho niśuṃbhaścāparau daityau bhaviṣyataḥ \|*  
*tadāhaṃ nandabhāryāyāṃ yaśodāyāṃ yaśomayī \|\| 46 \|\|*

*yonijaṃ rūpamāsthāya janiṣye gopagokule \|*  
*haniṣyāmyasurau tanmāṃ vyāhariṣyanti nandajām \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. I shall be born of the womb in the family of cowherds as the daughter of Yaśodā wife of Nandā, when Śumbha and Niśumbha will be born as Daityas. I shall kill the demons and become famous. Men will therefore call me Nandajā.

## \|\| 9.50.48 \|\|

*bhrāmaraṃ rūpamāsthāya vadhiṣyāmyaruṇaṃ yataḥ \|*  
*bhrāmarīti ca māṃ loke kīrtayiṣyanti mānavāḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Since I shall be taking the form of a bee and killing the demon Aruṇa, men will glorify me as Bhrāmarī.

## \|\| 9.50.49 \|\|

*kṛtvā bhīmaṃ punā rūpaṃ rakṣāṃsyatsyāmyahaṃ yadā \|*  
*bhīmā devīti vikhyātaṃ tanme nāma bhaviṣyati \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Again I shall take a terrible form and devour the Rākṣasas. I shall then be famous as Bhīmā Devī.

## \|\| 9.50.50 \|\|

*yadāyadā'surotthaiva bādhā bhuvi bhaviṣyati \|*  
*tadātadāvatīryāhaṃ śaṃ kariṣyāmyasaṃśayam \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Whenever there is harassment caused by the Asuras I shall surely incarnate and work for your welfare.

## \|\| 9.50.51 \|\|

*yā śatākṣī smṛtā devī saiva śākaṃbharī matā \|*  
*saiva prakīrtitā durgā vyaktirekaiva triṣvapi \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. The goddess Śatākṣī, Śākambharī and Durgā are identical. In all the three the individual is the same.

## \|\| 9.50.52 \|\|

*na śatākṣīsamā kāciddayālurbhuvi devatā \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā'rudatprajāstaptā yā navāhaṃ maheśvarī \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. There is no deity so compassionate as Śatākṣī the great goddess who cried for nine days on seeing her people scorched and distressed.

## Chapter 51 - Review of holy rites

## \|\| 9.51.1 \|\|

*munaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*vyāsaśiṣya mahābhāga sūta paurāṇikottama \|*  
*aparaṃ śrotumicchāmaḥ kimapyākhyānamīśituḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O blessed disciple of Vyāsa, O Sūta the most excellent among the knowers of the Purāṇas, we wish to hear some other story of the lord.

## \|\| 9.51.2 \|\|

*umāyā jagadambāyāḥ kriyāyogamanuttamam \|*  
*proktaṃ sanatkumāreṇa vyāsāya ca mahātmane \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. We wish to hear the most excellent sacred rites of Umā, the mother of the universe, those which were narrated to Vyāsa the great Ātman by Sanatkumāra

## \|\| 9.51.3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*dhanyā yūyaṃ mahātmāno devībhaktidṛḍhavratāḥ \|*  
*parāśakteḥ paraṃ guptaṃ rahasyaṃ śṛṇutādarāt \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. All of you are blessed great souls; steady in your devotional rites to the goddess. Now listen devotedly to the well-guarded secret of the great Śakti.

## \|\| 9.51.4 \|\|

*vyāsa uvāca \|*  
*sanatkumāra sarvajña brahmaputra mahāmate \|*  
*umāyāśśrotumicchāmi kriyāyogaṃ mahādbhutam \|\| 4 \|\|*

Vyāsa said:—

4\. O omniscient Sanatkumāra, O intelligent son of Brahmā, I wish to hear the wonderful holy rites of Umā.

## \|\| 9.51.5 \|\|

*kīdṛkca lakṣaṇaṃ tasya kiṃ kṛte ca phalaṃ bhavet \|*  
*priyaṃ yacca parāmbāyāstadaśeṣaṃ vadasva me \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Please tell me what is pleasing to the great mother. What are its characteristics and on doing what can it be efficacious?

## \|\| 9.51.6 \|\|

*sanatkumāra uvāca \|*  
*dvaipāyana yadetattvaṃ rahasyaṃ paripṛcchasi \|*  
*tacchṛṇuṣva mahābuddhe sarvaṃ me varṇayiṣyataḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

Sanatkumāra said:—

6\. O Dvaipāyana of great intellect, listen to the full description I am going to give of the secret that you ask.

## \|\| 9.51.7 \|\|

*jñānayogaḥ kriyāyogo bhaktiyogastathaiva ca \|*  
*trayo mārgāssamākhyātāḥ śrīmāturmuktimuktidā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Three paths of the glorious mother have been narrated which yield both worldly pleasures and salvation. They are paths of knowledge, holy rites and devotion.

## \|\| 9.51.8 \|\|

*jñānayogastu saṃyogaścittasyaivātmanā tu yaḥ \|*  
*yastu bāhyārthasaṃyogaḥ kriyāyogaḥ sa ucyate \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Jñānayoga is the union of the mind with the Ātman. The union with the external objects is called Kriyāyoga.

## \|\| 9.51.9 \|\|

*bhaktiyogo mato devyā ātmanaścaikyabhāvanam \|*  
*trayāṇāmapi yogānāṃ kriyāyogassa ucyate \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Bhaktiyoga is the concept of the unity of the Ātman with the goddess. Of the three I mention Kriyāyoga in detail.

## \|\| 9.51.10 \|\|

*karmaṇā jāyate bhaktirbhaktyā jñānaṃ prajāyate \|*  
*jñānātprajāyate muktiriti śāstreṣu niścayaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. It is concluded in the sacred scriptures that devotion is generated by activities, knowledge is born of devotion, liberation is the result of knowledge.

## \|\| 9.51.11 \|\|

*pradhānaṃ kāraṇaṃ yogo vimuktermunisattama \|*  
*kriyāyogastu yogasya paramandhyeyasādhanam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. O excellent sage, the chief reason of liberation is the Yoga. Kriyāyoga is the greatest means of liberation.

## \|\| 9.51.12 \|\|

*māyāntu prakṛtiṃ vidyānmāyāvi brahma śāśvatam \|*  
*abhinnaṃ tadvapurjñātvā mucyate bhavabandhanāt \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. One shall know the Māyā as Prakṛti and the wielder of Māyā as the eternal Brahman. After realising the identity of the two one is released from the world by bondage.

## \|\| 9.51.13-14 \|\|

*yastu devyālayaṃ kuryātpāṣāṇandāravantathā \|*  
*mṛnmayaṃ vātha kāleya tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu \|*  
*ahanyahaniyogena jayato yanmahāphalam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*prāpnoti tatphalandevyā yaḥ kārayati mandiram \|*  
*sahasrakulamāgāmi vyatītaṃ ca sahasrakam \|*  
*tārayati dharmātmā śrīmāturdhāma kārayan \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. O Vyāsa, listen to the merit of the person who builds a temple to the goddess either of stone or of wood or of mud. He who builds a temple to the goddess attains the benefit which a person who worships every day through Yoga attains in plenty. That virtuous soul who builds the temple for the glorious mother enables a thousand past and a thousand future members of his family to attain liberation.

## \|\| 9.51.15 \|\|

*koṭijanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ svalpaṃ vā yadi vā bahu \|*  
*śrīmāturmandirārambhakṣaṇādeva praṇaśyati \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The sins committed by a person in a crore of births, whether they be small or big, are quelled at the very moment when the foundation for temple of the glorious mother is laid.

## \|\| 9.51.16-17 \|\|

*nadīṣu ca yathā gaṃgā śoṇaḥ sarvanadeṣu ca \|*  
*kṣamāyāṃ ca yathā pṛthvī gāṃbhīrye ca yathodadhiḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

*grahāṇāṃ ca samastānāṃ yathā sūryo viśiṣyate\|\| \|*  
*tathā sarveṣu deveṣu śrīparāmbā viśiṣyate \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. The glorious great Ambā is the most excellent among all the deities just as Gaṅgā and Śoṇa among the rivers, the earth in forbearance, the ocean in majesty and the sun among the planets.

## \|\| 9.51.18 \|\|

*sarvadeveṣu sā mukhyā yastasyāḥ kārayedgṛham \|*  
*pratiṣṭhāṃ samavāpnoti sa ca janma nijanmani \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. She is the most important among all the deities. He who causes her temple to be built attains established power in every birth.

## \|\| 9.51.19-21 \|\|

*vārāṇasyāṃ kurukṣetre prayāge puṣkare tathā \|*  
*gaṃgāsamudratīre ca naimiṣe'marakaṇṭake \|\| 19 \|\|*

*śrīparvate mahāpuṇye gokarṇe jñānaparvate \|*  
*mathurāyāmayodhyāyāṃ dvārāvatyāṃ tathaiva ca \|\| 20 \|\|*

*ityādi puṇyadeśeṣu yatra kutra sthale'pi vā \|*  
*kārayanmāturāvāsaṃ mukto bhavati bandhanāt \|\| 21 \|\|*

19-21. One who makes the temple for the mother in any of these holy centres becomes free from bondage—viz. Vārāṇasī, Kurukṣetra, Prayāga, Puṣkara, the banks of the Gaṅgā or the shores of the ocean, Naimiṣa, Amarakaṇṭaka, the highly meritorious Śrīparvata, Gokarṇa, Jñānaparvata, Mathurā, Ayodhyā, Dvāravatī etc.

## \|\| 9.51.22 \|\|

*iṣṭakānāṃ tu vinyāso yāvadvarṣāṇi tiṣṭhati \|*  
*tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi maṇidvīpe mahīyate \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He is honoured in the Maṇidvīpa for as many thousand years as the number of years the brickwork holds good.

## \|\| 9.51.23 \|\|

*pratimāḥ kārayedyastu sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitāḥ \|*  
*sa umāyāḥ paraṃ lokaṃ nirbhayo vrajati dhuvam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. He who makes idols with the full complement of characteristics fearlessly attains indeed the great, region of Umā.

## \|\| 9.51.24 \|\|

*devīmūrtiṃ pratiṣṭhāpya śubhartuṃ grahatārake \|*  
*kṛtakṛtyo bhavenmartyo yogamāyāprasādataḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. After installing the image of the goddess, in the auspicious stars, planets and the season, man becomes contented and blessed by the favour of the Yogamāyā.

## \|\| 9.51.25 \|\|

*ye bhaviṣyanti ye'tītā ākalpātpuruṣāḥ kule \|*  
*tāṃstāṃstārayate devyā mūrtiṃ saṃsthāpya śobhanām \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. After installing an auspicious image of the goddess one can redeem the men of one’s family both of the past and the future.

## \|\| 9.51.26 \|\|

*trilokīsthāpanātpuṇyaṃ yadbhavenmunipuṃgava \|*  
*tatkoṭiguṇitaṃ puṇyaṃ śrīdevīsthāpanādbhavet \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. O leading sage, by installing the goddess the merit derived is a crore times more than that derived by establishing the three worlds.

## \|\| 9.51.27 \|\|

*madhye devīṃ sthāpayitvā pañcāyatanadevatāḥ \|*  
*caturddikṣu sthāpayedyastasya puṇyaṃ na gaṇyate \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The merit cannot be calculated of the man who installs the Pañcāyatana deities with Ambikā in the centre and the other four in the four quarters.

## \|\| 9.51.28-31 \|\|

*viṣṇornāmnāṃ koṭijapādgrahaṇecandrasūryayoḥ \|*  
*yatphalaṃ labhyate tasmācchatakoṭiguṇottaram \|\| 28 \|\|*

*śivanāmno japādeva tasmātkoṭi guṇottaram \|*  
*śrīdevīnāmajāpāttu tataḥ koṭiguṇottaram \|\| 29 \|\|*

*devyāḥ prāsādakaraṇātpuṇyantu samavāpyate \|*  
*sthāpitā yena sā devī jaganmātā trayīmayī \|\| 30 \|\|*

*na tasya durlabhaṃ kiṃcicchrīmātuḥ karuṇāvaśāt \|*  
*varddhate putrapautrādyā naśyatyakhilakaśmalam \|\| 31 \|\|*

28-31. One attains great merit by repeating the names of Viṣṇu a crore times during the solar and lunar eclipses, a hundred crore times by repeating the names of Śiva, a crore times by repeating the names of Śrīdevī, a crore times by building the palace for the goddess. To the man who installs the image of the goddess, mother of the universe, identical with the three Vedas there is nothing inaccessible, thanks to the merit of the glorious mother. His sons, grandsons and others flourish. All his sins perish.

## \|\| 9.51.32 \|\|

*manasā ye cikīrṣaṃti mūrtisthāpanamuttamam \|*  
*tetyumāyāḥ paraṃ lokaṃ prayānti munidurlabham \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Even those who desire mentally the installation of the excellent image attain the great region of Umā inaccessible even to the sage.

## \|\| 9.51.33-34 \|\|

*kriyamāṇantu yaḥ prekṣya cetasā hyanucintayet \|*  
*kārayiṣyāmyahaṃ yarhi saṃpanme saṃbhaviṣyati \|\| 33 \|\|*

*evantasya kulaṃ sadyo yāti svargaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*mahāmāyāprabhāveṇa durlabhaṃ kiṃ jagattraye \|\| 34 \|\|*

33-34. If a man, on seeing the image being made or the temple being built thinks mentally: “If I get sufficient wealth I too will make”, his family attains heaven undoubtedly. What is there in the three worlds which cannot be secured by the power of Mahāmāyā?

## \|\| 9.51.35 \|\|

*śrīparāmbājagadyoniṃ kevalaṃ ye samāśritāḥ \|*  
*te manuṣyā na mantavyāssākṣāddevīgaṇāśca te \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Those who have resorted only to the goddess the cause of the universe, are not ordinary men. They are the Gaṇas of the Goddess herself.

## \|\| 9.51.36 \|\|

*ye vrajantaḥ svapantaśca tiṣṭhanto vāpyaharniśam \|*  
*umeti dvyakṣaraṃ nāma bruvate te śivāgaṇāḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Those who repeat the two syllables “U-mā” day and night, sitting, sleeping or moving become Śivā’s Gaṇas.

## \|\| 9.51.37 \|\|

*nitye naimittike devīṃ ye yajanti parāṃ śivām \|*  
*puṣpairdhūpaistathādīpaiste prayāsyantyumālayam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Those who worship the goddess Śivā, with flowers, incense and lamps whether as daily routine or on special occasions due to certain reasons will attain Umā’s abode.

## \|\| 9.51.38 \|\|

*ye devīmaṇḍapaṃ nityaṃ gomayena mṛdāthavā \|*  
*upaliṃpanti mārjanti te prayāsyantyumālayam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Those who clean, scrub and smear the altar of the goddess with cowdung or clay will attain Umā’s abode.

## \|\| 9.51.39 \|\|

*yairdevyā mandiraṃ ramyaṃ nirmāpi tamanuttamam \|*  
*tatkulīnāñjanānmātā hyāśiṣaḥ saṃprayacchati \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. The goddess mother bestows her blessings on the family of the person who builds a beautiful temple for the goddess.

## \|\| 9.51.40 \|\|

*madīyāḥ śatavarṣāṇi jīvantu premabhāgjanāḥ \|*  
*nāpadāmayanānītthaṃ śrīmātā vaktyaharniśam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The glorious mother repeats day and night: “May my devotees live for a hundred years. Let them not be the victims of adversities.”

## \|\| 9.51.41 \|\|

*yena mūrtirmmahādebyā umāyāḥ kāritā śubhā \|*  
*narāyutantatkulajaṃ maṇidvīpe mahīyate \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Ten thousand people of the person who has caused the image of the goddess Umā to be made, are honoured in the Maṇidvīpa.

## \|\| 9.51.42 \|\|

*sthāpayitvā mahāmāyāmūrtiṃ samyakprapūjya ca \|*  
*yaṃyaṃ prārthayate kāmaṃ taṃtaṃ prāpnoti sādhakaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Whatever an aspirant solicits after installing and worshipping the image of the goddess he attains that.

## \|\| 9.51.43 \|\|

*yaḥ snāpayati śrīmātuḥ sthāpitāṃ mūrtimuttamām \|*  
*ghṛtena madhunāktena tatphalaṃ gaṇayettu kaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Who can calculate the merit of a person who performs the ablution of the installed image of the glorious mother, with clarified butter after smearing it with honey?

## \|\| 9.51.44 \|\|

*candanāgurukarpūra māṃsīmustādiyugjalaiḥ \|*  
*ekavarṇagavāṃ kṣīraiḥ snāpayetparameśvarīm \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The devotee shall perform the ablution of the goddess either with water scented with sandal, aguru, camphor māṃsī, mustā etc. or with the milk of single-coloured cows.

## \|\| 9.51.45 \|\|

*dhūpenāṣṭādaśāṃgena dadyādāhutimuttamām \|*  
*nīrājanaṃ careddevyā ssājyakarpūravartibhiḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The excellent offering shall be made with the incense of eighteen ingredients. The waving of the light for the goddess shall consist of wicks soaked in ghee or camphor.

## \|\| 9.51.46 \|\|

*kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ navamyāṃ vāmāyāṃ vā paṃcadiktithau \|*  
*pūjayejjagatāṃ dhātrīṃ gaṃdhapuṣpairviśeṣataḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. On the fifth, eighth, ninth and tenth days of the dark lunar half and on new moon days the devotee shall worship the mother of the worlds with fragrant flowers.

## \|\| 9.51.47 \|\|

*saṃpaṭhañjananīsūktaṃ śrīsūktamatha vā paṭhan \|*  
*devīsūktamatho vāpi mūkamantramathāpi vā \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Jananīsūkta, Śrīsūkta or Devīsūkta shall be recited or Mūlamantra shall be chanted.

## \|\| 9.51.48 \|\|

*viṣṇukrāntāṃ ca tulasīṃ varjayitvākhilaṃ sumam \|*  
*devīprītikaraṃ jñeyaṃ kamalantu viśeṣataḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. All flowers except Viṣṇukrāntā and Tulasī are pleasing to the goddess particularly the lotus.

## \|\| 9.51.49 \|\|

*arpayetsvarṇapuṣpaṃ yo devyai rājatameva vā \|*  
*sa yāti paramaṃ dhāma siddhakoṭi bhiranvitam \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. He who offers golden or silver flowers to the goddess goes to the greatest abode where crores of Siddhas live.

## \|\| 9.51.50-51 \|\|

*pūjanānte sadā kāryaṃ dāsairenaḥ kṣamāpanam \|*  
*prasīda parameśāni jagadānandadāyini \|\| 50 \|\|*

*iti vākyaisstuvanmantrī devībhaktiparāyaṇaḥ \|*  
*dhyāyetkaṇṭhīravārūḍhāṃ varadābhayapāṇikām \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. After the worship the rite of forgiveness shall be performed by the devotees for the sins committed:—“O goddess, O bestower of happiness and bliss be pleased.” He shall eulogise her with these words and meditate on the goddess as seated on a lion with boons and the majestic gesture of protection.

## \|\| 9.51.52 \|\|

*itthaṃ dhyātvā maheśānīṃ bhaktābhīṣṭaphalapradām \|*  
*nānāphalāni pakvāni naivedyatve prakalpayet \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. After meditating on the goddess, the bestower of desires on the devotees he shall offer various ripe fruits as Naivedya.

## \|\| 9.51.53 \|\|

*naivedyaṃ bhakṣayedyastu śaṃbhuśakteḥ parātmanaḥ \|*  
*sa nirbhūyākhilaṃ paṅkaṃ nirmalo mānavo bhavet \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. The man who partakes of Naivedya of Śiva Śakti shakes off all dirt and becomes pure.

## \|\| 9.51.54 \|\|

*caitraśuklatṛtīyāyāṃ yo bhavānīvrataṃ caret \|*  
*bhavavandhananirmuktaḥ prāpnuyātparamaṃ padam \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. He who performs the rite of the goddess on the third day of the bright half of Caitra is liberated from the bondage of worldly existence and attains the supreme goal.

## \|\| 9.51.55-56 \|\|

*asyāmeva tṛtīyāyāṃ kuryāddolotsavaṃ budhaḥ \|*  
*pūjayejjagatāṃ dhātrīmumāṃ śaṃkarasaṃyutām \|\| 55 \|\|*

*kusumaiḥ kuṃkumairvastraiḥ karpūrāgurucandanaiḥ \|*
